# Ground Truth for openssl-3.1.0-3.2.0

## Important Changes

### Architecture-related Changes

- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0044] Migrate the logging function in the HTTP server to an independent log module and clean up the related code. (Architecture-related: log module is independent) (PRs: #18434; commits: 8a2ec00)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0054] Default security level increased from 1 to 2. (Architecture-related: security level) (PRs: #16760; commits: b3a33da)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0196] The asn1parse command adds three new input formats: PEM, DER and B64. PEM is used by default, and the original -strictpem option is abandoned to maintain backward compatibility. (Architecture-related: command line input format) (PRs: #7317, #7320; commits: 34df960, ca857d7)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0210] Added checks for multiple unknown options in option initialization, and added a new function to set unknown_name. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #16416; commits: 2c27244)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0289] A new app_conf_try_number function is added to simplify the acquisition of configuration values and unify the error handling mode. (Architecture-related: new public function app_conf_try_number) (PRs: #20971; commits: b778268)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0292] Allows clients to connect to HTTP servers over IPv6. (Architecture-related: Platform compatibility) (PRs: #21033; commits: 830b6a1)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0293] Removed restrictions on cross-signing self-signed certificates, now allowing cross-signing self-signed certificates. (Architecture-related: Certificate Behavior) (PRs: #21137; commits: 7f4cc3b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0306] Added fallback implementations for UINT32_C and UINT64_C macros to support different 64-bit processor ABIs. (Architecture-related: Platform compatibility) (PRs: #21951; commits: 09a4b4b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0317] When OPENSSL_NO_QUIC_THREAD_ASSIST is defined, the quic_thread_assist.c file is no longer compiled. (Architecture-related: build and installation methods) (PRs: #21332; commits: 1a2a0e1)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0513] Introduce HAS_PREFIX() and CHECK_AND_SKIP_PREFIX() macros, replace manual string prefix checking, fix boundary processing and add auxiliary functions. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #15847; commits: 2ff286c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0516] Fix the HTTP server port output format, reconstruct the socket address acquisition logic, adjust the OCSP responder function signature to remove the port parameters. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #16061; commits: 4599ea9)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0520] Fixed the problem of handling AKID and SKID extensions according to configuration, and refactored the logic to support the none option and the suppression of self-signed certificates. (Architecture-related: configuration option changes) (PRs: #16300, #16342; commits: adbd77f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0523] Fix compilation errors caused by AT_SECURE not being declared on Debian's kfreebsd kernel. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #16477; commits: 3a1fa01)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0527] Fixed the problem of EC public key export using compressed format by default, changing it to uncompressed format by default, and following the format parameters set by the user. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #13139, #14800, #16595, #19681; commits: a16e866)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0530] Fix the EVP_PKEY_eq() function to correctly handle keys containing only the private key component, and adjust the match function of DH, DSA, EC, ECX and RSA key management implementations to relax selector bit interpretation. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #16267, #16765; commits: edc8566, ee22a37, f3ba626)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0536] Fixed an issue reported by Coverity: Check return value during zlib compression/decompression initialization and return an error on failure; Fixed logical dead code in EVP_DigestSignFinal. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #16962; commits: 182cc64, 73a815d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0546] The x509 tool returns a non-zero exit code when the certificate IP/Email/host name verification fails, and the verification logic is restructured to unify error handling. (Architecture-related: exit code behavior) (PRs: #17022; commits: 9567fd3)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0548] Fixed the problem of proxy environment variables being ignored due to callback design flaws in the use of HTTPS proxy, and adjusted the proxy judgment logic. (Architecture-related: proxy behavior) (PRs: #17088, #17310; commits: 068549f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0551] Fixed the problem of incorrectly canceling keep-alive when parsing non-200 status code responses in OSSL_HTTP_REQ_CTX_nbio(), and adjusted the error checking order. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #17171; commits: 38288f4)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0552] Fix the check of port parameters in proxy mode in the OSSL_HTTP_set1_request() function to make it consistent with the documentation: When using HTTP proxy, the server address must be provided, and the port is an optional parameter. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #17186; commits: 266383b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0581] Fixed the bug that openssl req -x509 cannot generate certificates from CSR, now supports using the -CA and -CAkey options to generate CA-signed certificates, and added regression tests. (Architecture-related: external behavior) (PRs: #17736, #17782; commits: df5c86e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0593] Fix wincrypt.h symbol conflict, adjust preprocessing instructions in types.h and add build test. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #9981, #18131; commits: 3c58d44)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0603] Add necessary header include for NonStop platform to support fixed size integer types. (Architecture-related: Platform compatibility) (PRs: #17669, #18325; commits: ec26144)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0609] Fix the problem of incorrect EVP_CIPHER_CTX_ctrl return value check, and change the original Boolean value judgment to the correct numerical comparison. (Architecture-related: public API behavior repair) (PRs: #18368; commits: d649c51)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0617] Fixed the dsaparam and gendsa commands to support the -provider and -propquery options, and updated the related help text. (Architecture-related: command line interface) (PRs: #18576, #19898; commits: 30b2c35, 4380686)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0619] Fixed an issue where the decoded-from-explicit flag of the EC group was lost when moving across providers. Now the flag will be exported correctly during serialization. (Architecture-related: cross-provider behavior) (PRs: #18600, #18609; commits: 95a6fbd)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0621] Fixed the problem that the -provider and -propquery options in multiple command line tools (dhparam, ecparam, pkeyparam, dgst, genrsa) did not take effect. (Architecture-related: public command line interface) (PRs: #18717; commits: 0185538, 2b8f687, 653a770, ae3c30a, b2ccfd8)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0637] Make the six operations mutually exclusive in the smime command, and add checks to prevent multiple operations from being specified at the same time. (Architecture-related: smime command behavior) (PRs: #18917; commits: 2786160)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0668] Fix SHUT_RD and SHUT_WR macro definitions on Windows platform, and add missing header file references. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #19345; commits: 4ccb89b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0673] Replace BIO_free(bio_err) with BIO_free_all(bio_err) to correctly handle the BIO chain that may be returned in a VMS environment. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #19446; commits: a73bdc2)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0676] Fixed an issue where the public key was incorrectly considered required when using PEM_read_bio_PrivateKey_ex, and now becomes optional when requesting a private key. (Architecture-related: public API behavior) (PRs: #19498, #19505; commits: adb408d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0680] Changed the thread pool support function to be compiled only when the thread pool is enabled, which fixed related issues. (Architecture-related: build and installation methods) (PRs: #19691, #19695; commits: f5a3669)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0681] Fixed a compilation warning caused by using OSSL_PARAM_construct_uint on platforms where uint32_t is defined as unsigned long int, use OSSL_PARAM_construct_uint32 instead. (Architecture-related: Platform compatibility) (PRs: #19322; commits: 1e065a1)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0683] Removed unnecessary cancellation status and type settings in POSIX threads, and fixed the Android compilation failure problem. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #19559, #19779; commits: 14c593e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0684] Fixed HPKE test failure when chacha20 or poly1305 is disabled, fixed conditional compilation macro. (Architecture-related: build conditions) (PRs: #19784; commits: 36b4d7a)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0734] Fix multiple issues related to EC and RSA encryption algorithms, including buffer size, key type conversion, parameter translation and digest initialization. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #20889, #20890, #20899, #20900, #20922, #20993, #20994, #21008, #21019; commits: 3410a72, 43d5dac, 9107087, be6497a, c5aa719)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0735] Fixed bugs related to atomic operations and thread disabling, and added atomic loading function. (Architecture-related: internal API) (PRs: #20765, #20856; commits: 5da3e02, 629b408)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0738] Fix the macro attribute conflict with cmocka, replace __attribute__((malloc)) in OSSL_CRYPTO_ALLOC macro with __attribute__((__malloc__)). (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #20776, #20869; commits: 0bf7e94)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0763] Fixed the no-autoload-config option so that it no longer automatically loads the configuration during engine and provider lookup, and fixed the locking mechanism for provider lookup. (Architecture-related: Engine and provider lookup) (PRs: #21621; commits: cb8e641)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0797] Fixed the warning caused by using the %n format specifier when cross-compiling, and rewritten the path splicing logic. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #22511; commits: 2e40770)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1505] Deprecate the OPENSSL_LH_stats series of functions, and migrate internal LHASH definitions to new macros. (Architecture-related: public API: Deprecated OPENSSL_LH_stats) (PRs: #17937; commits: 5317b6e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2214] Document the unexpected behavior of shared library pinning during static builds in the installation documentation, and provide workarounds using the no-shared and no-pinshared configuration options. (Architecture-related: build and installation methods) (PRs: #20977, #20985; commits: ce451fb)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2321] Optimize the configuration script and update the file only when the content of configdata.pm changes to avoid unnecessary source code reconstruction. (Architecture-related: Build configuration optimization) (PRs: #16377, #16378; commits: 764cf5b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2324] Fix the VMS installation script and uniformly use platform->shlib_version_as_filename() to obtain the shared library version file name. (Architecture-related: VMS platform compatibility) (PRs: #16842; commits: 93b670a)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2325] Fix the problem of logical name undefinition and definition mismatch in VMS installation, ensure that the shutdown script correctly undefines the engine directory logical name defined by the startup script. (Architecture-related: VMS installation script repair) (PRs: #16842; commits: 0df8e71)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2330] To support C++20 header-units, the self-contained issue of header files has been fixed: stdio.h is conditionally included in multiple header files, and the inclusion order of some header files has been adjusted to avoid including other header files within the extern "C" block. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #18221; commits: eab9dbb)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2331] Fix build issues on TANDEM and older POSIX systems and allow locale support to be disabled via the -DOPENSSL_NO_LOCALE configuration option. (Architecture-related: Platform compatibility) (PRs: #18233, #18282; commits: b98f989)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2335] Change the C version detection macro from __STRICT_ANSI__ to __STDC_VERSION__, and define the internal macro OSSL_NO_C99 based on this to handle pre-C99 compatibility issues. (Architecture-related: Build compatibility) (PRs: #19197; commits: b1104a3)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2337] Added test support for Alpine Linux (musl) in CI. (Architecture-related: Platform compatibility: Alpine Linux (musl) (PRs: #19577; commits: e8cec34)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2340] Added a new Hybrid CRT (Hybrid CRT) build target for the Windows platform, and updated related documentation. (Architecture-related: build and installation methods) (PRs: #20081; commits: 18891ef)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2341] Added support for Clang 16 compiler in CI workflow. (Architecture-related: Platform compatibility) (PRs: #20346; commits: 6de73f5)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2342] Modified the util/wrap.pl.in script to unify the behavior of platforms such as VMS, and removed the no longer needed util/local_shlib.com.in and util/unlocal_shlib.com.in files. (Architecture-related: build and installation methods) (PRs: #20415; commits: 1939ee7)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2354] Added no-apps build option to disable the building of applications (such as openssl program), and updated related documentation and CI configuration. (Architecture-related: Build configuration: no-apps option) (PRs: #21212; commits: ff88545)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2359] Added an alternate definition of socklen_t for the VMS platform to address the lack of this type in the current VMS C header files. (Architecture-related: Platform compatibility) (PRs: #21951; commits: 3ae4686)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2361] When building on NonStop platforms, exclude references to the poll.h header file because it is not defined for that platform. (Architecture-related: Platform compatibility) (PRs: #22001, #22006; commits: aff9922)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2364] Improve coverage mapping, remove support for OpenSSL 1.1.1 stable version, and expand compilation configurations for other branches to enhance test coverage. (Architecture-related: build and installation methods) (PRs: #22104; commits: 798d69c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2366] Add build tasks using brotli and zstd in the CI configuration, and add Windows compression test workflow. (Architecture-related: Build dependency: brotli/zstd) (PRs: #18186; commits: b540aae)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2456] Add extern "C" declaration to hpke.h and thread.h header files to support correct inclusion in C++ code. (Architecture-related: public API: C++ compatibility) (PRs: #21305, #21307; commits: ed5c0df)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2457] Removed the call to the internal function err_free_strings_int() and left the empty implementation to maintain compatibility. (Architecture-related: Compatibility: public API Compatibility) (PRs: #17974; commits: 1c8787d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2461] Update OpenSSL version references to update all references to OpenSSL 3.1 in the master branch to 3.2, including deprecated macros and API level checks. (Architecture-related: public API compatibility) (PRs: #19350; commits: 45ada6b)

### Core Cryptographic and Protocol Library

- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0001] Reconstruct the QUIC record layer, add complete implementations of the sender (QTX) and receiver (QRX), support shared encryption level management, key updates and AEAD restrictions. (Architecture event: QUIC record layer reconstruction) (PRs: #18949, #19703; commits: 043a41d, 1957148)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0002] Added QUIC I/O reactor framework, which provides initialization, polling, network read and write status judgment, tick callback and blocking waiting functions. (Architecture events: QUIC I/O reactor framework) (PRs: #19703; commits: 6952321)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0003] Added a new record method header file recordmethod.h to define the record/packet protection interface. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #18132; commits: 79a1f3e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0004] Added new header file e_ostime.h to ensure that struct timeval is correctly defined when including ssl.h. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #22323; commits: 6cf23ce)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0005] Allow empty passwords in PEM_write_bio_PKCS8PrivateKey_nid. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #17507; commits: 1d28ada)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0011] Removed the old QUIC virtual handshake implementation, which has been replaced by the real TLS handshake. (Architecture-related: QUIC handshake implementation replacement) (PRs: #19748; commits: c28f1a8)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0013] Separate the SSL/TLS record layer from the SSL object, migrate it to the new record layer module, reconstruct the reading and writing, encryption, MAC calculation, key derivation and other functions of TLS 1.0/1.1/1.2/1.3, DTLS and SSLv3, remove the direct dependence on the SSL object, introduce a new record layer method interface, and adjust the relevant public header files and function signatures. (Architecture event: Record layer reconstruction) (PRs: #17942, #18132, #18612, #19198, #19343; commits: 10560ae, 1853d20, 19d0044, 222cf41, 2b891e3, 2f6e24e, 3105901, 38b051a, 3c7b9ef, 4030869, 4bf610b, 50023e9, 5b24990, 6366bdd, 7f2f0ac, 8577312, 88d6168, 9b7fb65, 9cd9e09, 9dd9023, a566864, aedbb71, b5cf81f, cffafb5, e2d5742, eddb067, f2892e2)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0014] Reconstruct the writing record layer logic into independent record layer objects and methods, and migrate the relevant code. (Architecture event: Record layer reconstruction) (PRs: #19198; commits: 2b71b04)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0015] Move the alarm sending check out of the record layer and move it to the upper layer write function for processing. (Architecture event: Record layer reconstruction) (PRs: #19198; commits: 3eaead7)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0016] Migrate the write buffer management logic from the SSL connection layer to the write record layer, and reconstruct the related functions to use the record layer internal data structure. (Architecture event: Record layer reconstruction) (PRs: #19198; commits: 151f313)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0017] The calculation logic of the record version number is moved out of the record layer, and is instead calculated by the upper-layer state machine and passed to the record layer through the template, and a new auxiliary function is added to set the record protocol version. (Architecture event: Record layer reconstruction) (PRs: #19198; commits: 1d36767)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0018] Move the need_empty_fragments flag into the record layer structure, and set the flag according to conditions when the record layer is initialized, removing the old location and setting logic. (Architecture event: Record layer reconstruction) (PRs: #19198; commits: b9e4e78)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0019] Move the record filling callback completely into the record layer, passing it through the dispatch array, avoiding direct access to SSL_CONNECTION. (Architecture Event: Record Layer Refactoring) (PRs: #19198; commits: 5f95eb7)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0020] Migrate the callback function and block size parameters related to TLS 1.3 record filling from the SSL_CONNECTION structure to the OSSL_RECORD_LAYER structure, and add corresponding parameter definitions. (Architecture event: Record layer reconstruction) (PRs: #19198; commits: eb7d6c2)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0021] Re-enabled multiple blocks of code and migrated them to the record layer, restructured buffer management and write-related functions. (Architecture event: Record layer refactoring) (PRs: #19198; commits: 23bf52a)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0022] Migrate the pipeline related code from the SSL layer to the record layer, add the tls_free method to support resource release of the record layer; migrate the number of write pipelines to the new record layer structure. (Architecture event: Record layer reconstruction) (PRs: #19198; commits: c618679, e7694c6)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0023] Refactor the write code, move multi-block write functionality to a separate file, and adjust related functions to use the new record layer structure. (Architecture Event: Record Layer Refactoring) (PRs: #19198; commits: bafe524)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0024] Added QUIC receiving stream management function, introducing SFRAME_LIST structure and QUIC_RSTREAM object for managing received stream data. (Architecture event: QUIC protocol support) (PRs: #19351; commits: bbf902c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0025] Migrate record filling logic from tls_common.c to tls13_meth.c. Only TLS 1.3 methods need to handle record filling, other methods are ignored. (Architecture event: Record layer reconstruction) (PRs: #19343; commits: 2582de2)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0026] Add a new encryption preparation step, extract logic such as MAC calculation and encryption space reservation from the record writing function into an independent function, and register this step in each protocol method. (Architecture event: Record layer reconstruction) (PRs: #19343; commits: 757ef3b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0027] Change the way of saving retransmission status in dtls1_buffer_message to use standard record layer functions, and change the parameters of dtls1_retransmit_message and dtls1_set_message_header from SSL* to SSL_CONNECTION*. (Architecture event: Record layer reconstruction) (PRs: #19424; commits: b9e37f8)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0028] The old buffer management code has been removed, and related functions have been migrated to the new record layer. (Architecture event: Record layer reconstruction) (PRs: #19424; commits: e158ada)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0029] Create internal SSL objects for QUIC-TLS to represent TLS connections, and refactor the SSL initialization flow to support passing in method parameters. (Architecture Event: QUIC-TLS Integration) (PRs: #19748; commits: a7f4188)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0030] Separate QUIC related code from FIPS source files, and add QUIC packet processing functions. (Architecture event: QUIC supports reconstruction) (PRs: #19658; commits: 25624c9)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0033] Reconstruct OSSL_LIB_CTX internal object management, change from dynamic CRYPTO_EX_DATA to hard-coded static members and initialize them in advance to reduce lock competition; add decoder_cache support. (Architecture-related: core module reconstruction) (PRs: #17116, #17881; commits: 927d056)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0036] Centralize ARM SHA3 CPU detection logic to armcap.c, add ARMV8_WORTH_USING_SHA3 flag and enable it for Apple M1/M2; unify SHA3 capability detection macro to ARMV8_HAVE_SHA3_AND_WORTH_USING. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #21398; commits: 08e6eb2, ba9472c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0038] Move the TLS1_FLAGS_QUIC macro definition from the public header file to the internal header file, making it private. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #21905; commits: d492e34)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0039] Reconstruct the legacy blake2 EVP structure, directly use base blake2 to implement it, avoid relying on the provider-specific init function, and change the corresponding init function in the provider to static. (Architecture-related: Separation of responsibilities between EVP and Provider) (PRs: #22079; commits: df9ecd2)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0040] Removed the unused reset function and its related implementation in OSSL_RECORD_METHOD. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #19748; commits: e5103df)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0041] Reconstruct the QUIC congestion control abstract interface and change the packet loss and confirmation notification logic to pass each data packet to the congestion control module individually. (Architecture-related: QUIC congestion control interface) (PRs: #20423; commits: 9069917)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0042] Reconstruct the option processing mechanism of QUIC connections to separate connection-level and stream-level options, allowing streams to inherit and update options independently. (Architecture-related: QUIC option processing mechanism) (PRs: #20061; commits: db2f98c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0045] Migrate OSSL_TIME related code to libcrypto, and adjust conditional compilation to support UEFI environment. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #19082; commits: 02d0f87)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0065] New QUIC stream status query API, including SSL_get_stream_type, SSL_get_stream_id, SSL_is_connection, SSL_is_stream_local and SSL_stream_reset, used to obtain stream identifier, type and connection status. (Architecture event: QUIC protocol integration) (PRs: #20765, #21905; commits: 19cb088, 1bca3f1, c3a04ea, ca5b030, e1dee2e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0067] Add SHA-3 based PRF support to PBES2, integrating SHA-3 hashing into PBKDF2 as an alternative to HMAC. (Architecture event: Added SHA-3 PRF support) (PRs: #16237; commits: 5702392, c73ba81)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0068] Added Brotli compression support (RFC7924), implemented the corresponding BIO and COMP methods, and displayed Brotli disabled status in the command line tool. (Architecture event: Compression module added Brotli compression support) (PRs: #18186; commits: 12e96a2)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0069] Added ZSTD compression support, including compression/decompression methods, BIO filters and related tests. (Architecture event: Compression module adds ZSTD compression support) (PRs: #18186; commits: caf9317)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0074] Enable KTLS receive functionality in TLS 1.3, and fix the sequence number and BIO usage of the receive path. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #17942; commits: 7c78932)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0075] Added SSL_get0_iana_groups() and SSL_client_hello_get_extension_order() functions, allowing users to obtain the groups and extension order in the TLS handshake without memory allocation, for low-cost calculation of SSL fingerprints (JA3). (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #16910; commits: 13a53fb)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0076] Added basic QUIC protocol support: Added OSSL_QUIC method declaration, empty implementation and auxiliary functions, as well as variable length integer encoding/decoding and WPACKET subpackage operation interface. (Architecture event: QUIC protocol support) (PRs: #18307, #18382; commits: 416d0a6, 770ea54, 99e1cc7)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0079] Ensured various SSL options were passed to the record layer and updated relevant test functions. (Architecture Event: Record Layer Change) (PRs: #18132; commits: 79eebb0)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0080] An event queue is implemented in SSL, supporting the creation, addition, removal and retrieval of events. (Architecture events: SSL protocol engine module changes) (PRs: #18345; commits: e6be47e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0081] Implemented KTLS support in the new read record layer code, and restructured the encryption status switching and key update functions. (Architecture event: SSL protocol engine module change) (PRs: #16963, #18132; commits: cc110a0, ff3e450)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0082] Supports passing data from one epoch to the next epoch, and adds record layer parameter parsing and cleanup logic when connection is released. (Architecture event: SSL protocol engine module change) (PRs: #18132; commits: 359affd)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0103] Reimplemented brainpool curve support in TLSv1.3, simplified processing logic and created a new TLS_GROUP_ENTRY value. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #19315; commits: 16f0e91, c9ee6e3)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0104] Rename the internal function ossl_sleep to the public function OSSL_sleep and move it to crypto/sleep.c to solve the problem that dependent functions cannot be found when linking shared libraries. (Architecture event: HPKE module added) (PRs: #19330; commits: 82d28c6)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0105] Introduce BIO preparation steps before writing, and add KTLS buffer allocation and release functions. (Architecture event: SSL protocol engine module reconstruction) (PRs: #19343; commits: ace3819)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0106] Integrate QUIC_RSTREAM into the RX unpacker, replacing the previous placeholder implementation, so that the received data can be queued and flow controlled through the flow controller. (Architecture event: QUIC protocol engine change) (PRs: #19351; commits: a17c713)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0107] Add pseudo packet loss support to QUIC ACKM, allowing specified packets to be forced to be marked as pseudo loss during connection retries and avoid triggering congestion control. (Architecture event: QUIC protocol engine changes) (PRs: #19703; commits: e5d5756)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0108] Added a new ossl_quic_demux_set_bio function to the QUIC demuxer, allowing the BIO it reads data to be changed at runtime. (Architecture event: QUIC protocol engine change) (PRs: #19703; commits: 964f0de)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0109] Support the calculation and verification of QUIC Retry Integrity Tag, and add the unused field in the QUIC packet header structure to ensure serialization consistency. (Architecture event: QUIC protocol engine change) (PRs: #19703; commits: 70d4589)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0110] Added new transmission parameters and related constants to the QUIC protocol, and added a connection ID comparison function; at the same time, reject integer transmission parameters with additional trailing bytes to enhance parsing robustness. (Architecture event: QUIC protocol engine change) (PRs: #19703, #22523; commits: 56a1a0a, cecc05c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0111] The QUIC record layer now allows rekeying of the INITIAL encryption layer on connection retries, other encryption layers still do not. (Architecture Event: QUIC Protocol Engine Change) (PRs: #19703; commits: b2c94b9)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0112] Implemented the front-end I/O API for QUIC, including connection and stream management, read and write operations, error handling and blocking mode support, and connected it to the SSL API function, enabling QUIC connections to use SSL read and write, handshake and event timeout interfaces. (Architecture event: QUIC protocol engine integration) (PRs: #19703; commits: 03bacce, 22d53c8)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0113] Added a new QUIC-TLS record layer implementation, including record reading and writing, status management, transmission parameter callback and other functions, and replaced the virtual handshake layer with a real QUIC TLS implementation, adding necessary TLS parameters and extended support. (Architecture event: QUIC-TLS record layer implementation) (PRs: #19748; commits: 19863d4, 2723d70)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0114] Realize the complete functions of QUIC channel, including connection initialization, handshake management, key update, flow control, transmission parameter processing and state machine management; at the same time, complete the implementation of the receiving unpacker based on QUIC_CHANNEL, reconstruct the frame processing function and add protocol error checking and stream state management. (Architecture event: QUIC channel and unpacker implementation) (PRs: #19703; commits: 3a37c92, f538b42)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0115] QUIC TXP no longer sends STREAM frames before the handshake is completed, adds an API to notify the handshake completion and updates related tests. (Architecture event: QUIC transmission behavior change) (PRs: #19703; commits: cda88ba)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0116] Allow MTU to change over time and automatically detect MTU, improve URXE state management and return handling. (Architecture events: QUIC MTU automatic detection) (PRs: #19703; commits: d7668ff)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0117] Add an internal API for setting a custom TLS record layer, add a new parameter in the record layer method, allowing custom record layer methods to be specified through internal functions; extend the new_record_layer function to support passing the master key, length and KDF digest parameters, and update related calls. (Architecture event: TLS record layer interface extension) (PRs: #18132, #19748; commits: 34a4068, 3f9175c, bea8d70)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0118] Added KTLS zero-copy sendfile support, which is disabled by default on Linux, and added the KTLSTxZerocopySendfile option to enable this feature, and updated related documentation and unit tests. (Architecture event: SSL protocol engine KTLS support) (PRs: #18650; commits: cd715b7)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0119] Add internal support for SSL objects, allowing custom extensions to be registered at the SSL level instead of just the SSL_CTX level. (Schema Event: SSL Protocol Engine Custom Extension Support) (PRs: #19748; commits: f6da3bb)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0120] Added BIO polling descriptor API to support obtaining read and write polling descriptors, and updated SSL related functions to adapt to QUIC connections. (Architecture event: SSL protocol engine QUIC adaptation) (PRs: #19703, #21979; commits: 68801bc, 7e1b0dc)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0124] Added TLS server support for QUIC, including error handling improvements and ALPN negotiation callbacks. (Architecture events: SSL_Protocol_Engine module adds QUIC TLS server support) (PRs: #19748; commits: 4e3a55f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0125] Implement zero-copy reading for QUIC receive streams, add ossl_quic_rstream_get_record and ossl_quic_rstream_release_record APIs, and introduce a ring buffer as side storage for stream frame data. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module adds QUIC zero-copy reading API) (PRs: #19794; commits: 2113ea5)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0126] Added the ability to query connection termination reasons for QUIC channels and test servers, introduced the QUIC_TERMINATE_CAUSE structure and extended related APIs. (Architecture events: SSL_Protocol_Engine module added connection termination reason query) (PRs: #20030; commits: 149a8e6)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0127] Added a callback function to set the TLS handshake message mutator, allowing the handshake message to be modified before it is written. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #20030; commits: d03fe5d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0128] Optimize QUIC's SSL_shutdown implementation, add SSL_shutdown_ex API, and support passing application error codes when shutting down locally. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #19550, #19897; commits: cbf965b, e804322)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0129] Add support for detecting end of stream and sending end of stream signal to QUIC front-end I/O API. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #19897; commits: a997996)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0130] QUIC TXP adds the ability to generate probe packets, and adjusts the congestion control check logic to allow probe packets to be sent even when they cannot be sent. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change (QUIC support) (PRs: #19925; commits: fee8f48)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0131] Implement memory-based datagram BIO (BIO_s_dgram_mem), reuse BIO_s_dgram_pair code and add tests. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change (QUIC support) (PRs: #20012; commits: 3a857b9)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0132] Added the TLS1_FLAGS_QUIC flag bit, which is set when the QUIC connection is initialized, and provides the SSL_IS_QUIC_HANDSHAKE macro to detect whether the SSL connection is used for QUIC handshake. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change (QUIC support) (PRs: #20061; commits: 43788fb)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0133] Implement the SSL_rstate_string and SSL_rstate_string_long functions for QUIC connections, and adjust related functions to correctly support QUIC. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module changes (QUIC support) (PRs: #20061, #20827; commits: 79ee017, 9ea0e72)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0134] Implement the SSL_has_pending function in QUIC, and add an internal interface to check pending data. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change (QUIC support) (PRs: #20061; commits: 9280d26)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0135] Add HelloRetryRequest support for QUIC connections, modify SSL_stateless function to correctly handle QUIC SSL. (Architecture event: QUIC support integration) (PRs: #20061, #21636, #22644, #22646; commits: 2f563dc, 5ac7ee4, a1c56bb)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0136] Add SSL_set_fd support for BIO_s_datagram for QUIC SSL, and extend test cases. (Architecture event: QUIC support integration) (PRs: #20061; commits: 5e6015a)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0137] Add version setting restrictions to QUIC SSL connections, force the minimum protocol version to be TLS1.3, and add tests. (Architecture event: QUIC support integration) (PRs: #20061; commits: 0eecf84)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0138] Added channel-only tick mode, which only processes the state synchronized by the channel mutex in thread-assisted mode. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #20348; commits: ccd3103)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0139] Add thread-assisted front-end support for QUIC connections, including initialization, startup and cleanup functions. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #20348; commits: f2f7c4f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0140] Support rstream's get/release recording mode at the QUIC TLS layer to avoid internal data copying when reading. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #20404; commits: 7257188)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0141] Allow partial release of TLS records so that cleartext sanitization can be done at the record level and avoid forcing removal of const qualifications. (Architectural event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module changes) (PRs: #20404; commits: 7a4e109)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0142] Implement the NewReno congestion control algorithm for QUIC and switch its use in QUIC channels. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #20423; commits: ab11c16, f68fd1c, f6f45c5)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0163] Add validation of preferred_addr transport parameter for QUIC conformance. (Architecture Event: QUIC Protocol Implementation) (PRs: #21135; commits: 54bd1f2)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0164] Allow applications to trigger QUIC key updates (TXKU) via SSL_key_update, and added corresponding internal interfaces and tests. (Architecture Event: QUIC Protocol Implementation) (PRs: #21029; commits: 2525109, 692a3ca)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0165] Support the SSL_OP_CLEANSE_PLAINTEXT option, set the plaintext clear flag when initializing the QUIC stream, and add a data erasure function for the send stream. (Architecture event: QUIC protocol implementation) (PRs: #21182, #21311; commits: 6ba2edb, a02571a)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0166] Implementing RFC 9000 10.1 requirements, set the QUIC idle timeout period to at least three times the current probe timeout (PTO). (Architecture Event: QUIC Protocol Integration) (PRs: #21429; commits: b056e9f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0167] Added a new public function for calculating the ciphertext payload length for QUIC QTX. (Architecture event: QUIC protocol integration) (PRs: #21458; commits: 41d3998)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0168] Added QUIC channel ping and stateless reset functions, adjusted the server shutdown interface to support application error codes. (Architecture event: QUIC protocol integration) (PRs: #21135; commits: 9ff3a99)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0169] Implement rate limiting in the QUIC closed state, ensuring that the closed transmission size does not exceed three times the received size, enhancing protocol compliance. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #21429; commits: 50e7684)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0170] Added internal interface for setting QUIC send buffer size, and helper functions for getting connection and stream types. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #21484; commits: 3415677)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0171] Implement the stream refresh function when the QUIC connection is closed, and add an interface to obtain the polling descriptor and network read and write status. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #21484; commits: 8a2e9ab)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0172] Support most control commands for QUIC-based BIO_SSL, adjust the internal read-write BIO acquisition method and add QUIC compatibility check. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #21539; commits: a2ca189)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0173] Add unreliable transmission support to QUIC control frame queue (CFQ) so that PATH_RESPONSE and other frames are no longer retransmitted when lost. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #21547; commits: 371c295)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0174] Introduce addressed mode in QUIC, support disabling the peer address mode by setting NULL or AF_UNSPEC address, and adjust the processing logic at the connection layer. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #21715; commits: 617b459, 62665fc)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0175] Improve QUIC blocking configuration: automatically set network BIO to non-blocking mode, reconstruct blocking support detection logic, and add infinite timeout parameters. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #21715; commits: 0818c17, 51e671e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0176] QUIC implements automatic reply to PATH_RESPONSE frame after receiving PATH_CHALLENGE frame. (Architecture event: QUIC protocol integration) (PRs: #21547; commits: 7eb330f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0177] Add a receiver flow control mechanism for the CRYPTO stream of the QUIC channel. (Architecture event: QUIC protocol integration) (PRs: #21547; commits: 098914d, ab6c634)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0178] QUIC QRX Added functions to allow processing of 1-RTT packets after the handshake is completed and rejoining the delay queue. (Architecture Event: QUIC Protocol Integration) (PRs: #21547; commits: 2a6f1f2, 869ab3e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0179] In the QUIC TLS tick function, it no longer returns directly after the handshake is completed, but continues to call SSL_read() to process possible post-handshake messages. (Architecture event: QUIC protocol integration) (PRs: #21591; commits: f85d343)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0180] Added support for QUIC servers to use pre-existing SSL_CTX contexts instead of always creating new ones. (Architecture Event: QUIC Protocol Integration) (PRs: #21591; commits: 829eec9)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0181] Add stateless reset processing support for QUIC channels. (Architecture events: QUIC protocol integration) (PRs: #21649; commits: cdd9163)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0182] In QUIC, if the NewSessionTicket message contains the early_data extension, its max_early_data value must be 0xffffffff, otherwise it is regarded as a protocol violation; new verification logic. (Architecture event: QUIC protocol verification logic change) (PRs: #21686; commits: 04c7fb5)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0183] Added minimal version negotiation packet processing logic for QUIC connections. (Architecture Event: QUIC Version Negotiation Processing) (PRs: #21764; commits: 777a8a7)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0184] Optimize the TLS 1.3 handshake key switching timing, and switch the client write key as early as possible when early data is not used to support QUIC requirements. (Architecture event: TLS 1.3 key switching optimization) (PRs: #21810; commits: 84a1492)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0185] Implement a backpressure mechanism for flow creation for the QUIC application layer, add flow count flow control check, block or reject new flow creation when the peer flow limit is reached. (Architecture event: QUIC flow control backpressure mechanism) (PRs: #21811; commits: 9d6bd3d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0186] QUIC APL supports waiting for shutdown initiated by the peer, and has added relevant flags and waiting mechanisms. (Architecture event: QUIC shutdown waiting mechanism) (PRs: #21815; commits: 25a0c4b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0188] Add the ability to query local initiation status for QUIC streams, and enhance the cleanup operation to support sending streams. (Architecture event: QUIC stream function enhancement) (PRs: #21905; commits: d2e9e12)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0189] Added SSL_inject_net_dgram, quic_get_stream_error_code, ossl_quic_get_stream_read_error_code and ossl_quic_set_write_buffer_size functions, and added frame_type field in connection closing information. (Architecture event: QUIC new public API function) (PRs: #21915; commits: 56df4cf)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0190] Implemented the SSL_want function for QUIC connections, supporting returning the desired I/O status. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change (QUIC protocol support) (PRs: #21915; commits: 5debf07)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0191] Added SSL_net_read_desired and SSL_net_write_desired functions for TLS/DTLS connections so that they can correctly return the expected read and write status in non-QUIC scenarios. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change (QUIC protocol support) (PRs: #21979; commits: 3432157)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0192] Added the ability to set PSK lookup session callbacks for QUIC servers. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #22011; commits: 1e4fc0b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0193] SSL_get_rpoll_descriptor and SSL_get_wpoll_descriptor now also support TLS/DTLS connections, no longer limited to QUIC. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #21979; commits: f262443)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0194] Add support for SSL_get_shutdown() for QUIC connections, enabling it to return SSL_SENT_SHUTDOWN and SSL_RECEIVED_SHUTDOWN status. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module changes (QUIC support) (PRs: #22408; commits: 7757f5e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0197] Enable brainpool curve support for TLS 1.3, and add related signature algorithms and group ID mappings. (Architecture-related: TLS 1.3 curve support) (PRs: #7485, #19315; commits: 0a10825, 3f76339)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0198] Add Chacha20-Poly1305 encryption algorithm support to FreeBSD's kernel TLS. (Architecture-related: Platform compatibility) (PRs: #13752; commits: 77f3936)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0199] Implement RFC7250 Raw Public Key (RPK) support, allowing TLS connections to be established using only private keys, and add related API, unit testing, documentation and command line tool support. (Architecture-related: Raw Public Key (RPK) support) (PRs: #18185; commits: 3c95ef2)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0200] Added -digest option to openssl cms command to support using precomputed digest for signing; also added CMS_final_digest() API function. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #15348; commits: 07342ba)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0201] In CMP certificate path verification, when a certificate is found to be expired or not yet valid, it will no longer reject it directly. Instead, it will first call the verification callback set by the user to decide whether to accept it. (Architecture-related: CMP certificate verification behavior) (PRs: #21656; commits: 080bd08)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0203] Add a global read-write lock for object database operations to make OBJ series calls thread-safe. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #15713; commits: 397065c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0204] Added multiple registered OIDs for CMPv2 extension, including CMP module identification, information type and registration control identification. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #15778; commits: 34959f7)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0205] Enable BIO_gets() functionality for BIO_s_connect(), and refactor HTTP tests to support testing of text content. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #16028, #16030; commits: 7a9b09f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0206] CMP mock server adds the -ref_cert option and the ossl_cmp_mock_srv_set1_refCert() function, which is used to verify the certificate information in the revocation request based on the reference certificate. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #16041, #16050; commits: b971d41)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0207] Allows specifying cipher strings using the fully qualified name of the cipher suite, and adding corresponding tests. (Architecture-related: external behavior) (PRs: #16179; commits: d1b26dd)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0208] Implement the optional hashAlg field for CMPv3's certConf messages, set this field when using the fallback hashing algorithm and set the protocol version to 3. (Architecture-related: external behavior) (PRs: #18294; commits: 74107c4)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0209] Added support for RFC8879 compressed certificate extension, including compressed sending and receiving of server and client certificates, and added related APIs and configuration options. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #18186; commits: b67cb09)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0212] Add TCP Fast Open (TFO) support for socket BIO and s_client/s_server, support Linux, macOS and FreeBSD, disabled by default, can be enabled through the enabled-tfo option. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #8692; commits: a3e53d5)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0213] Added necessary OIDs defined in the ETSI specification for CAdES processing. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #18655; commits: 5f7d4e9)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0214] Added preemptive thread support, providing internal APIs for thread creation and synchronization primitives for POSIX and Windows platforms, and supporting compile-time disabling and runtime enabling. (Architecture-related: public API, platform compatibility) (PRs: #12255; commits: 4574a7f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0215] Added codesign purpose support in X.509 certificate verification, and added the corresponding verification parameter code_sign. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #18567; commits: 178696d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0216] Added signed BIGNUM conversion functions, including BN_signed_bin2bn, BN_signed_bn2bin, etc., and restructured the internal implementation to handle signed and unsigned data uniformly. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #17139; commits: 4e26fe5, 5288303, c2cab43, f5e8050)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0217] Add support for signed BIGNUM in OSSL_PARAM and OSSL_PARAM_BLD APIs, and fix zero value boundary cases. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #17162; commits: 17898ec, 748a296, f171985)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0218] Added support for id-it-caCerts to the CMP module, added related API functions and internal functions, and added the -srvcertout command line option. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #18656, #19231; commits: b6fbef1, d477484)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0219] Added assembly architecture support for the RISC-V 64-bit platform, including cpuid runtime detection, bit operation extension Zb[abcs] and byte swap macros BSWAP4/BSWAP8, and repaired SHA macro conditional judgment. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #17640, #18275; commits: 360f6dc, cb2764f, e4fd3fc)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0220] Added asn1_string_to_time_t function and added tests, and modified OBJ_find_sigid_by_algs to allow digest-free search signature algorithm and added tests. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #13944, #17645, #17733, #18668; commits: 065121f, 0654421, 6097eb2)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0221] Enhance SSL thread safety and verification mechanism: add locks for platforms that do not support TSAN, change retry verification to SSL_set_retry_verify function, add StrictCertCheck configuration option. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #17479, #17489, #17568, #17825, #17989; commits: 336d92e, 5c41cee, acce055, d8ed9e4, dfb39f7, e6b8f35)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0223] Added functions to obtain the verification certificate storage and chain certificate storage of SSL and SSL_CTX. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #18035, #18038; commits: 948cf52)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0224] Added more SRTP protection configuration files, including RFC 8723 dual AEAD configuration and ARIA series configuration. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #18030; commits: a425c0f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0225] Add public string case-insensitive comparison functions OPENSSL_strcasecmp and OPENSSL_strncasecmp. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #18069; commits: 4b2bd27, fba140c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0226] Introducing CMP protocol version 3, while keeping version 2 as the default version. (Architecture-related: versions and compatibility) (PRs: #18294; commits: c4ad4e5)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0227] Added support for BIO_sendmmsg and BIO_recvmmsg to BIO_dgram. (Architecture-related: new features of BIO layer) (PRs: #18270; commits: 664e096)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0228] Export the CMS_EnvelopedData type and add the CMS_EnvelopedData_decrypt function. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #18301, #18667; commits: 98b183d, e2f6960)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0229] Added d2i_PUBKEY_ex_fp and d2i_PUBKEY_ex_bio functions to support passing in library context and attribute query when reading public keys from files or BIO. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #18427; commits: 820723d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0230] Two new BIO types, BIO_s_dgram_pair and BIO_s_dgram_mem, are added to provide ring buffer-based datagram pair and memory datagram functions. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #18442; commits: b88ce46)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0231] Added datagram mode support to BIO_s_mem, switched through new BIO control commands, and introduced the BIO_s_dgram_mem method. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #18596; commits: 3bfc58a, 5a4ba72, ce602bb)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0232] Added CMS_SignedData_verify function as an extended variant of CMS_verify. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #18667; commits: d7d3dae)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0233] Added X509_PUBKEY_set0_public_key function, extracting and optimizing the public key setting logic from X509_PUBKEY_set0_param. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #18668; commits: 9df7158)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0234] Add partial string matching support for Content-Type values to OSSL_HTTP_REQ_CTX_nbio, allowing parameters after semicolons to be ignored. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #18675; commits: 52f6169)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0235] Added OSSL_trace_string function and OSSL_TRACE_STRING_MAX constant, used to format the output trace string. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #18704; commits: 0243e82)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0236] RISC-V rev8 instruction support has been extended to the zbkb extension, and early breakage constraints have been added for the two-instruction bswap. (Architecture-related: Platform Compatibility: RISC-V zbkb extension) (PRs: #18827; commits: 48b6776)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0237] Removed the unused 1-bit GCM implementation and added the ossl_gcm_ghash_4bit function. (Architecture-related: public API: added ossl_gcm_ghash_4bit) (PRs: #18835; commits: 7b6e19f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0238] The -CAfile parameter now supports certificate files in DER format. When loading, PEM format will be tried first, and DER format will be automatically tried after failure. (Architecture-related: public API: -CAfile supports DER format) (PRs: #18917; commits: 57c0205)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0239] Improved error handling in X509_STORE_CTX_init, and added X509_STORE_CTX_init_rpk function. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #18918; commits: 4fdc16a)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0240] Added BIO_sendmmsg and BIO_recvmmsg series APIs, including method setting/getting, sending/receiving functions, error judgment and debugging callback support. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #18923; commits: e0c4e43)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0241] Added new CRMF API function OSSL_CRMF_CERTTEMPLATE_get0_publicKey(), used to obtain the public key in the certificate template. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #18930; commits: c0f6792)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0242] Allows setting arbitrary bag attributes through callback functions when exporting PKCS12, and adds PKCS12_create_ex2 and PKCS12_SAFEBAG_set0_attrs interfaces. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #19025, #19359; commits: af63793, e869c86)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0243] Allows accepting private key input when a public key is expected, and refactored loading logic to support silent mode. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #19076; commits: 0e89b39)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0244] Added the for_comp flag to the certificate compression process to skip unnecessary error handling during the compression process. (Architecture-related: Certificate compression behavior) (PRs: #18186; commits: 72620ac)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0245] The OSSL_CRYPTO_ALLOC macro is introduced to provide the compiler with a hint for returning pointers without aliases. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #19180; commits: e103595)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0246] Added support for password-based encryption (PBE) using hmacWithSM3. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #19204; commits: 48963ff)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0247] Added id-ct-signedTAL content type OID for RPKI's Trust Anchor Key signed object, and added OID definition for ASPA object. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #17002, #19221; commits: b0c1214, fcae2ae)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0248] In the record layer, the public type COMP_METHOD is used instead of the internal type SSL_COMP, and the ability to query the current compression method through the record layer is added. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #19217; commits: 1e76110)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0249] CMP has added support for rootCaCert in genm messages and rootCaKeyUpdate in genp messages, including related implementations of client API, server simulation and command line tools. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #21129; commits: 01b0485)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0250] Added two new API functions, OSSL_CMP_CTX_get0_libctx() and OSSL_CMP_CTX_get0_propq(). (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #19715; commits: 2da163c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0251] Certificates generated by default now use the X.509 V3 version unless the -x509v1 option is specified in the req command. (Architecture-related: external behavior) (PRs: #19271; commits: 342e365)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0253] Added CPU feature detection support for LoongArch64 architecture, and defined cpuid related macros and variables. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #19364; commits: 7f2d618)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0254] Added protocol version compatibility check in key_share extension processing of TLS 1.3. (Architecture-related: Protocol Compatibility) (PRs: #19317; commits: 247b8e5)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0259] Add option to disable implicit deny for RSA decryption, allowing users to choose whether to use implicit deny padding mode via control parameters. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #13817; commits: 5ab3ec1)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0260] Added a new API to the QUIC congestion control module to query the next time to release the sending budget. (Architecture-related: internal API) (PRs: #19703; commits: 97c5c52)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0261] Add OID 1.2.156.10197.1.104.10 and its corresponding NID, short name and long name for the SM4-XTS algorithm. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #19619; commits: de8f6a3)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0262] Added zlib one-time compression/decompression function, provided COMP_zlib_oneshot method, and adjusted the compilation conditions of COMP_zlib. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #19520, #19603; commits: 3840271)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0264] Add libctx support to the X9.31 key generation function and add coverage tests. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #19677; commits: bcd94b6)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0265] Add manual URXE injection support for QUIC QRX, refactor the internal receive callback into a public injection interface, and adjust related cleanup and initialization logic. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #19734; commits: 4e392f6)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0266] EVP_PKEY_Q_keygen now supports SM2 key generation. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #18247, #19736; commits: 3f32d29, b807c2f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0268] Reconstruct the HPKE context initialization to obtain the AEAD encryption algorithm in advance and save the algorithm information to avoid repeated acquisition; at the same time, the parameter verification of the exported function is enhanced. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #19784; commits: d9ed306)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0269] Updated QUIC version string from QUIC to QUICv1. (Architecture-related: protocol version identifier) (PRs: #19703; commits: 6848e5e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0271] Allow OBJ_create to accept NULL OID parameters to create objects and NIDs. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #19876; commits: b79da97)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0274] Add additional libctx and propq support to PKCS12, add related APIs and fix memory release issues. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #19716, #19718, #19942; commits: fe2a734)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0276] Support processing signedAndEnveloped type content in PKCS7_decrypt. (Architecture-related: public API) (commits: 35da6af)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0277] Add support for IgnoreUnexpectedEOF option in SSL configuration command. (Architecture-related: SSL behavior options) (PRs: #20089; commits: 51cf034)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0278] Support incoming password callback when store is opened for early authentication of PKCS11 modules. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #20131; commits: 96e6780)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0279] Added EC_GROUP_to_params function, used to convert elliptic curve group to OSSL_PARAM array. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #20205; commits: a8aad91)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0280] Fix -reqin option, add OSSL_CMP_MSG_update_recipNonce function. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #20204; commits: 4b0c27d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0283] Allows overriding the time source of the QUIC channel, and adds a callback function pointer for debugging or testing. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #20348; commits: b212d55)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0284] To support Windows XP, add the condition variable signal function ossl_crypto_condvar_signal, adjust the timeout waiting interface, and remove the timeout_expired parameter. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #20348; commits: 1dd04a0)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0285] Added SSL_get_handshake_rtt function, used to obtain handshake round-trip time. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #20248; commits: cee0628)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0286] Enable POSIX thread support on VMS platforms and remove unused memory barrier functions. (Architecture-related: Platform compatibility) (PRs: #20440; commits: ac21c17)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0287] Added SSL_R_CONN_USE_ONLY error code definition, and removed the old number. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #20765; commits: 71e5551)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0290] Added selected Microsoft OIDs, introduced ms-corp alias for OID 1.3.6.1.4.1.311, updated all related OID definitions. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #19630, #20986; commits: f3afe15)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0291] Added SSL_get0_group_name() function, used to obtain the group name used for key exchange. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #20866; commits: 6866824)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0295] Add missing PKI/PMI object identifiers, including related OID definitions and macros. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #21232; commits: bac2f6d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0296] Supports all X.509v3 extensions of NULL syntax, and adds corresponding parsing and printing functions. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #21342; commits: 91bc783)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0298] Added OSSL_trace_string public API function. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #21468; commits: bbaeadb)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0299] Add datagram mode support to BIO_s_connect, and support configuring non-blocking mode in datagram mode. (Architecture-related: BIO_s_connect datagram mode) (PRs: #21715; commits: 533254e, 7841dba)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0300] Enhanced BIO_s_datagram: supports non-blocking mode configuration, adds peer address detection API and capability negotiation control command. (Architecture-related: public API extension) (PRs: #21715; commits: 000ef78, 1bd35ed, f3295bd)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0302] Added two new setting functions, X509_STORE_CTX_set_get_crl and X509_STORE_CTX_set_current_reasons, to support custom CRL verification. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #21679, #21737; commits: 4a469cb)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0303] EVP_PKEY_can_sign() and EVP_PKEY_assign() now support the RSA-PSS key type, treating it the same as standard RSA. (Schema-related: public API) (PRs: #21819; commits: e297298)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0304] In the polling descriptor structure, the type field is changed to unsigned integer, and a custom value member of the integer type is added to support third parties using integers instead of pointers. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #21905; commits: 2619d10)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0309] Added new public function BIO_ADDR_copy(), used to copy BIO_ADDR objects. (Architecture-related: public API: BIO_ADDR_copy) (PRs: #22164; commits: 30224a2, a18c9f8, d058ae6)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0313] Newly added unbiased random integer generation functions ossl_rand_uniform_uint32 and ossl_rand_range_uint32. (Architecture-related: public API: Newly added random number generation function) (PRs: #22499; commits: 3fe56ba)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0445] Fixed an issue in the QUIC transport packetizer where CONNECTION_CLOSE frames were generated simultaneously on multiple encryption levels and will now only be generated on the lowest non-dropping encryption level. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #19734; commits: 7f9d124)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0446] Disabled the use of non-QUIC cipher suites in QUIC connections and added related test cases. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #20061; commits: 09d56d2)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0447] Disable the use of NPN (Next Protocol Negotiation) in QUIC context. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #20061; commits: 68dbff4)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0448] Post-handshake authentication is disabled during QUIC connection initialization. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #20061; commits: 7163617)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0449] Disable setting the maximum fragment length in non-disabled mode on QUIC SSL connections. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #20061; commits: d0638fd)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0450] Disable the early data function of QUIC SSL and add related test cases. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #20061; commits: 82a2bec)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0451] It is forbidden to perform pipeline-related operations such as setting the maximum send fragment, splitting the send fragment and the maximum number of pipes on QUIC SSL connections, and adding corresponding tests. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #20061; commits: 38c0ff1)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0452] Restrict use of SSL_CTX_set_ssl_version and SSL_set_ssl_method in QUIC context. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #20061; commits: 3ea30e7)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0453] Updated QUIC's SSL_set_quiet_shutdown and SSL_get_quiet_shutdown implementations to clarify that QUIC is not currently supported, and adjusted the internal structure to use SSL_CONNECTION. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #20061; commits: f66f0d3)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0454] Prevent the SSL_clear operation in the QUIC SSL implementation to return failure and add corresponding tests. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change (QUIC SSL implementation) (PRs: #20061; commits: 5f69db3)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0455] Fix a possible segfault when calling SSL_shutdown on a QUIC connection. (Architecture Event: SSL_Protocol_Engine Module Change (QUIC Protocol Integration) (PRs: #20514; commits: 4e15b44)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0456] Update the QUIC scheduling method, use the SSL object pointer instead of the QUIC_CONNECTION pointer, and fix an error code. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #20765; commits: 6d495cc)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0457] Delay the creation of the default XSO to support client-first or server-first QUIC application protocols. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #20765; commits: 21c8069)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0458] Fixed multiple issues in QUIC protocol implementation, including role identification, stream state management, flow control, locking, callback mechanism, STOP_SENDING processing, FIN detection, stream release behavior, one-way stream detection, list usage errors and time callback parameter passing, etc. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #20423, #20765; commits: 13ac037, 2289401, 5d27e7e, 66ec534, 8b52789, 93651dc, 9aaafc2, acc6fde, b89c81e, dfb9ae1, e8fe7a2)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0459] Fixed multiple issues in QUIC multi-stream support: SSL_get_stream_type function logic correction, multi-stream count reference processing, send stream buffer space management, receive buffer check optimization and variable uninitialization issues. (Architecture event: QUIC protocol implementation) (PRs: #20765; commits: 22b1a96, 433d107, 59c5c01, 9cab4bd)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0460] Fix multiple issues in QUIC protocol implementation, including acknowledgment handling, flow control, flow mapping, BIO acquisition and thread safety. (Architecture Event: QUIC Protocol Implementation) (PRs: #20423, #20856, #20930; commits: 0f06e7f, 3dde343, 45b7c7e, bb7f370, e8142d2)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0461] Fix issues related to QUIC key update: prevent old keys from processing new data packets, correctly execute the key update process, and strictly implement verification of ACK packet sequence numbers and key epochs. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #21029; commits: 54fb007, 8a65e7a, c93f766)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0462] Adjust QUIC connection ID handling logic to comply with RFC 9000 specifications, strictly limit the number of active connection IDs, and close connections when the peer limit is exceeded. (Architecture event: QUIC connection ID handling) (PRs: #21078; commits: 985429f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0463] Fix QUIC network error handling, return SSL_ERROR_SYSCALL when encountering a network error. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #21087; commits: 5c3474e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0464] Implement error state saving and recovery for the QUIC channel, save the thread error state when a permanent error is encountered, and restore it to the user after the protocol is closed. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #21087; commits: 9c3ea4e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0465] Following RFC 9000 section 3.3, correct stream state consistency with allowed frame types: ensure STREAM frames are no longer sent in the Reset Sent state, and correct final_size calculation for RESET_STREAM frames. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #21135; commits: 01715f2)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0466] Repair QUIC protocol consistency: add packet type auxiliary function, improve transmission parameter parsing, and add packet verification and empty packet payload detection required by RFC 9000. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #21135; commits: 0911cb4)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0467] Release QUIC stream send and receive buffers that are no longer needed, and correctly calculate the final size of RESET_STREAM frames. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #21135; commits: 28d0e35)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0468] The QUIC flow state machine model now correctly handles the final size, and the processing logic of STOP_SENDING frames has been optimized. (Architectural event: QUIC protocol implementation change) (PRs: #21135; commits: 418e122)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0469] Force QUIC frame type encoding to use a minimum number of bytes, and add related detection and testing. (Architecture event: QUIC protocol implementation change) (PRs: #21135; commits: 6c1d0e2)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0470] Force checking of reserved bits in QUIC encrypted packet headers. If non-zero, it is considered a protocol violation and triggers a connection error. (Architecture event: QUIC protocol implementation change) (PRs: #21135; commits: 08cb9a8)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0471] According to the requirements of RFC 9000 section 17.2.5.1, add a check for Retry packets in the QUIC channel: if its SCID is the same as the DCID of the initial packet, the Retry packet is discarded. (Architecture event: QUIC protocol implementation change) (PRs: #21135; commits: 212616e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0472] Per RFC 9000 section 13.3, restrict MAX_STREAM_DATA frames to be generated only when the receive stream is in the RECV state. (Architectural Event: QUIC Protocol Implementation Change) (PRs: #21135; commits: 22f21fb)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0473] According to RFC 9000 section 19.8, there is an enforced limit on the maximum stream size, and stream frames exceeding 2^62 - 1 will trigger a protocol error. (Architectural event: QUIC protocol implementation change) (PRs: #21135; commits: 283938f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0474] Fix the handling of the final size of RESET_STREAM frames in the QUIC protocol to make it comply with the RFC 9000 specification. (Architecture event: QUIC protocol implementation changes) (PRs: #21135; commits: 7e3fa44)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0475] Per RFC 9000 section 12.5, CFQ is prohibited from sending disallowed frame types in a specific PN space. (Architectural Event: QUIC Protocol Implementation Change) (PRs: #21135; commits: 8d2e353)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0476] Following RFC 9000 Section 12.5, the application layer CONNECTION_CLOSE frame is masked to ensure that it is converted into a transport layer CONNECTION_CLOSE frame when sent at a non-1-RTT encryption level to avoid leaking application data. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #21135; commits: 96fa10f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0477] Fixed the problem in the QUIC protocol that when processing NEW_TOKEN frames with empty tokens, errors were not reported as required by RFC 9000. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #21135; commits: b5b40c4)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0478] Fix handling of STREAM_DATA_BLOCKED frames to comply with RFC 9000 specification: ignore frames for deleted streams and return protocol errors for send-only streams. (Architectural event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module changes) (PRs: #21135; commits: f084a8f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0479] According to RFC 9000 Section 19.14, add verification of the maximum stream ID encoding value when parsing STREAMS_BLOCKED frames, and trigger a connection error when the limit is exceeded. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #21135; commits: f80e61b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0480] Implement the RFC 9000 specification for QUIC clients and reject Initial packets sent by the server containing non-zero token length. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #21135; commits: fd0d593)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0481] Fix the retirement processing logic of FIN and reset status in QUIC streams to ensure that the stream map is correctly notified when the read is completed or a reset is received. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #21135; commits: 08d4b7e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0482] QUIC no longer processes received packets after the connection is terminated. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #21135; commits: 8a6a00e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0483] Added send stream state verification in QUIC APL to ensure that write operations are only performed in valid stream states, and fixed related error handling. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #21135; commits: abfe3d5)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0484] According to the RFC 9000 specification, when a client receives a zero-length connection ID, it treats the RETIRE_CONNECTION_ID frame as a protocol violation and triggers error handling. (Architectural event: QUIC protocol implementation change) (PRs: #21135; commits: f37befa)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0485] Fixed the issue where SSL_connect() failed to block correctly in blocking mode for QUIC connections. (Architecture event: QUIC protocol implementation changes) (PRs: #21204; commits: fb4a2bb)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0486] Set the ping deadline to infinite when initializing the QUIC channel to prevent timeout immediately before the connection is accepted. (Architectural event: QUIC protocol implementation change) (PRs: #21204; commits: 2719568)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0487] Fixed QUIC handling in SSL status string functions, added helper functions for selecting BIO methods based on connection type, and cleaned up code related to multi-block transfers and version checking. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module changes) (PRs: #20061; commits: d6e7ebb)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0488] Fix early data attribute inheritance problem in QUIC context, disable block padding, normalize header file protection macros. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #20061; commits: 6e5550a)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0489] Correct QUIC acknowledgment delay behavior according to RFC 9000, including correctly initializing the maximum acknowledgment delay and setting it to 0 in the Initial/Handshake package space. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #21349; commits: 76908b4, f13868d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0490] Improve the processing logic of QUIC connection closing and draining status according to RFC 9000, including adjusting ACK deadline calculation, adding connection closing reason copy function and optimizing polling descriptor update. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #21429; commits: afe4a79)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0491] Uniformly use SSL_R_QUIC_PROTOCOL_ERROR error cause and record detailed error information. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #21337, #21476; commits: 2b8126d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0492] Fix the error return value when the QUIC connection is closed, automatically drain the stream that does not end normally, and optimize the congestion control wake-up time. (Architecture event: QUIC protocol implementation change) (PRs: #21484; commits: 63fac76)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0493] Fix the error code sent by the QUIC channel when the transmission parameters are not received, and change it to the correct encryption extension missing alarm. (Architecture event: QUIC protocol implementation change) (PRs: #21547; commits: 3ad5711)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0494] Fixed the issue in the QUIC channel where the client did not correctly discard the Initial encryption level after sending the Handshake packet for the first time. (Architecture event: QUIC protocol implementation change) (PRs: #21547; commits: 3eb0f9a)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0495] Correctly convert TLS handshake errors into QUIC protocol errors, add error codes and error reporting interfaces, and trigger protocol error processing at the channel layer. (Architecture event: QUIC protocol implementation change) (PRs: #21547; commits: 80bcc4f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0496] Enforce PN monotonicity after key update on the QUIC receiving end, discarding packets that use the old key and have a PN greater than or equal to the starting PN of the new key. (Architectural event: QUIC protocol implementation change) (PRs: #21547; commits: 0c1cc36)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0497] Fix the missing unlock call problem detected by Coverity in QUIC APL, and introduce lock management reconstruction. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #21565; commits: 4669a3d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0498] Ensure that the underlying TLS SSL object is marked as closed when the QUIC connection is closed so that the session is available for recovery. (Schema Event: SSL_Protocol_Engine Module Change) (PRs: #21591; commits: f219abe)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0499] Improve the error reporting mechanism of the QUIC channel, add error status recovery and more detailed error information, and optimize error handling during the TLS handshake. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #21547; commits: 741170b)
