- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0500] Fix the error handling when the server sends post-handshake CertificateRequest in the QUIC protocol, treating it as a protocol violation instead of an unexpected message. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #21686; commits: b644a93)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0501] In a QUIC connection, receiving a TLS KeyUpdate message is treated as unexpected and triggers a connection error. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #21686; commits: 50a0af2)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0502] Fixed an issue where QUIC flags were lost when clearing SSL connection data, ensuring QUIC state is retained after cleanup operations. (Architecture Event: QUIC Protocol Integration) (PRs: #21686; commits: 0f2add9)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0503] Fixed the address mode judgment logic in QUIC connection, processing read and write capabilities separately. (Architecture event: QUIC protocol integration) (PRs: #21715; commits: 3760747)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0504] Fixed the processing logic of control commands in QUIC mode, added internal functions to correctly route QUIC and non-QUIC ctrl calls, and adjusted the QUIC compatibility of control commands such as version checking and fragmented sending. (Architecture event: QUIC control command processing reconstruction) (PRs: #21764; commits: c5b882a)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0505] Fixed multiple issues in QUIC protocol implementation: added QUIC cipher suite lookup function, handled repeated transmission parameters, correctly passed TLS errors. (Architecture event: QUIC protocol implementation change) (PRs: #22011, #22039, #22066; commits: 547ea58, 70e809b, 982dae8)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0506] Fix the problem of not using BIO_free_all when releasing the BIO chain in QUIC connection to ensure consistent behavior with TLS. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #22157; commits: 18fd0ea)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0509] Improved the error distinction of X509 storage lookup, now the function returns -1 to indicate internal error, 0 to indicate not found, and added lock check and sorting optimization. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #14417; commits: 0ce8271)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0510] Memory allocation functions now report ERR_R_MALLOC_FAILURE errors when they fail, and expose the OSSL_ERR_STATE_free function. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #6251, #14833; commits: 5639ee7)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0511] Add a global lock to the OBJ_add_sigid function to ensure thread safety when adding signature algorithms concurrently. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #15713; commits: c568900)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0512] New NID allocation uses atomic operations when TSAN is supported, otherwise uses locks; refactors lock management and allows creation of OID-less ASN1_OBJECT. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #15713; commits: 29c80c6)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0514] Enable checking of the return value of the EVP operation in the encryption performance test, and fix the conditional judgment in the error handling logic. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #15902, #15905; commits: 98283a6)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0515] Correct the error checking logic of PBM calculation in CRMF, adjust the const qualifier of the template getter function, and add relevant comments. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #15539, #16006; commits: 084d3af)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0517] Public and private DRBG no longer use derived functions, clean up parameter passing, and avoid passing unknown parameters. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #16117, #16156; commits: 505d44c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0518] Fix the handling of memory allocation failure in X509_ALGOR_set0(), add return value checking to ensure error propagation, simplify the code and update the documentation. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #16251; commits: 04bc3c1)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0519] Improve the parsing error report of configuration values/options by the v2i_AUTHORITY_KEYID function, and add unknown options, error values and other error reasons. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #16345; commits: 6e98b7f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0522] Prefer the issuer certificate in DANE TLSA records over the peer certificate to build the certificate chain correctly. (Architecture-related: external behavior) (commits: 661de44)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0525] Fixed the compatibility issue of random API in versions below macOS 10.12, and raised the minimum supported version to 10.12. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #16517, #16592; commits: 24cdb1b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0526] Roll back unnecessary performance optimizations in PKCS7_verify(), remove temporary read-only memory BIO, simplify logic and fix error handling. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #16590; commits: 30adf6d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0528] Fixed the issue where the BIO_free function returns a value of 0 when the callback returns a non-positive number. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #16682; commits: d8f6c53)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0529] Fixed the problem that BIO_ctrl, BIO_callback_ctrl, BIO_pop and BIO_next functions no longer log errors when passing in NULL parameters. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #16681, #16686; commits: 398ae82)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0531] Optimize the acquisition logic of the EVP_PKEY operation, give priority to obtaining the operation implementation from the key's KEYMGMT, and add a fallback mechanism. When the key cannot be exported from the current provider, try to obtain the operation implementation from the provider to which the key belongs. (Architecture-related: external behavior) (PRs: #16614, #16725; commits: 5246183, 839ffdd)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0534] Fix X509_PUBKEY_dup function to try to copy the key instead of just incrementing the reference count. (Architecture-related: public API behavior change) (PRs: #16648; commits: 7e35458)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0535] Fixed an error in the return value check of BN_bn2binpad and BN_bn2nativepad functions to ensure correct judgment of negative or zero values. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #16942, #16943; commits: 098f262, 944fcfc)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0538] Fixed the issue when d2i_PublicKey handles the EC parameter in the provided key, and added the corresponding test case. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #16989, #17065; commits: 615a9b8)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0539] OSSL_PARAM_BLD_push_BN and OSSL_PARAM_BLD_push_BN_pad now return errors for negative parameters, and related documentation has been updated. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #17070, #17074; commits: db65eab)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0540] Fix feature detection failure for ARMv7 and ARM64 CPUs on FreeBSD, by correctly using AT_HWCAP and AT_HWCAP2 constants instead of hardcoded values. (Architecture-related: Platform compatibility) (PRs: #17082; commits: c1dabe2)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0541] Fix the parsing error of OSSL_HTTP_proxy_connect() function when parsing HTTP response headers. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #17247, #17250; commits: 2490d10)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0542] Fix the problem of HTTP client clearing TLS BIO through callback function when disconnecting, and ensure that OSSL_HTTP_close handles the cleanup correctly. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #17318; commits: cdaf072)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0543] Fixed the problem that negative input of unsigned integer type was not rejected during parameter parsing, and the error will now be reported correctly; at the same time, sign extension of OSSL_PARAM_INTEGER type is allowed in OSSL_PARAM_allocate_from_text(). (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #17103, #17104; commits: 8585b5b, 946bc0e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0545] Allow copying of uninitialized digest contexts, and fix EVP_DigestFinalXOF error handling on finalized contexts. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #17117, #17118; commits: 9ece832)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0547] Fixed the issue where EVP_PKEY_CTX_get_rsa_pss_saltlen() failed to return correctly when specifying an integer value, and added a regression test. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #17136; commits: 6f87463)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0549] Disable the post-cryptographic MAC extension for the GOST cipher suite in TLS 1.2. (Architecture-related: TLS protocol behavior) (PRs: #17150; commits: d724da6)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0550] Fix the timeout processing of OSSL_HTTP_get() during redirection, add timeout check and add retry timeout error code. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #17190; commits: f0d5a3b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0553] Fixed an issue in libssl that incorrectly returned SSL_ERROR_WANT_RETRY_VERIFY when handling X509_verify internal errors. Verification errors are now treated as verification failures to avoid application crashes. (Architecture-related: external behavior) (commits: c1c1bb7)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0554] Fixed the todata and fromdata operations of EVP_PKEY so that the private key data is no longer incorrectly included when selecting the public key, and related tests were added. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #17200; commits: 944f822)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0555] Re-enable CCM mode for KTLS as the kernel has fixed a decryption failure issue in CCM mode in TLS 1.3. (Architecture-related: Platform compatibility) (PRs: #17207; commits: 34c2f90)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0557] Fixed a memory leak caused by the OSSL_CMP_MSG_read function not releasing the allocated message object when file reading failed. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #17225; commits: d580c27)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0558] Ensure that digest methods created through EVP_MD_meth_new() take the traditional path when initialized; and add tests for custom digests created through EVP_MD_meth_new() to verify that their init and cleanup functions are called correctly. (Architecture-related: external behavior) (PRs: #17149, #17255; commits: d9ad5b1, fbbe720)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0559] Fixed a memory leak that may occur when reusing EVP_MD_CTX in EVP_DigestInit_ex(), ensuring that the old md_data is properly released and the cleanup function is called before initialization. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #17149, #17255; commits: 357bccc)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0560] Fixed the problem of null pointer dereference in the BN_hex2bn function, and added a check for the static data flag to prevent errors from being caused when passing in static BIGNUM. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #17296, #17297, #17298, #17299; commits: 7c78bd4, f050745)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0561] Fixed the issue where EVP_PKEY_fromdata incorrectly returns the newly allocated pkey on failure, and will now be correctly released and empty. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #17407, #17411; commits: 5b03b89)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0562] Fixed the issue in EVP_PKEY_derive_set_peer_ex where the peer key was exported to the wrong keymgmt. Make sure to use the keymgmt corresponding to the operation for export. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #17424, #17425; commits: 64a8f60)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0563] Fixed compilation errors caused by SSE disabling in UEFI environment, and added EFIAPI calling convention for abs_val() and pow_10() functions. (Architecture-related: Platform compatibility) (PRs: #17442; commits: 328bf5a)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0564] Added multiple error codes and corresponding error description strings to the CRYPTO module. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #17440; commits: 826da14)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0565] Added detailed error messages for built-in parameter conversion functions, including error conditions such as range overflow, negative unsigned, imprecise real number, wrong type, buffer too small, empty function parameter and unknown real size. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #17440; commits: ac1082f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0566] Added error reporting for the failure return path of the OSSL_PARAM_merge function, and uniformly used OPENSSL_strcasecmp instead of strcasecmp. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #17440; commits: a10a576)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0567] Fixed the problem of incorrectly reading *siglen in EVP_DigestSignFinal when sigret is NULL. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #16962, #17460; commits: a4e0118)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0568] Fixed the problem of incomplete temporary data cleaning on the s390x platform, ensuring that the entire parameter buffer is cleared correctly. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #17486; commits: 79c7acc)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0569] Fixed the problem that EVP_PKEY_CTX_set_dh_nid and EVP_PKEY_CTX_set_dhx_rfc5114 did not return errors correctly during parameter conversion, added error handling for invalid values, and added related tests. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #17485, #17498; commits: 59d3fd1, f58bb2d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0570] Fixed an issue where record length checks could be missed when KTLS was enabled, and added KTLS test support. (Architecture-related: KTLS compatibility) (PRs: #17504; commits: 8fff986)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0571] Fixed the malloc failure issue caused by zero-byte memory allocation in PEM_write_bio_PKCS8PrivateKey() when passing in an empty password string, and added relevant test cases. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #17507; commits: 59ccb72)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0574] Fixed the counter overflow problem in the PPC AES GCM implementation, added a new wrapper function and adjusted the macro definition to correctly handle the counter. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #17607; commits: 345c99b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0575] Internal/numbers.h is included in speed.c to solve the problem that SIZE_MAX is not defined on platforms such as z/OS. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #17629, #17632; commits: 25a0a44)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0576] Restored the check of DH public key size, and added verification of ECDHE uncompressed point format to comply with TLS 1.3 specification; at the same time, the relevant security check logic was adjusted. (Architecture-related: TLS 1.3 protocol behavior) (PRs: #15465, #17630, #17667, #17678; commits: 2c0f7d4, d5530ef)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0577] When compiling with clang and BTI enabled on aarch64 architecture, fall back to swapcontext implementation to fix setjmp/longjmp compatibility issues. (Architecture-related: Platform compatibility) (PRs: #17698; commits: d2d2401)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0578] Fix the processing logic of obtaining default values from CSR and reference certificates in OSSL_CMP_CTX_setup_CRM(), and update related documents. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #17726; commits: c8c9234)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0579] At security level ≥3, treat the SSL_kDHEPSK and SSL_kECDHEPSK cipher suites as forward secure (PFS) cipher suites. (Architecture-related: Security Behavior) (PRs: #17743, #17763; commits: b139a95)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0580] Use timegm on FreeBSD instead of mktime and timezone variables, fix compilation error in asn1_string_to_time_t, and add memory allocation failure check. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #17765; commits: 0176fc7)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0582] Make ASYNC_set_mem_functions thread-safe, by adding read-write locks to protect the setting and use of custom memory allocation functions. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #17762; commits: 43ed242)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0583] Removed the call to pthread_atfork because the related function is a no-op and some platforms do not support this function causing link failure. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #17842; commits: 5979596)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0585] Fixed the build warning caused by time_t being a 64-bit type on OpenBSD, and adjusted the printing format of SSL session time value. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #17917; commits: 9362638)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0586] Disable duplicate HelloRetryRequest messages. (Architecture-related: TLS protocol behavior) (PRs: #17934, #17936; commits: d204a50)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0587] In TLSv1.3, limit the lifetime hint of session tickets to at most 1 week to comply with RFC 8446 specification, and add corresponding test cases. (Architecture-related: TLS protocol behavior) (PRs: #17948, #17952; commits: 0089cc7)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0588] Fixed the problem of OSSL_PARAM_get_*_ptr series functions incorrectly retaining the wrong state when the pointer does not match the string type, and added the ossl_param_get1_octet_string function to extract parameters to the allocated buffer. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #17997; commits: 327a720)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0589] Allows DH parameters to be specified anywhere in the SSL configuration command, and adds corresponding test cases. (Architecture-related: external behavior) (PRs: #17326, #18041; commits: b2b8d18)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0590] Fix the compilation problem in recordmethod.h caused by the conflict between the function pointer name new and C++ reserved words, and rename it to new_record_layer. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #18132; commits: 11653dc)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0592] Fixed a logical error in the memory allocation failure that may occur due to not checking the return value of X509V3_add_value in the X509V3_parse_list function. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #18077; commits: bcd5645)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0594] Fix the DH private key check: it no longer directly fails when the q parameter is missing, but instead performs a reasonable range check based on p and length; at the same time, the boundary conditions of the public key check are enhanced. (Architecture-related: external behavior) (PRs: #18098, #18099; commits: 0615ced)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0597] Move the call of ossl_deinit_casecmp to the end of OPENSSL_cleanup to ensure that the cleanup function that relies on OPENSSL_strcasecmp can be executed normally. (Architecture-related: external behavior) (PRs: #18161; commits: 1d64b06)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0598] Fix BIO_get_ktls_send and BIO_get_ktls_recv macros to ensure that their return value is only 0 or 1. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #18176, #18178; commits: 524bac5)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0600] Fixed the issue where KTLS was enabled prematurely resulting in connection failure when using BIO_new_connect. Instead, it was enabled after the connection was successful. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #18318; commits: 598bd77)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0601] Make OSSL_LIB_CTX_load_config thread-safe and add read-write lock protection for configuration module list operations. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #18226, #18331; commits: ef7a9b4)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0602] Implement the UnsafeLegacyServerConnect configuration option to make it consistent with the documentation description. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #18295, #18296; commits: 65b2bb9)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0604] Add missing declarations to the random seed function, correct parameter types to match the core schedule definition, and add a new user entropy acquisition function. (Architecture-related: Random seed function interface) (PRs: #18198; commits: 9574842)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0605] Check expected Content-Type only when HTTP status code is 200. (Architecture-related: HTTP response handling) (PRs: #18204; commits: e3477d3)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0607] Fixed undefined behavior caused by seed length being 0 in EC_GROUP_new_from_ecparameters, added length check and returned error. (Architecture-related: public API behavior fix) (PRs: #18365; commits: 97de614)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0608] Fix the return value check defect in the EVP_PKEY_get_params function to ensure correct judgment of whether the parameter acquisition is successful. (Architecture-related: public API behavior repair) (PRs: #18367; commits: 7e5e911)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0610] Upgraded the serverinfo format in SSL_CTX_use_serverinfo() to v2, and fixed related error handling. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #18183, #18614; commits: 555dd93)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0611] Fix the problem of incomplete return value checking of functions such as EVP_CIPHER_CTX_set_key_length to ensure that negative return values can be handled correctly. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #18397; commits: 8d9fec1)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0612] Fixed the problem of incorrect return value checking of EVP_PKEY_CTX_set_rsa_pss_saltlen and EVP_PKEY_CTX_get_rsa_pss_saltlen functions. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #18399; commits: 7263a7f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0613] Repair the return value check of the EVP_PKEY_CTX_set/get_* series of functions, and change the original Boolean value judgment to the correct <=0 comparison to ensure that failure situations can be captured correctly. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #18399; commits: 2cba2e1)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0614] Improve RSA key generation behavior: in non-FIPS mode, fallback to multi-prime key generation method when public key exponent e is less than 65537; explicitly throw error "no prime candidate" when no prime candidate is found. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #18429; commits: 27c1cfd, d2399d8)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0615] Fix "defined but not used" warning/error caused by conditional compilation macro error in OPENSSL_atexit function. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #18503; commits: 979575c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0620] Fixed the issue in DTLS that the handshake sequence number was not reset correctly when retransmitting ClientHello. (Architecture-related: DTLS handshake behavior) (PRs: #18635, #18654; commits: 81926c9)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0622] Improve the use of the use_ssl parameter in the OSSL_HTTP_open function to ensure that a clear Boolean value is passed to the callback function. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #18674; commits: 35750cb)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0624] Fix the memory leak caused by X509V3_add1_i2d not releasing the memory when deleting the extension under the X509V3_ADD_DELETE flag. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #18677, #18698; commits: 4798e06)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0626] Make all EVP _is_a functions accept and handle NULL parameters safely, avoiding null pointer dereference. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #18799; commits: ee8db8c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0627] Fix the signed displacement problem detected by UBSAN under the aarch64 architecture, and add U suffix to integer constants in related macro definitions to ensure unsigned operations. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #18813, #18816; commits: 1efd853)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0628] When initializing the DTLS record layer, make sure to use the datagram memory BIO instead of the normal memory BIO. (Architecture-related: DTLS record layer behavior) (PRs: #18132; commits: db1a505)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0629] Remove the reference to rlayer.rstate, and fix the ssl3_pending function to correctly return the length of pending application data. (Architecture-related: public API behavior) (PRs: #18132; commits: 8bbf7ef)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0630] Add a manual locking mechanism to VC++ 2008 and earlier x86 compilers to solve Windows XP 32bit compatibility issues caused by the lack of InterlockedOr64 inline implementation. (Architecture-related: Platform compatibility) (PRs: #18856; commits: 2d46a44)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0631] Fixed an issue in DTLS where SSL_pending() and SSL_has_pending() did not consider buffering application data. (Architecture-related: public API behavior) (PRs: #18868; commits: 6d6b295)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0632] Fixed a segmentation fault in PEM_write() caused by calling strlen() with the header parameter being NULL. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #18825, #18865; commits: 2059574)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0633] Fix the regression problem in the CMS_final function that ignores the result of CMS_dataFinal, and ensure that the error is returned correctly when the signature fails. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #18876; commits: b037561)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0634] Fixed the problem in EVP_CIPHER_CTX_get_iv_length() that may return out-of-bounds values due to lack of range check, and improved the default logic to fall back to the cipher's IV length when the cipher context does not support obtaining the IV length. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #18875; commits: e0e338c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0635] Fixed the issue where the return value of SMIME_crlf_copy was ignored, and now correctly returns the error when memory allocation fails. (Architecture-related: external behavior) (PRs: #18876; commits: 67c0460)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0636] Fixed a regression in EC_KEY_set_private_key(), which now allows setting the private key to NULL and maintains backward compatibility. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #18744, #18942; commits: b304b3e, d93f154)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0638] Check whether the IV length is less than zero. If EVP_CIPHER_CTX_get_iv_length returns -1, it is regarded as an error and processed. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #18891; commits: 83ab43d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0639] Fix the regression introduced by the GCM mode reconstruction, adjust the default implementation of each platform and improve the RISC-V instruction set branch support. (Architecture-related: RISC-V support) (PRs: #18896, #18903; commits: 186be8e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0640] Fixed the problem in the session cache that new entries were accidentally deleted due to the order of addition, adjusted the addition logic, and supplemented related tests. (Architecture-related: public API behavior) (PRs: #18690, #18905; commits: 4842a27)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0641] Fixed the problem that the PKCS7_dataVerify function did not use the certificate revocation list (CRL) when verifying the PKCS#7 signature, and also cleaned up the related variable naming. (Architecture-related: public API behavior) (PRs: #18913; commits: 2b44565)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0643] Improved error checking and error reporting of X.509 attribute-related functions, and optimized code style. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #18931; commits: ba9e372)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0644] Corrected the processing logic of fallback topic in OSSL_CMP_CTX_setup_CRM(), giving priority to using the topic name from CSR as the default value. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #18929; commits: 7af110f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0645] Add direct error return for insertion failure in X509_VERIFY_PARAM_add0_table, and add sorting in X509_VERIFY_PARAM_lookup to ensure lookup correctness, while improving coding style. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #18918; commits: 38ebfc3)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0646] Added missing direct error reporting and improved coding style in stack.c, and also corrected the loose use of stack API in multiple files. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #18918; commits: 30eba7f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0647] Fix the setting logic of the modified flag in the X509 and (PRs: #19090; commits: 39d356e, 8e39049, 9249a34)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0649] Unify ZLIB conditional compilation macros to the OPENSSL_NO_ZLIB prefix, and make the BIO_f_zlib() function always available. (Architecture-related: public API and build configuration) (PRs: #18186; commits: 59d2129)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0650] Fixed the return type of the rlayer_skip_early_data callback function, corrected it from pointer type to int, and solved the test failure problem caused by copy and paste errors. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #19037, #19048; commits: e921882)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0651] Fixed an issue in TLSv1.3 that caused connections to defective servers to fail due to strict checking of legacy_record_version. Clients will now ignore incorrect record versions. (Architecture-related: TLS compatibility) (PRs: #18132, #19051, #19058; commits: 2093428)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0652] Fixed the issue where the key length was not set correctly when deriving ECX keys on the s390x platform, and adjusted the derivation logic to be consistent with the common code. (Architecture-related: s390x compatibility) (PRs: #19158; commits: 3cca05c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0653] Fixed issues with BIO_sendmmsg and BIO_recvmmsg on FreeBSD, including handling the situation where the loopback socket does not support local addresses on BSD systems, and corrected the way to obtain the address parameter in sendmmsg. (Architecture-related: FreeBSD compatibility) (PRs: #19156, #19164; commits: 0768779)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0654] Fixed the fallback logic of AES-GCM on Power 8 CPUs to correctly use the default implementation when necessary instructions are missing. (Architecture-related: Platform compatibility) (PRs: #19163, #19182; commits: 9ab6b64)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0655] Fixed that CMS_add0_cert no longer reports an error when the certificate already exists, but instead silently ignores and returns success; also fixed the resource leak problem when reference counting fails in CMS_add1_cert and CMS_add1_crl. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #19199; commits: 65def9d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0656] Fixed the compatibility issue between the EVP_PKEY_eq function and the 3.0.0 FIPS provider, adjusting the selection parameters when comparing according to whether the key contains a public key. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #19201, #19648; commits: c342004, e5202fb)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0657] Fixed the problem of omitting to reset ctx->genm_ITAVs in OSSL_CMP_CTX_reinit() to avoid errors in subsequent OSSL_CMP_exec_GENM_ses() calls. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #19216; commits: 1c04866)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0658] Fixed the code format problem in the CMP and CRMF modules, and corrected the parameter checking logic of the certificate revocation request and the conditional judgment when printing PKIStatusInfo. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #19230; commits: 357bfe7)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0659] Fixed the problem that BIO_ctrl_pending and BIO_ctrl_wpending may return wrong values when the underlying error is returned, and set the upper limit of the return value to SIZE_MAX to prevent overflow. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #19240; commits: c6be0aa, e9809f8)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0660] Fixed an incorrectly reported error in d2i_CMS_ContentInfo and adjusted related test cases. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #19003, #19255; commits: 678b489)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0662] Added a workaround for converting UTC timestamps to time_t by setting the environment variable TZ=UTC for the DJGPP platform. (Architecture-related: Platform compatibility) (PRs: #19274; commits: cffb65f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0663] Fixed the problem of missing IP address family length check, and added length check in the normalization and normalization functions. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #19273; commits: 9351f67)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0664] Fixed the marking and return value issues of modified fields in X509, X509_CRL and X509_REVOKED related set and sign functions. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #19271; commits: 7e0013d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0669] Fix the issue where the cached TBSCertificate is no longer implicitly refreshed when signing the certificate. Ensure that the modified flag is correctly set before signing to ignore the cached encoding. (Architecture-related: public API: X509_sign_ctx) (PRs: #19388, #19393; commits: 963e0bc)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0670] Fix SCTP compilation error, add ossl_statem_get_state function, and change the parameter type of SSL_in_before and ossl_statem_clear from SSL* to SSL_CONNECTION*. (Architecture-related: public API: parameter type change) (PRs: #19371, #19398; commits: 846975f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0679] Added missing default fallback implementation for ghash on x86_64 platforms. (Architecture-related: Platform Compatibility) (PRs: #19673, #19674; commits: be0161f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0685] Fixed the implementation of SSL_tick and SSL_get_tick_timeout in the QUIC front-end I/O API so that it returns the success status correctly. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #19703; commits: fbe2573)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0686] Adjusted error returns and error codes in QUIC handshake processing, added cleaning support for send streams, and modified the timeout query interface to accept SSL objects. (Architecture-related: interface changes) (PRs: #19703; commits: ca41f6b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0687] Treat received unknown frame types as protocol violations, triggering protocol errors directly instead of ignoring and continuing processing. (Architecture-related: QUIC protocol behavior) (PRs: #20030; commits: ce3106b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0688] Fixed the handling of zero-valued BIGNUM in the parameter API, ensuring that zero-valued BIGNUM is allocated at least one data byte when building the parameter via OSSL_PARAM_BLD, and that zero is correctly represented in OSSL_PARAM_set_BN. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #20011, #20013; commits: c2ae891, c455f87)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0689] Fixed the constants used to compare name printing flags in X509_REQ_print_ex and (PRs: #19963; commits: d7f20c7, e176569)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0690] Disabled enabling partial write mode (EPW) while AON write operations are in progress, and removed related repair functions. (Architecture-related: QUIC write operation behavior) (PRs: #19703; commits: dfc227b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0691] Corrected the conditions for judging read completion in the QUIC front-end I/O API to ensure that the completion status is correctly returned when the write length is zero. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #19703; commits: 3f0c310)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0695] Rolled back the changes that added datagram support to BIO_s_mem() and restored the original streaming memory BIO behavior. (Architecture-related: external behavior) (PRs: #20012; commits: 6e193d4)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0696] Fixed the bin2bn function: when the input length is zero, it directly returns a cleared BIGNUM, making calls with s==NULL and len==0 safer. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #20033; commits: 1b24b5a)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0697] Fixed the problem that the bn2bin function does not accept negative lengths, and added related tests. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #20033; commits: c9466f3)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0699] It is forbidden to call SRTP related functions in QUIC TLS connection. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #20061; commits: f082205)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0702] Fixed the problem of incomplete check of EVP_CIPHER_asn1_to_param return value, changed the two conditions that only checked <0 to <=0, and correctly handled EVP_CIPHER_get_asn1_iv to return zero as success. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #20116, #201213; commits: 114d99b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0703] Fixed PKCS12_newpass() to support PBES2-encrypted PKCS12 files, and added libctx and propq parameter support. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #19092, #20134; commits: 9191dfb)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0705] Fixed the regression problem when checking newly registered certificates in OSSL_CMP_certConf_cb, improved the certificate verification logic, and added corresponding tests. (Architecture-related: public API: CMP certificate verification) (PRs: #20160; commits: 6b58f49)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0706] Fix the problem of status setting in transaction processing between CMP server and client, including correctly setting recipNonce when message verification fails, and supplementing the missing rejection status when the client rejects a new certificate. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #20190; commits: 036a444, e7041bf)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0707] Fixed the problem of an error being reported when only two keys are set when randomly generating keys with dual-key triple DES; fixed the error checking of RSA_public_decrypt, ASN1_item_i2d and other functions; fixed the null pointer dereference problem that may occur in EVP_PKEY related functions. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #20212, #20224, #20250, #20251, #20294; commits: 587e040, 5df5032, 8195e59, ab5a172)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0708] Fix a possible double free problem in CMS_add1_crl, and a use-after-free memory error in the SSL library. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #19199, #20299, #20300; commits: 6f9e531, c4a44e7)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0709] Fix the memory leak problem caused by repeated calls to SSL_CTX_set1_groups_list. Release the original memory before overwriting the pointer. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #20317; commits: 62ea5ff)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0710] Fixed skipping subdirectories in the SSL_add_dir_cert_subjects_to_stack function to avoid loading the directory as a certificate file. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #20312; commits: 1dc35d4)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0711] Fix the double release vulnerability of the OSSL_HTTP_get function, by initializing the request context pointer to NULL and setting it to empty after release to avoid repeated release. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #20278, #20351; commits: 7fed519)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0712] Fixed multiple minor issues in thread-assisted mode: added an interface to check whether the client is connected, ensured that thread-assisted resources are properly cleaned when released, and corrected the condition variable release method. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #20348; commits: dbe7b51)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0713] When the context copy fails in the signing and verification operations, an error will no longer be returned immediately. Instead, the original context will continue to be used to avoid interrupting the process due to copy failure. (Architecture-related: public API behavior) (PRs: #20375; commits: 0fc00fc)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0714] Fix the infinite loop problem caused by invalid parameters (such as q=1 or priv=0) during the DSA signing process, and add retry limit and parameter verification. (Architecture-related: public API behavior) (PRs: #20268, #20384; commits: 3a4e09a)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0715] Fix the problem in ECDSA signature that may cause infinite loop due to invalid group parameters, add minimum order number check and maximum retry limit. (Architecture-related: public API behavior) (PRs: #20384; commits: 5f820bd)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0716] Fixed typos in certificate verification error strings, and added support for Raw public key untrusted error codes. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #20450; commits: 1caa483)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0717] Fix the type conversion error when assigning size in EVP_DigestFinal_ex. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #20424, #20425; commits: b1cd268)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0718] Fix the problem in EVP_PBE_CipherInit_ex that the fallback mechanism fails because the return value of EVP_get_digestbynid is not assigned to md. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #20447; commits: c09c202)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0719] Handle the case where EVP_PKEY_get_default_digest_name returns UNDEF, allowing specific algorithms to not specify a digest. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #20460; commits: af99d55)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0720] Add the finalized flag to the EVP summary context to prevent the completed context from being incorrectly reused. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #20375; commits: 3fc2b7d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0721] Provide more specific error codes for QUIC handshake failures, such as SSL_R_REMOTE_PEER_ADDRESS_NOT_SET when the peer address is not set, and SSL_R_BIO_NOT_SET when the BIO is not set. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #20514; commits: 44a1ac5)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0722] Fix the behavior of the SSL_has_pending function under QUIC connection so that it can correctly detect whether there is pending data. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #20514; commits: 560470b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0723] Fix the regression problem of OBJ_nid2obj function returning NULL for NID_undef, so that it returns UNDEF object. (Architecture-related: public API behavior change) (PRs: #20555, #20556; commits: 908ba3e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0724] Adjust the order of HPKE API parameters, moving libctx and propq to the end as optional parameters to maintain consistency with other HPKE APIs. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #20583; commits: 8b7b9aa)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0725] Correct the SM4 capability detection condition to limit it to the __aarch64__ architecture and remove the checks for __arm__ and __ARM_MAX_ARCH__. (Architecture-related: Platform compatibility) (PRs: #20604, #20620; commits: 09cb871)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0727] Fix the problem that OPENSSL_die does not call abort() correctly in UEFI environment. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #20714; commits: c0e090b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0728] Fix the return type of the OSSL_CRMF_CERTTEMPLATE_get0_publicKey function and remove the const qualifier. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #20736; commits: 09f30b0)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0729] Fix a regression in SSL certificate directory loading when building without POSIX IO: skip the current directory and parent directory when reading the directory, avoid using stat() to check whether the file is a directory. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #20786; commits: 3155b5a)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0730] No longer sending empty renegotiation info SCSV in TLS 1.3 and above; copying minimum and maximum protocol versions only if the SSL object's method type is the same as SSL_CTX; tightening the determination of valid DTLS versions. (Architecture-related: Protocol Behavior) (PRs: #20764, #20830; commits: 1eef26b, 4f373a9, 861cd89)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0731] Initialize the rstate variable of the record layer, ensure that the SSL_rstate_string*() API returns a value consistent with the old version; adjust the QUIC record layer state string return value to make it consistent with the TLS version. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #20808, #20827; commits: 5758245, 73bac6e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0732] Fixed the issue where the CMP client returns an error response when using the -csr option when the private key is not provided; restructured the private key acquisition logic and added a new public key acquisition function; fixed the issue of checking the new certificate when the old certificate does not have a private key. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #20832; commits: 2d65859, e0f1ec3)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0736] Fix the retry flag copying problem in the flush operation of zlib and ok BIO, so that the flush operation can be retried. (Architecture-related: BIO behavior) (PRs: #21298; commits: bcbc7d6)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0737] Fix the stack corruption problem in the UI module, ensure that UI_new returns NULL and handle it correctly to avoid stack corruption caused by uninitialized password buffers. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #20893, #20957; commits: a64c48c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0739] Fix the parameter type of the macsaltlen option so that it accepts positive integers instead of no parameters. (Architecture-related: Command Line Interface) (PRs: #20969, #20979; commits: 26cf076)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0740] Optimize the OSSL_sleep function, use sleep() and usleep() combination on Unix systems to solve long-latency compatibility issues, and simplify sleep implementation on Windows platforms. (Architecture-related: Platform compatibility) (PRs: #21010; commits: 2631a94)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0742] Correct the implementation of QUIC time callback coverage, change the function return value from void to int, and add thread safety lock protection, while ensuring that the callback is used correctly to obtain the time when the channel is created. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #21029; commits: e3e9794)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0744] Fix the memory leak of encrypted content keys in CMS_ContentInfo_free, and enhance the defensive check of null pointers in ossl_cms_get0_env_enc_content. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #21026, #21058; commits: 7a18574)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0745] Fix the problem that the RAND_poll function fails to reseed correctly in non-deprecated builds, ensuring that it can call RAND_seed for reseeding. (Architecture-related: public API: RAND_poll reseeding behavior fixed) (PRs: #21167; commits: cc343d0)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0746] Fixed OSSL_CMP_MSG_http_perform(), added OSSL_CMP_OPT_USE_TLS option to control whether to use TLS, and optimized the judgment logic of TLS usage. (Architecture-related: public API: added OSSL_CMP_OPT_USE_TLS option) (PRs: #21120, #21176; commits: ac0677b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0748] Fixed the problem that the BN_RECP_CTX_set function did not check whether the parameter d is zero, and failed to return early when d was zero. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #21111, #21255; commits: 43596b3)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0749] Fixed the problem of incorrect use of supported_groups that only applies to TLSv1.3 in TLSv1.2, and added a check for group version compatibility. (Architecture-related: TLS version compatibility) (PRs: #21081, #21274; commits: e609a45)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0750] Fixed the problem of CONF_modules_load_file_ex function trying to load an empty file name when the default configuration file path is empty. Instead, it directly returns success without reporting an error. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #21258, #21282; commits: 8b7d5ea)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0751] Fix the error in the return value of the ossl_config_int() function in UEFI systems to ensure that the correct value is returned in the UEFI environment. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #21300; commits: 500e479)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0753] Automatically disable tick suppression in QUIC blocking operations to avoid indefinite hangs. (Architecture-related: QUIC behavior) (PRs: #21484; commits: cae02d2)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0754] Mask most mutable API operations when the QUIC connection is in the shutdown flush phase to prevent unsafe read and write, handshake or stream creation operations during the shutdown process. (Architecture-related: QUIC shutdown behavior) (PRs: #21484; commits: 6d6b3a0)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0755] Fix LSX detection on LoongArch, use getauxval(AT_HWCAP) instead of cpucfg instruction to check both hardware and kernel support. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #21508, #21509; commits: c612289)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0757] In the DH_check function, when the modulus is too large, in addition to returning the error code, the corresponding error bit will be set in the output parameter to improve the robustness of the caller's error handling. (Architecture-related: public API: DH_check error handling) (PRs: #21524; commits: 81d10e6)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0758] Fix unreachable and redundant code in x509 certificate and CRL loading functions, and optimize error handling logic. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #21545; commits: ae29622)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0759] Fix the return value regression problem of X509_VERIFY_PARAM_add0_policy and (PRs: #21570, #21576; commits: e3d897d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0760] Fix the problem that SSL_has_pending() may generate an error when called before the connection is established, ensure that it returns 0. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #21578; commits: c31f061)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0761] Fix the conflict between the error return value in the ossl_cmp_asn1_get_int() function and the OSSL_CMP_CERTREQID_NONE constant, change the error return value from -1 to -2, and update the caller ossl_cmp_pkisi_get_status() to correctly handle the change. (Architecture-related: external behavior) (PRs: #21579; commits: 2c8d9f1)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0762] Fixed an issue where the PEM_read_bio_Parameters function should not prompt for a password when reading parameters. It will now fail directly instead of asking for a password. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #21588, #21603; commits: 0d0791e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0764] Fixed an error caused by certificate auxiliary data when creating PKCS#12, giving priority to the friendly name and local key ID passed by the caller. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #21675; commits: 388a8e7)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0765] Fixed the recursive deadlock problem caused by memory allocation failure in OPENSSL_init_crypto(OPENSSL_INIT_LOAD_CRYPTO_STRINGS), and avoided recursive calls by introducing the temporary storage and recovery mechanism of the error string loading area. (Architecture-related: initialization behavior) (PRs: #21683; commits: 6b1a127)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0766] Fixed the backward compatibility issue of OSSL_HTTP_REQ_CTX_set_request_line function, now allowing path parameters to start with http:// for proxy scenarios. (Architecture-related: public API behavior) (PRs: #17923, #21690; commits: 45c0218)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0767] Added checks on i2d_X509_NAME return value in X509_NAME_hash_ex and X509_NAME_hash_old to avoid continuing to use invalid data when encoding fails. (Architecture-related: public API behavior) (PRs: #21697; commits: 945fde5)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0768] Fixed configuration of BIO helper functions in QUIC context: avoid using buffering, and set datagram socket type. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #21715; commits: 573f16c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0770] Added two missing privilegeWithdrawn and aACompromise entries in the OCSP CRLReason table. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #21743; commits: 1c8a7f5)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0772] Fixed the retry flag passing of dgram_bio in BIO connection, added TCP Fast Open support, simplified the release logic, and adjusted the IPv6 detection macro. (Architecture-related: TCP Fast Open support) (PRs: #21715; commits: abeb41b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0777] Fixed an issue where the assembler reported an error when loongarch_arch.h was included in the assembly file, and protected external variable declarations through conditional compilation. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #21838, #21839; commits: 84a0b1b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0779] Fix OPENSSL_init_crypto loading configuration into the current default library context instead of the initial global default library context during implicit initialization. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #21897; commits: ecb6cdf)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0781] Fixed a regression problem in the OSSL_PARAM_BLD_push_BN_pad() function that did not allow NULL BIGNUM to be passed in. NULL parameters are now supported again. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #21945; commits: 2ce79d9)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0783] Change the HTTP server listening address from IPv6 wildcard to universal wildcard to fix test failure on IPv4-only machines. (Architecture-related: Platform compatibility) (PRs: #21989; commits: b0da24b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0784] Fix bio_dgram_test failure on NonStop platform, adjust default buffer size. (Architecture-related: Platform compatibility) (PRs: #22013, #22058; commits: 572f290)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0786] Fixed the problem of linear search not working properly in CHARSET_EBCDIC environment, and improved the error handling and locking mechanism in OBJ_create. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #22108; commits: a47fc4e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0787] Added an error code for invalid attribute query for the random number module. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #21964; commits: a9483b8)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0790] Removed the initialization of FFC parameters in the init method of DH and DSA to maintain behavior consistent with version 1.1.1 and support methods of intercepting existing keys. (Architecture-related: external behavior) (PRs: #22185; commits: 706512e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0791] Modified the semantics of the FFC public key verification function so that it no longer returns failure when encountering a non-fatal error. Instead, it sets an error code and returns success, thereby preventing DH_check_pub_key from failing due to non-fatal problems. (Architecture-related: external behavior) (PRs: #22287, #22291; commits: eaee176)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0792] Fixed CVE-2023-5363: The key length and IV length parameters are processed in advance when the EVP cipher is initialized, to avoid security issues in the AEAD cipher where the parameters are processed too late, resulting in IV truncation or the use of uninitialized bytes. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #19822; commits: a2fe10c, f3a7e6c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0793] Fixed the problem of ssl.h compilation failure due to using gets as the parameter name in the MingW environment. Change the parameter name in the BIO_meth_set_gets function prototype to ossl_gets. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #22296, #22316; commits: 2e471a7)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0794] Fixed CMS related bugs: added missing error reporting call in CMS_add1_signer, and fixed algorithm OID check. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #22225, #22390, #22392; commits: 72a99ef, bd16091)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0795] Added a length check for excessively large Q parameters in the DH key calculation and generation functions, and unified the behavior of DH_check_pub_key. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #22453; commits: d73028b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0796] Fixed an issue where EVP_PKEY_get_bits, EVP_PKEY_get_security_bits and EVP_PKEY_get_size functions were not adding error entries to the error queue when failing. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #22459; commits: 51f4115)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0798] Fixed the drainage calculation logic in QUIC transmission and used more accurate send buffer judgment conditions. (Architecture-related: QUIC transmission behavior) (PRs: #22569; commits: 18a431b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0800] Fix the problem of socket non-blocking mode setting on Nonstop platform, use fcntl(F_GETFL) instead of FIONBIO; also fix the error handling when host or port in BIO_accept is NULL. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #22588, #22696; commits: 0ef3e13)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0801] Fix the ASN1_TIME object memory leak that may be caused when PKCS7_add_signed_attribute fails in PKCS7_add0_attrib_signing_time. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #22772; commits: 1ad7f4b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0802] Fix a possible memory leak in PKCS7_add_attrib_smimecap when PKCS7_add_signed_attribute fails. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #22744; commits: f06d408)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0803] Fixed a memory leak in CMS_sign_receipt caused by the incorrect path not releasing the ASN1_OCTET_STRING object. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #22758; commits: 757d649)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0804] Change the scope of the no_legacy_server_connect option from server to client. (Architecture-related: external behavior) (PRs: #18296; commits: d1b3b67)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0805] The ARMV7_TICK feature is no longer automatically detected and is only used if the user explicitly enables it via OPENSSL_armcap. (Architecture-related: Platform compatibility) (PRs: #18852; commits: f2ec24c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1487] Moved the maximum fragment length check from the SSL object to the record layer. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #18132; commits: ffbd6e6)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1488] Migrate the encryption code, record type processing, buffer allocation and other logic of each version of TLS/SSL from the general module to the record layer methods specific to each protocol, remove the DTLS special processing, and reconstruct the record layer write path. (Architecture event: Record layer method reconstruction) (PRs: #19217, #19343; commits: 2c50d7f, 7ca61d6, 91fe8ff, 9251c3c, a857267, aca70ca, bfda3ae)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1489] The discard_enc_level implementation of QUIC TXP no longer automatically notifies QTX to discard the encryption level, the caller needs to handle it by itself. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #19703; commits: df03868)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1490] Optimize the receiving processing logic of the QUIC channel, and only reprocess the receiving queue after receiving a new receiving end key. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #19703; commits: 92282a1)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1491] Connect the SSL_CTX control function and callback control function in the QUIC front-end I/O API to the standard SSL implementation, and remove the ossl_quic_conn_from_ssl function that is no longer needed. (Architectural event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #19703; commits: 8a1a6d6)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1492] Rename the want_net_read and want_net_write fields in the QUIC internal API, add protocol error handling logic, and improve transmission parameter processing. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #19703; commits: b639475)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1493] Reconstruct QUIC's SSL option setting mechanism, add ossl_quic_set_options, ossl_quic_clear_options and ossl_quic_get_options functions to limit QUIC connections to only allow some options. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #20061; commits: 18ca1c8, f0d9757)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1494] Optimize QUIC channel lock management: Move mutex locks out of the connection structure to support flexible initialization and destruction, and support releasing and reacquiring mutex locks during polling wait. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #20348; commits: 4847599, c019e1e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1495] Refactor the QUIC API: change the get_record() data field to const, change the front-end API parameter from QUIC_CONNECTION to SSL, introduce the QUIC_XSO type to replace QUIC_STREAM, and rename SSL_tick and SSL_get_tick_timeout to SSL_handle_events and SSL_get_event_timeout respectively. (Architecture events: SSL_Protocol_Engine module changes) (PRs: #19703, #20404, #20765, #20879; commits: 072328d, 2eb91b0, 6084e04, a1660c9, a35e38a, cb5c208, f8636c7)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1496] Enhanced QUIC functionality: Forward unknown control commands to underlying SSL, termination reason function returns constant pointer and adds status check, congestion control options interface migrates to OSSL_PARAM, reconstructs error promotion mechanism to support per-stream error handling. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #20423, #20514, #20765, #20856, #21484; commits: 723cbe8, 878df9b, b864110, f8ffab0, faa3a18)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1497] Rename the incoming stream deny policy related APIs and fields, and remove SSL_attach_stream and SSL_detach_stream from the public API to internal functions. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #20765; commits: 83df44a, de52162)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1498] Reconstruct QUIC flow state management: define flow status based on RFC, add status query function, remove recv_fin_retired field and use state machine instead. (Architecture event: QUIC protocol integration) (PRs: #21135; commits: 2f018d1, 5ed3a43)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1499] NewReno congestion control wake-up deadline changed to immediate, no need to wait. (Architecture event: QUIC protocol integration) (PRs: #21204; commits: b49d9de)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1500] Reconstruct QUIC TLS error handling, add raise_error function to uniformly record handshake layer errors and save error status, adjust ossl_quic_tls_get_error interface to return error status object. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #21547; commits: 7a2bb21)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1502] Update SSL error code definition, renumber stream-related error codes and add error description string. (Architecture-related: public API: error code definition) (PRs: #21135; commits: bac3f4d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1503] Migrate the load_csr_autofmt function to the shared library and update the call, adding verification option parameters. (Architecture-related: public API: load_csr_autofmt migration) (PRs: #15725, #18900; commits: 200d844)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1504] Rename OSSL_CMP_CTX_get0_trustedStore to OSSL_CMP_CTX_get0_trusted, and update related test cases. (Architecture-related: public API: function rename) (PRs: #17277; commits: 6be83cc)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1506] Modify X509_http_nbio and X509_CRL_http_nbio macro definitions to avoid including ocsp.h, and adjust header file dependencies. (Architecture-related: public API: header file dependency adjustments) (PRs: #17148, #17980; commits: f593f32)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1507] Change the parameter types of settings and options in recordmethod.h to const. (architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #18132; commits: 0c974fc)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1508] Remove the ossl_namemap_add_name_n API and simplify the internal implementation. (Architecture-related: public API removal) (PRs: #18341; commits: b00cf0e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1509] Refactor the ASN1_item_pack function, use ASN1_STRING_set0 instead of manual memory management, and correct error codes and length checks. (Architecture-related: public API behavior changes) (PRs: #18668; commits: 3384750)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1510] Extract GCM function pointers into independent structures, and add dedicated functions to initialize these function pointers. (Architecture-related: public API structure changes) (PRs: #18835; commits: 92c9086)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1511] Clean and refactor the implementation of X509_check_private_key and X509_REQ_check_private_key, and mark the parameters of X509_REQ_get0_pubkey and X509_REQ_check_private_key as const. (architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #18930; commits: aaabe58)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1512] Remove the const qualifier of the second parameter of the PKCS12_SAFEBAG_set0_attrs function. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #19359; commits: 9eaf07f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1513] Rename the record layer types SSL3_BUFFER and SSL3_RECORD to TLS_BUFFER and TLS_RL_RECORD respectively. (Architecture-related: Record layer type rename) (PRs: #18132, #19586; commits: 1704961, 22094d1, e9189cc)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1514] Simplify the custom interface of QUIC BIO polling descriptor and change the custom field from an array to a single pointer. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #19703; commits: 692df8d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1515] Rename the QUIC probe request API function name from ossl_ackm_get_probe_request to ossl_ackm_get0_probe_request, and remove the to-do annotation in the document. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #19925; commits: 2477e99)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1516] Refactor the aarch64 feature detection code, remove the SIGILL-based detection method, use more reliable methods such as getauxval and sysctl, and fix the HWCAP macro redefinition warning on FreeBSD. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #20188, #20305; commits: 52a3814)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1517] Rename OSSL_CMP_get_caCerts to OSSL_CMP_get1_caCerts, and improve OSSL_CMP_exec_certreq documentation. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #21129; commits: ec5a9cd)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1518] Partially roll back support for Windows CA certificate storage and restore the original certificate directory search logic. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #18070, #21190; commits: dfdbc11)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1520] Change BIO's reference counting from lock-based atomic operations to structure-based atomic operations. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #21260; commits: a22d196)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1521] Migrate the reference counting of ECX keys from lock-based atomic operations to structure-based atomic operations. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #21260; commits: 99b7bea)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1523] Update the reference count and lock release in SSL_SESSION_free to be atomic operations, and add the release of peer_rpk. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #21260; commits: 43a07d6)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1524] Update the reference counting implementation of encoders and decoders to struct-based atomic operations. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #21260; commits: 7d6ab12)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1525] Updated X509_STORE_free to use structure-based atomic operations. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #21260; commits: a903a13)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1526] Migrate the reference counting mechanism of various objects in the EVP module from atomic operations based on independent locks to atomic reference counting based on internal structures. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #21260; commits: 6be83ac)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1527] Remove unused QUIC server method declarations and implementations, and clean up related test code. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #20061; commits: 3f7b67f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1528] Change the BIO_CONNECT_free function to static, and clean up the error handling of memory allocation. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #21715; commits: ed28cb8)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1529] Rename the QUIC function SSL_set_initial_peer_addr to SSL_set1_initial_peer_addr. (architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #21701, #21814; commits: ce7a9e2)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1530] Change the rectype parameter type of the write_record function from int to uint8_t to unify the TLS record type representation. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #21823; commits: eb1eaa9)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1531] Revise the QUIC connection close information API, merge the is_local and is_transport fields in SSL_get_conn_close_info into the flags field. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #21905; commits: 7d9e447)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1532] Advance the deprecated version of lh_stats related functions from OpenSSL 3.2 to 3.1, and correct the relevant deprecated macro definitions. (Architecture-related: public API compatibility) (PRs: #22247; commits: 6a92159, 6f66602)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1533] Added internal hardening checks to HPKE implementation, including stricter parameter validation and more accurate error reporting. (Architecture-related: public API behavior) (PRs: #22493; commits: 2a4f8da)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1718] Fixed the problem of SSL_trace() in QUIC API test on big-endian platform, changing the type field of record template structure to unsigned character type. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #21411; commits: b6bf1cb)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1719] Add multi-stream support to QUIC test server, add tick, connection status and termination status query functions, and modify the read and write interface to support specified stream ID. (Architecture-related: Internal API: QUIC test server) (PRs: #20765, #20856, #21087; commits: 0345cac, 0554f72, 274bb48, 4f2d32d, 7ba8f79, 97f30fd, a350db7, b757beb, fca44cf)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1720] Add internal API for white-box testing key updates for QUIC, including setting TXKU threshold coverage, getting send and receive key epochs, and fixing idle timeout calculations and retry token handling. (Architecture-related: Internal API: QUIC key update testing) (PRs: #21029; commits: 16f3b54)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1723] Allow qtestlib to use simulation time implementation, and modify test cases to use this feature, removing sleep calls that may fail. (Architecture-related: Test framework changes: Simulation time) (PRs: #21332; commits: f9fcc7c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2033] Optimize the modulo exponential operation function, remove unused variables, adjust the initialization order, add parameter alias checking, and extend the acceleration path to support more modulus sizes. (Architecture-related: public API) (commits: f9a4e2b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2034] Optimize dual 1536/2048-bit modular exponentiation operation for Intel IceLake CPU, adopt AVX512_IFMA256 ISA, and extend related functions to support multiple modular bit widths. (Architecture-related: Platform compatibility) (PRs: #14908; commits: f87b4c4)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2035] Add runtime feature detection for ARM platforms to enable EOR3 instructions and loop unrolling optimizations to improve AES-GCM performance. (Architecture-related: Platform compatibility) (PRs: #15916; commits: 954f45b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2036] Enable vectorized AES-GCM implementation based on AVX512 vAES and vPCLMULQDQ instructions, and update structure member order annotations to match new assembly module dependencies. (Architecture-related: Platform compatibility) (PRs: #17239; commits: 63b996e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2037] Optimize ChaCha20 performance for ppc64le architecture, add support for POWER10 processor, and achieve about 50% performance improvement through 8-way parallel processing. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #16637; commits: f596bbe)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2038] Add RISC-V hardware acceleration support to the SHA512 algorithm, and introduce macro definitions based on RISC-V extension instructions through conditional compilation. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #16638; commits: 611685a)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2041] Add MD5 assembly implementation for aarch64 architecture, enable it by modifying the conditional compilation macro, significantly improve performance. (Architecture-related: Platform compatibility: aarch64 MD5 assembly) (PRs: #16928; commits: 04904a0)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2042] Use SM3 hardware instructions to accelerate the SM3 algorithm on the aarch64 platform, and automatically detect hardware support. Platforms that do not support it still use the original C implementation. (Architecture-related: Platform compatibility: aarch64 SM3 hardware acceleration) (PRs: #17454; commits: 71396cd)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2043] Adds clmul instruction-based GCM multiplication acceleration for RISC-V 64-bit architecture, supports implementation selection through runtime detection of Zbb and Zbc extensions, and retains the C language fallback implementation. (Architecture-related: Platform compatibility: RISC-V GCM multiplication acceleration) (PRs: #17640; commits: 999376d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2044] Add support for SVE extension on aarch64, and use SVE instructions to accelerate ChaCha20 encryption, improving performance by up to 20%. (Architecture-related: Platform compatibility: aarch64 SVE ChaCha20 acceleration) (PRs: #17916; commits: b1b2146)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2046] Optimize the EVP_MD_CTX_copy_ex function to reduce frequent writes to the EVP_MD object reference count and alleviate cache competition issues when multi-threads are used frequently. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #17064, #17857; commits: c0b7dac)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2047] Add assembly implementation based on RISC-V Zksh extension for SM3 algorithm, supporting both RV32 and RV64 architecture. (Architecture-related: RISC-V support) (PRs: #18287; commits: 7ae2bc9)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2048] Add ROTATE inline assembly support for SM3, and move ROTATE inline assembly to header files to simultaneously optimize SM3, SHA and other hash functions. (Architecture-related: core header file changes) (PRs: #18287; commits: eea820f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2049] Enable AES-GCM loop unrolling and EOR3 optimization for Neoverse N2 processor to improve performance. (Architecture-related: Platform compatibility: Neoverse N2) (PRs: #18350; commits: 9224a40)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2050] Optimize the implementation of tolower and related character judgment functions, use direct ASCII range comparison instead of table lookup to avoid memory access to improve performance; at the same time, change some macro definitions to function declarations and adjust the interface signature. (Architecture-related: public API: interface signature adjustment) (PRs: #18344; commits: 286053f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2051] Reintroducing the fixed-length (n=6) PPC Montgomery multiplication optimization and adding support for the Power10 MADD300 instruction. (Architecture-related: Platform compatibility: Power10) (PRs: #18883; commits: eae7010)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2053] Add AES's VPAES vector extension optimization to the LoongArch64 architecture and define the corresponding capability detection macro. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #19364; commits: ef91754)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2054] Add SM4 optimization support to the Kunpeng-920 platform, enabling related acceleration by identifying HiSilicon CPU models. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #19547; commits: 88c53cf)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2055] Optimize the performance of FIPS RSA key generation, use modular inversion operation to replace the greatest common divisor test, and add the BN_are_coprime function; at the same time, adjust the number of Miller-Rabin prime number test rounds to the value specified by FIPS 186-5, replacing the original fixed 64 rounds. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #19578, #19579; commits: d2f6e66, dd1d7bc)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2056] Optimize GCM mode performance for the RISC-V platform, choose different implementations according to the extensions supported by the runtime (Zbc, Zbkb, Zbb), use the clmul instruction and reduce register usage; at the same time, integrate GHASH hardware acceleration to enable the GCM mode to obtain complete hardware acceleration capabilities on RISC-V. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #20078; commits: b246843, f3fed0d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2057] On the PowerPC64LE platform, enable the assembly implementation of felem_square and felem_mul when Altivec and ISA 3.0 (Power 9 and above) support is detected. (Architecture-related: Platform compatibility) (PRs: #21471; commits: 966047e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2059] Enabled additional optimizations for GHASH, RAND and AES for Arm64 platforms on Windows. (Architecture-related: Windows Arm64 optimizations) (PRs: #21673; commits: 636ee1d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2126] Fixed memory security issues in the EVP module caused by too small buffers or integer overflows, including signed integer overflows in EVP_EncryptUpdate and EVP_DecryptUpdate. (Architecture-related: EVP interface security fixes) (PRs: #16789, #17869, #17870; commits: 1832bb0, 43da9a1)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2127] An implicit rejection mechanism is introduced in RSA PKCS#1 v1.5 decryption, and a random message generated based on the private key and ciphertext is returned when the padding check fails to prevent Bleichenbacher side channel attacks. (Architecture-related: RSA PKCS#1 v1.5 implicit rejection) (PRs: #13817; commits: 7fc67e0, 8ae4f0e, c3aed7e, ddecbef)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2128] Fix the out-of-bounds access problem caused by IV length check in CBC and CTR modes, switch to more secure boundary verification and return errors. (Architecture-related: CBC/CTR mode security fix) (PRs: #17898; commits: d1592f2)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2129] Fix the problem of signature verification errors returning positive values in OCSP_basic_verify to avoid incorrectly trusting OCSP responses under the OCSP_NOCHECKS flag. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #18053; commits: 21f89f5)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2130] Disable SSL3, TLS1.0, TLS1.1 and DTLS1.0 at security level 1 and above, and only allow the use of these protocols at security level 0. (Architecture-related: Security policy and protocol compatibility) (PRs: #18194, #18236; commits: 7bf2e4d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2131] Enable and implement the security callback function in the record layer to replace the original compression security detection logic. (Architecture-related: Record layer security callback) (PRs: #18132; commits: ed0e298)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2132] Add a default private key length based on RFC7919 for the DH named group, and automatically set a smaller key length when generating security prime parameters to improve performance; at the same time, modify the private key generation logic to require that security strength parameters must be provided. (Architecture-related: DH private key generation behavior) (PRs: #18480; commits: ddb13b2)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2133] Fix the use of custom EVP_CIPHER and EVP_MD objects, ensure that initialization uses custom objects first, and fix possible null pointer dereferences in digest processing. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #18970, #19300; commits: 25d47cc)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2134] Add a second verification to the public key check function to ensure that the public key meets the requirements of SP 800-56A. (Architecture-related: public key verification) (PRs: #19510; commits: 5b234be)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2136] Fixed the protection type mismatch problem in CMP message verification, rejecting mismatched protection methods. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #19729; commits: fc93335)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2138] Fix the comparison method of x400Address in GENERAL_NAME_cmp, replace ASN1_TYPE_cmp with ASN1_STRING_cmp, fix CVE-2023-0286. (Architecture-related: public API behavior change) (commits: 7880536)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2141] In EVP_PKEY_get_bn_param() ensure that the successfully filled temporary buffer is erased before being released to prevent sensitive data from remaining. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #20639; commits: 34e4a96)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2142] Added negative integer check to ASN1_BIT_STRING_set_bit and ASN1_BIT_STRING_get_bit functions to prevent potential overflow issues. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #20719, #20862; commits: 1258a8e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2143] The length of OBJECT IDENTIFIER that can be processed by the OBJ_obj2txt function is limited to no more than 586 bytes to prevent performance issues caused by over-long sub-identifiers (CVE-2023-2650). (Architecture-related: public API) (commits: d63b3e7)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2144] Fixed the denial of service vulnerability in DH_check() when handling extremely large modulus, and added a maximum modulus check (32768 bits). When this limit is exceeded, an error will be returned directly. (Architecture-related: public API: DH_check adds a maximum modulus check) (PRs: #21451; commits: 9e0094e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2145] Fixed CVE-2023-3817 and added a check for the validity of the q parameter in DH_check to avoid denial of service attacks caused by invalid q values. (Architecture-related: public API: DH_check adds a new q parameter check) (PRs: #21384, #21451, #21550; commits: 1c16253, 1e398be, 4b29762, 4ec53ad)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2146] Changed the default algorithm of TSA's ess_cert_id_alg option from sha1 to sha256 to improve security. (Architecture-related: public API: ess_cert_id_alg default value changed to SHA256) (PRs: #21794, #21845; commits: 10536b7, 305dc68)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2147] Changed the default salt length of PBES2 KDF from 8 bytes to 16 bytes to meet FIPS compliance requirements. (Architecture-related: External behavior) (PRs: #21858; commits: 3859a02)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2211] Add man pages for SSL_get_certificate and SSL_get_privatekey, and update the missing documentation list. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #17815; commits: 2a92195)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2212] Update the QUIC API design document, add multi-stream operation API definition, and rename some APIs (such as SSL_tick to SSL_handle_events). (Architecture-related: public API rename) (PRs: #19769; commits: 64aa8ea, e4c2988)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2215] Add comments to the OPENSSL_INIT_set_config_filename and OPENSSL_INIT_set_config_appname functions to explain why standard strdup is used instead of OPENSSL_strdup. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #21742; commits: 26f75c2)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2320] Disable P10-specific Chacha20 and AES-GCM assembly implementations on AIX systems to avoid build issues. (Architecture-related: Platform Compatibility) (PRs: #18145, #19865; commits: 50d9b2b, abfc152)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2322] Add optional support for Armv8.3-A Pointer Authentication and Armv8.5-A Branch Target Identification in aarch64 assembly code, controlled through compiler macros. (Architecture-related: ARM security feature support) (PRs: #16674; commits: 19e277d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2323] Remove the explicit inclusion of synchapi.h which does not exist in WinSDK 7.1, and use windows.h which already includes this header file instead. (Architecture-related: Windows platform compatibility) (PRs: #16603; commits: eeb6120)
