- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2327] Enable USE_SWAPCONTEXT on IA64 architecture, use swapcontext instead of setjmp/longjmp to correctly save the register stack engine, fix asynchronous interface test failure. (Architecture-related: IA64 architecture support) (PRs: #17370; commits: d26b376)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2328] On the s390x architecture, mark the internal cpuid symbol OPENSSL_s390xcap_P and the function OPENSSL_cpuid_setup as hidden to avoid accidentally becoming global symbols in static libraries. (Architecture-related: symbol visibility) (PRs: #17946; commits: 37816ef)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2329] Fix compilation failure on macOS 10.7 and 10.8 due to incompatibility of clang's __atomic_is_lock_free parameter, by disabling the __atomic_* built-in functions on these systems. (Architecture-related: Platform compatibility) (PRs: #18055, #18056; commits: d39de47)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2332] Added compilation configuration option OPENSSL_NO_UNIX_SOCK, used to control Unix domain socket support, and adjusted related conditional compilation logic. (Architecture-related: build configuration) (PRs: #18256; commits: 081f348)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2333] Fix UWP build, VirtualLock function is manually declared in memory safety related code. (Architecture-related: Platform compatibility) (PRs: #18311; commits: ff1efe6)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2334] For HP-UX platform, avoid including non-existent sys/select.h and use sys/time.h instead. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #18395; commits: 737e849)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2336] Added Windows on Arm build target, supported using clang-cl as assembler and compiler, and updated armcap source code to adapt to Windows platform feature detection. (Architecture-related: Platform compatibility: Windows on Arm) (PRs: #19523, #20081, #20525; commits: 4a3b626, b863e1e, e9460bb)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2338] Support building OpenSSL with QUIC functionality disabled, add conditional compilation protection for internal functions and test cases, and add no-quic option in CI configuration for m68k cross-compilation. (Architecture-related: Build and installation mode: Support for building with QUIC disabled) (PRs: #19703, #21332; commits: 6292519, d001006)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2339] Disable atomic reference counting to support threadless builds, falling back to normal addition. (Architecture-related: Build and installation mode: Support threadless builds) (PRs: #19751; commits: b484c62)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2343] Fixed strict warning issue in Windows build, removed unnecessary const qualifier in CMP API to keep declaration consistent with definition. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #20436; commits: 6f792f4)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2344] Fix the OPENSSL_armcap_P symbol duplication problem on the ARM platform, limit its definition to 64-bit architecture, and ensure that only one copy is kept in libcrypto.a. (Architecture-related: Platform compatibility) (PRs: #20558, #20621; commits: 7b508cd, 93370db)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2345] Disable P10-specific AES-GCM assembly code builds on macOS. (Architecture-related: Platform Compatibility) (PRs: #20543; commits: 175645a)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2346] Fix build errors caused by missing InterlockedOr64 in the Visual Studio 2010 x86 compiler and extend conditional macro coverage to this compiler version. (Architecture-related: Platform compatibility) (PRs: #20557; commits: 8bdc370)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2347] Fixed a compilation error caused by incompatible function pointer types when using clang-cl 16 or higher. (Architecture-related: Platform compatibility) (PRs: #20654; commits: fae5a15)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2348] Introduces the compile option no-ecx, which is used to remove ECX related features, thereby reducing the binary size for platforms that do not require this feature. (Architecture-related: build option no-ecx) (PRs: #20781; commits: 4032cd9)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2349] Introduce HAVE_MADVISE and NO_MADVISE macro definitions to allow builders to explicitly control whether to call the madvise function in environments that lack it. (Architecture-related: Build Configuration: madvise control) (PRs: #20851; commits: 78634e8)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2350] Disable floating point related code in UEFI environment to avoid build problems caused by floating point operations. (Architecture-related: Platform compatibility: UEFI floating point disabled) (PRs: #20992; commits: 2c50057)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2352] Allows disabling HTTP support at compile time, and adds conditional compilation protection to related code. (Architecture-related: Build configuration: HTTP support can be disabled) (PRs: #21108; commits: 3ca28c9)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2355] Fix WebAssembly/WASI build, force use of timegm and add WASI support for base implementation OPENSSL_issetugid. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #21389; commits: 66f61ec)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2356] Fix the problem of build failure when IPv6 is disabled, and add conditional compilation protection for IPv6 related code. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #21495; commits: 9c8d04d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2357] Fixed compilation errors caused by missing SIZE_MAX definition on HP-UX and added necessary header file references in ssl/priority_queue.c. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #21554, #21562; commits: 7f14656)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2358] Moved the ALIGN32 and ALIGN64 macro definitions to common.h, and fixed the compilation problem caused by the lack of __GNUC__ definition under clang-cl.exe. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #21914, #21921; commits: 12d08fe)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2360] Added necessary header file inclusion in ssl/quic/quic_cfq.c to support platforms that do not define macros such as UINT64_MAX. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #21951; commits: cfbdc5d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2362] Fixed build failure due to lack of sendmmsg in older versions of glibc, by adding glibc version checking to ensure support for both recvmmsg and sendmmsg. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #22021, #22036; commits: 5d96106)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2459] Remove the legacy compatibility code of the VxWorks platform and use the standard sys/un.h header file uniformly. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #18260; commits: a668012)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2460] Fix the compilation problem of thread library functions on non-UNIX platforms, and limit the function definitions in threads_lib.c to only compile under UNIX systems. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #19322; commits: 523e54c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2462] Revise the field types in the SSL_get_conn_close_info related structure, change is_local and is_transport from char to int. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #20765; commits: bb9b8a3)

### Engine Plugin Framework

- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0252] Added {lib}_R_{lib}_LIB error library reference support for external modules such as engines, and implemented the corresponding error library initialization function in the e_capi engine. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #19301; commits: 79c8dcf)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0272] Remove the conditional compilation of ENGINE related categories in trace categories, so that all categories always exist, and optimize the category search logic. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #19915, #19917; commits: 78bd646)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0310] Add SP800-56Br2 6.4.1.2.1 (3.c) requirement for RSA key pair checking, ensuring key length is a positive even number. (Architecture-related: RSA key pair checking standard) (PRs: #22403; commits: 8b26854)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0533] Fixed the problem that EVP_CIPHER_CTX_copy in the dasync engine could not correctly copy the cipher context. (Architecture-related: public API: EVP_CIPHER_CTX_copy) (PRs: #16844, #16846; commits: a0cbc2d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0544] Fixed an issue where applying static linking to libcrypto/libssl would cause the engine to crash when using a different OpenSSL runtime, by preventing the engine's libcrypto from installing an atexit handler. (Architecture-related: Platform compatibility) (PRs: #15898, #17112; commits: 9362a1b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0572] When dynamically loading the engine, detect whether the engine is linked to OpenSSL 1.1.x (by checking the EVP_PKEY_base_id symbol), and if so, abort the load to avoid a crash. (Architecture-related: Platform compatibility) (PRs: #17112; commits: 14db620)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0693] Improved the detection logic of traditional keys when the engine loads private keys. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #17092, #17286, #19965; commits: 2b74e75)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1519] Update the reference counting operation of the engine module to a structure-based atomic operation, and add inline functions. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #21260; commits: e362070)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1717] Fixed enginetest failing when compiling with deprecated API disabled and specifying API version 1.1.1, added missing header include. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #17649, #17652; commits: 29af9fb)

### Provider Plugin Framework

- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0012] Removed DES cryptographic algorithm from FIPS provider. (Architecture-related: FIPS provider algorithm removed) (PRs: #19510; commits: fc0bb34)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0031] Added the ability to obtain EVP methods by specified provider, including introducing provider parameters in the underlying constructor, adding multiple internal functions for obtaining signatures, asymmetric cryptography, key exchange and KEM methods by provider, and supporting cache acquisition. (Architecture-related: Provider method acquisition) (PRs: #16614, #16725; commits: cfce50f, dc010ca, ff77814)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0032] Allow providers to override error mark functions, move ERR_set_mark, ERR_pop_to_mark and ERR_clear_last_mark to independent source files and add a new ERR_count_to_mark function; legacy providers use the upcall mechanism to reimplement ERR related functions. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #17474; commits: 8c2e588, fbe8870)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0034] Added an interface to remove all registered methods by provider, and modified the cleanup behavior when the provider is deactivated or self-check fails, instead of directly clearing the associated methods instead of just refreshing the cache; at the same time, the activation and deactivation logic is optimized. (Architecture-related: Provider method cleanup) (PRs: #16980, #18151; commits: 2e4d067, c59fc87)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0035] Removed AES SIV cryptographic algorithm from FIPS provider. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #20386; commits: c3f985c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0037] Remove the redundant internal header file include/internal/decoder.h, migrate the function declarations therein to include/crypto/decoder.h, and update the include paths of related source files. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #21733; commits: 98d8117)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0043] Adjust the sub-provider reference counting logic. Self-referential sub-providers no longer increase or release the reference count of the parent provider to avoid hindering the parent provider from unloading; non-self-referential sub-providers retain reference count propagation. (Architecture-related: provider reference counting) (PRs: #18151; commits: 4da7663)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0066] Added PVK KDF (PIN Verification Key key derivation function) implementation in providers. (Architecture event: Added PVK KDF implementation) (PRs: #15968, #17164, #17572, #19529; commits: 1ffac6c, 2d34e5b, 722fe8e, 8df9f34, a632bfa, fe01052)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0070] Added SM4-GCM and SM4-CCM algorithm support, including implementation and OID definition. (Architecture event: Provider_Crypto_Implementations added SM4-GCM/CCM algorithm) (PRs: #16491, #16731; commits: a596d38, c2ee608)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0071] Add default provider support for Keccak-224, 256, 384 and 512 algorithms, including s390x hardware acceleration, provider registration, function implementation and name definition. (Architecture event: Provider_Crypto_Implementations module change) (PRs: #13033, #16594; commits: 524f126)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0072] Added Argon2 key derivation function (KDF) implementation, supporting three modes: Argon2d, Argon2i and Argon2id. (Architecture event: Provider_Crypto_Implementations module change) (PRs: #12256, #20531, #20534; commits: 6dfa998, a901b31, c77fb9a)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0073] Add XOF (Extensible Output Function) support to the blake2b512 digest algorithm, allowing the output length to be set via context parameters. (Architecture event: Provider_Crypto_Implementations module changes) (PRs: #12256, #17529; commits: 0be4b04, 786b9a8)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0077] Added support for loading root certification authorities (CAs) from the Windows Crypto API. (Architecture Event: Added Windows Store support) (PRs: #18020, #18070; commits: 606e042)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0078] The AES-GCM-SIV algorithm defined in RFC8452 is implemented in the default provider. (Architecture event: Added AES-GCM-SIV algorithm) (PRs: #16721, #18693; commits: 0113ec8)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0083] Added a declaration of the receiver depacket function ossl_quic_depacketize in the QUIC header file. (Architecture event: QUIC protocol engine module change) (PRs: #18610; commits: d7fed97)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0084] Implement the initial versions of the QUIC ACK manager, statistics manager and congestion control API, and add supporting testing and design documentation. (Architecture event: QUIC protocol engine module changes) (PRs: #18018, #18676, #20423, #21349; commits: 422368a, 79a80f8, 891b639, fa4e92a, fd6c147)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0085] Added QUIC frame encoding and decoding functions, and added corresponding test cases. (Architecture event: QUIC protocol engine module change) (PRs: #18795, #20914; commits: b09e246, dffafaf)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0086] Implement deterministic ECDSA and DSA signatures based on RFC6979, add HMAC_DRBG KDF implementation, and introduce the nonce_type parameter in the signature API. (Architecture event: Encryption provider implementation module change) (PRs: #9223, #18809; commits: 6b3d287, f3090fc)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0087] Support decoding SM2 parameters, adjust the elliptic curve key text output function so that it correctly displays the parameter type when the curve is SM2. (Architecture event: Crypto provider implementation module change) (PRs: #18819; commits: 08ae9fa)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0088] A new QUIC receiving end depacketizer is added, which implements frame processing functions, flow management logic and frame validity checking based on packet type. (Architecture event: QUIC receiving end depacketizer is added) (PRs: #18838; commits: 69ed676, 8a16364)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0089] Added QUIC demultiplexer and record layer receiver, supporting packet parsing, header protection, key derivation and related testing. (Architecture event: QUIC demultiplexer and record layer receiver added) (PRs: #18838, #18949; commits: ec279ac, ecc920b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0090] Implemented functionality for obtaining human-readable status strings for the record layer. (Architecture Events: Record Layer Status String Function) (PRs: #18132; commits: d0b17ea)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0091] Record layer object's settings such as options, mode and read-ahead can now be updated after instantiation. (Architecture events: Record layer settings can be updated after instantiation) (PRs: #18132; commits: 4566dae)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0092] Added a custom recording layer setting function, and optimized the recording layer error handling logic. (Architecture event: Custom recording layer setting function) (PRs: #18132; commits: d3192c2)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0093] Added receiving time field and decoded packet sequence number field, and supports sending end key update. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #18949; commits: 948c656)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0094] Implemented the QUIC flow control function, and added sender and receiver flow control controllers. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #19040, #19703; commits: 508e087, c4abf9e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0095] Implemented DHKEM algorithm for EC, X25519 and X448 curves at the provider layer, and added authentication encapsulation and decapsulation API. (Architecture event: Provider_Crypto_Implementations module change) (PRs: #16495, #17172, #19068; commits: 78c44b0, d7b5f06)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0096] Implemented QUIC send stream management functions, including ACK processing and integer collection data structures. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #19159; commits: 8302259)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0097] Added QUIC congestion feedback queue (CFQ) implementation, including queue management, frame addition, tag sending/loss, release and other core functions. (Architecture event: QUIC protocol module is added) (PRs: #19206; commits: c282da8)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0098] Implemented the QUIC Transport Packet Information Manager (TXPIM). (Architecture event: QUIC protocol module added) (PRs: #19206; commits: d77aea5)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0099] Added implementation of QUIC frame loss detection (FIFD) module. (Architecture event: QUIC protocol module added) (PRs: #19206; commits: 0ede517)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0100] Added RAND_set0_public and RAND_set0_private functions for public and private DRBGs, and modified DRBG creation logic to support injection of entropy in FIPS known answer tests. (Architectural event: Provider_Crypto_Implementations module changes) (PRs: #17479, #19510; commits: 7c8187d, 8ff861d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0101] Added implementation of QUIC TX packet packer and stream mapper, including frame generation, acknowledgment, loss handling, stream state management. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #19346; commits: a73078b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0102] Implemented preliminary support for loading signature algorithms from providers, and restructured signature algorithm-related data structures and functions in certificates and SSL contexts. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #16964, #19312; commits: 3ffd23e, ee58915)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0121] Implements hybrid public key encryption (HPKE) defined by RFC9180, supports all modes, suites and export mechanisms, and provides corresponding APIs, documentation and tests. (Architecture event: Added HPKE module) (PRs: #17172, #19068; commits: ad06248)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0122] Added XTS mode implementation for SM4 algorithm, supporting encryption/decryption, context management and parameter settings. (Architecture event: Provider_Crypto_Implementations module added SM4-XTS encryption mode) (PRs: #19619; commits: 2788b56)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0123] Added support for AES-128/192/256 GCM-SIV mode in FIPS provider. (Architecture event: Provider_Crypto_Implementations module added AES-GCM-SIV encryption mode) (PRs: #19777; commits: edaab86)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0143] Added datagram injection support for QUIC connections and added SSL_inject_net_dgram function. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change (QUIC) (PRs: #20451; commits: 553a4e0)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0144] Added SSL_is_tls() and SSL_is_quic() functions to determine protocol type. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change (Protocol Type API) (PRs: #20650, #20765; commits: 3e5a47d, 50769b1, 843f6e2)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0145] Add QUIC stream creation and management API, support any number of streams, including local and remote stream creation, and implement flow control and error handling. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change (QUIC stream API) (PRs: #20765; commits: 26ad16e, 2dbc39d, 9715e3a, ed83567, f20fdd1)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0146] Implement the SSL_get0_connection function for QUIC connections, allowing retrieval of the underlying SSL object. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change (SSL_get0_connection API) (PRs: #20765; commits: 020d038)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0147] Added processing of OSSL_PKEY_PARAM_EC_PUB_X,Y requests for EC legacy keys to support tpm2-tss loading legacy EC keys. (Architecture event: Provider_Crypto_Implementations module changes (EC key parameters) (PRs: #20535; commits: 9adbce7)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0148] Refactor the FIPS option processing code, introduce a unified option structure, and add new options to limit the summaries available to DRBG. (Architecture event: Provider_Crypto_Implementations module changes (FIPS option refactoring) (PRs: #20521; commits: 83ccf81)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0149] Added QUIC stream setting and acceptance API, including SSL_set_default_stream_mode, SSL_accept_stream and SSL_get_accept_queue_len. (Architecture event: QUIC protocol engine change) (PRs: #20765; commits: 8b7be3a, cb68ce9)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0150] Added QUIC incoming stream rejection policy API and automatic rejection implementation, supporting SSL_set_incoming_stream_reject_policy. (Architecture event: QUIC protocol engine change) (PRs: #20765; commits: 8a90df3, 995ff28)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0151] Implement QUIC MAX_STREAMS flow control, including receive stream controller support and receive path implementation. (Architecture event: QUIC protocol engine change) (PRs: #20765; commits: 5bd9ddd, 90cecc4, a6b6ea1)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0152] Implement QUIC implicit stream creation, automatically create streams when receiving remote STREAM frames, and comply with RFC requirements. (Architecture event: QUIC protocol engine change) (PRs: #20765, #20856; commits: 999e13f, dea57ec)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0153] Added QUIC stream garbage collection mechanism, including stream full confirmation judgment and sending partial ID guarantee function. (Architecture event: QUIC protocol engine change) (PRs: #20765; commits: 0847e63)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0154] Add frame acknowledgment and stream update callback support to QUIC FIFD initialization function. (Architecture event: QUIC protocol engine change) (PRs: #20765; commits: 9cacba4)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0155] Added the function of querying the STOP_SENDING and RESET_STREAM status of the peer for QUIC TSERVER. (Architecture event: QUIC protocol engine change) (PRs: #19703, #20765, #21565; commits: cbe7f58, d63b8cb, f0e22d1)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0156] Added the ability to pop up new inbound streams for the QUIC test server, and improved stream reset detection. (Architecture event: QUIC protocol engine change) (PRs: #20856; commits: 1df479a)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0157] The QUIC stream map initialization function extends parameters to be aware of the server role, and initializes more internal state. (Architecture event: QUIC stream map interface change) (PRs: #20856; commits: 5915a90)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0158] Add initial QUIC support for msg_callback, which currently only supports triggering callbacks when datagrams are received. (Architecture events: msg_callback QUIC support) (PRs: #20914; commits: 63dfde8, bfcf135)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0159] Add tracing support for QUIC datagrams, extending SSL_trace to dump QUIC datagram reception information. (Architecture event: SSL_trace QUIC datagram tracing) (PRs: #20914; commits: 45aac10)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0160] Modify the SSL_get_event_timeout API, add the is_infinite parameter to distinguish whether the timeout is infinite, and add support for DTLSv1 timeout control commands in the QUIC implementation to maintain compatibility. (Architecture event: SSL_get_event_timeout API changes) (PRs: #20879; commits: 2f90ea3, 7ea4971, b626a0f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0161] Adds SSL_trace support for QUIC packets, extends tracing capabilities to support multiple frame types and sent frames, and adds trace callback support for sent QUIC packets. (Architecture Event: SSL_trace QUIC Packet Tracing) (PRs: #20914; commits: 2a35a1b, 45454cc, 70f0ea2, e8528c9)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0162] Add NEW_CONNECTION_ID frame processing, adjust active connection ID limits, support recording key periods, add a new ACK send callback interface, allow encoding and decoding to retain header bits, and implement RFC 9000 Section 9.6 requirements to discard packets from new server addresses that have not been migrated. (Architectural event: QUIC protocol implements multiple changes) (PRs: #20892, #21029, #21135; commits: 3ffb7d1, 5b9452e, 754d228, 8f9c921, eff0465)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0187] The Store module adds a new deletion API OSSL_STORE_delete, and extends the loader registration to support deletion and opening extended functions. (Architecture event: the Store module adds a new deletion API) (PRs: #21901, #22251; commits: 0a8807b, 3f8b7b9)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0202] Added support for random number generation based on the RNDR/RNDRRS instructions for the ARM64 platform and used it as the default seed source. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #15361; commits: e8b597f, eb28fda, efa1f22)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0211] Added support for EncryptedPrivateKeyInfo output for ECX key encoding, and fixed related error codes. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #16466; commits: 0195cdd, 602bfb8)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0222] Add AES hardware acceleration support for RISC-V 64-bit and 32-bit platforms, covering ZKND/ZKNE/Zbkb instruction sets and multiple encryption modes. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #18197, #18308; commits: 5ccee69, 77d29ff, cbb15b3, ee11118)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0255] Add RIPEMD160 digest algorithm to the default provider. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #17722, #19375; commits: ecd8314)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0256] Supports all five EdDSA instances in RFC 8032 (Ed25519, Ed25519ctx, Ed25519ph, Ed448, Ed448ph), specify the instance through the EVP API and OSSL_PARAM, and allows passing in the context string. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #6277, #19705; commits: 836080a)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0257] Updated the KAT test of FIPS 140-3, modified the signature self-test function to set the DRBG status before each test, and added functions to reset and set the main DRBG; also added test cases for RSA, EC and DSA key generation self-test failures in FIPS mode, and fixed the problem that the buffer size parameter was not set correctly in the signature self-test. (Architecture-related: FIPS self-test behavior) (PRs: #16789, #19510, #20183; commits: 61adb6c, a11064c, dcd20cc)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0258] Added the -self_test_oninstall option to the fipsinstall command, which is used to run self-tests during installation; at the same time, the default behavior is changed to be controlled by the -self_test_onload option. (Architecture-related: FIPS installation behavior) (PRs: #19510; commits: 7057ddd)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0263] Rejoin 3DES into the FIPS provider and provide it as a non-approved algorithm for backward compatibility; update related documentation and testing at the same time. (Architecture-related: FIPS provider) (PRs: #19510, #19631, #20079, #20219; commits: 4072a76, 464c101, a0ea8ac, ccc860a, d4e105f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0267] Implement the OSSL_PROVIDER_get0_default_search_path function, and add documentation and tests. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #19752; commits: d3db25f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0270] Added two new error codes, PROV_R_INVALID_MEMORY_SIZE and PROV_R_INVALID_THREAD_POOL_SIZE, and their corresponding error description strings in the provider error system. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #12256; commits: 232dd87)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0273] Add KMAC support to KBKDF, and extend the input key and context size limit to 512 bytes. (Architecture-related: external behavior) (PRs: #17671, #19916; commits: 211c47c, bbbd121)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0275] ECDSA and DSA signing contexts can now retrieve the OSSL_SIGNATURE_PARAM_NONCE_TYPE parameter. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #20070; commits: 1d85794)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0281] Add option to force EMS check in TLS1_PRF KDF for FIPS module. (Architecture-related: FIPS configuration) (PRs: #19989, #20241; commits: 50ea5cd)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0282] Add keymgmt import/export type function extension with provider context, so that provider can dynamically return parameter array according to configuration and maintain backward compatibility. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #20255; commits: 5e3b845)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0288] Add the macro OSSL_DISPATCH_END as the end marker of the OSSL_DISPATCH array. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #20710, #20745; commits: 1e6bd31, 23e6489)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0294] Added support for SHA256/192 hash algorithm, which is a variant of SHA256 output truncated to 192 bits. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #21180; commits: 81bafac)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0297] Add limited random number support to WebAssembly WASI target, implemented based on getentropy. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #21344; commits: d88a0f5)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0301] Added OSSL_PROVIDER_load_ex function to support passing parameters when loading provider; refactored related loading functions. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #21604; commits: 9d2f7e1)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0305] Add dupctx support for aes-gcm, aes-ccm, aria-gcm and aria-ccm cryptographic implementations, by copying the context and fixing the internal key pointer. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #21887, #21933; commits: 0239fb3)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0307] FIPS self-test uses test RNG instead, avoids relying on real random number generators, and keeps DRBG settings original for subsequent replacement of seed sources. (Architecture-related: FIPS self-test) (PRs: #21964; commits: fffa78c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0308] Add the association of ECDSA and SHA3 hash functions in the object cross-reference table to support the ECDSA with SHA3 signature algorithm. (Architecture-related: public API: ECDSA with SHA3 signature algorithm support) (PRs: #22147; commits: 98e0755, de4aa81)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0311] Add user cleaning callbacks for random number entropy and nonce, and update the cleaning function to preferentially use user-provided callbacks. (Architecture-related: public API: Nonce entropy cleaning callback) (PRs: #22423; commits: 5516d20)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0312] Changed the settable parameters of BLAKE2b digest from OSSL_DIGEST_PARAM_XOFLEN to OSSL_DIGEST_PARAM_SIZE, added digest size validity check, and added support for obtainable parameters. (Architecture-related: public API parameter changes) (PRs: #22488, #22491; commits: 6a0ae39)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0440] Reconstruct the check logic for the consistency of the destination connection ID of the data packet in the QUIC receiving path to avoid use-after-free vulnerabilities caused by incorrect format of the first data packet. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #19703; commits: 0f7b5cc)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0441] Fixed an issue where QUIC channels were not properly handling delayed packets after generating a new key at the handshake layer, making sure to recheck the receive queue after getting the new key. (Architecture Event: SSL_Protocol_Engine Module Change) (PRs: #19703; commits: 4e64437)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0442] Fixed the reference counting and release logic of BIO in the QUIC front-end I/O API, transferred the release responsibility from QUIC_CHANNEL to QUIC_CONNECTION, and renamed related functions to reflect the ownership change. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #19703; commits: d1ac77b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0443] Fixed the implementation of SSL_get_error in the QUIC front-end I/O API and adjusted the order of QUIC error checking. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #19734; commits: e30c502)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0444] Fixed the WANT_READ signal problem of SSL_read in the QUIC front-end I/O API, ensuring that the WANT_READ error is correctly returned when no data is read in non-blocking mode. (Architecture event: SSL_Protocol_Engine module change) (PRs: #19734; commits: af8b52c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0521] Fixed the problem of false errors caused by unregistered scheme in OSSL_STORE_open_ex(). (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #16452; commits: 7c64ca7)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0524] Fix the type incompatibility problem of VMS code under x86_64 cross-compiler. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #16497; commits: 9150ca6)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0532] When SM2 uses an invalid digest, it will now correctly report an error to avoid silent failure. (Architecture-related: External behavior: SM2 signature) (PRs: #16833; commits: d5d95da)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0537] Fix DER encoder output structure for EC and SM2, removing incorrect public key serialization logic. (Architecture-related: DER encoding behavior) (PRs: #16977, #16983; commits: 2d49519)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0556] Change the OSSL_provider_init function declaration from extern to OPENSSL_EXPORT to ensure correct export on Windows systems. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #17203, #17215; commits: d977a26)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0573] Fixed scoping of extern "C" blocks in C++ wrappers to ensure function declarations are wrapped correctly. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #17557, #17562; commits: 1bfd20f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0584] The EC_GROUP_new_from_params function in the FIPS module no longer supports explicit elliptic curve parameters, only accepts named curves, and returns the new error code EC_R_EXPLICIT_PARAMS_NOT_SUPPORTED. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #17978, #17998; commits: 5313746, 638c3a2)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0591] Fix the processing logic of URI in the SSL_CERT_DIR environment variable, and add the SSL_CERT_URI environment variable as the default certificate URI source. (Architecture-related: certificate loading behavior) (PRs: #18068, #18070; commits: 021859b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0595] Fix undefined behavior of CMAC, GMAC, HMAC and SipHash when EVP_MAC is reinitialized, ensure correct reinitialization, and prohibit Poly1305 from reusing keys. (Architecture-related: external behavior) (PRs: #17811, #18100; commits: c9ddc5a)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0596] Introduce a reservation mechanism for OSSL_METHOD_STORE, and solve the race conditions in multi-threaded concurrent construction methods by adding independent large locks. (Architecture-related: internal architecture) (PRs: #18152, #18153; commits: e1eafe8)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0599] Fix the locale initialization problem and ensure that the string comparison function works correctly; remove the dependence on the locale code and directly implement the strcasecmp and strncasecmp functions. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #18172, #18282, #18322, #18344; commits: 92d0501, f505be9, fb4cdca)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0606] Fixed default key length regression for Blowfish CFB and OFB ciphers, corrected from 64 bits to 128 bits. (Architecture-related: Blowfish default key length) (PRs: #18359, #18362; commits: 7a9e93d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0616] Fix DSA, DH, EC and RSA key export routines to ensure success is not incorrectly returned when parameter assignment fails. (Architecture-related: Key Management Module) (PRs: #18483, #18507; commits: 46c1c2d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0618] Fixed the build failure caused by the lack of SIZE_MAX macro definition in kdf_exch.c, and added the necessary header file inclusion. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #18605; commits: c6010d1)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0623] Adjust the random number seed generation method for the OpenVMS platform, using timestamp plus sequence number instead of high-precision time function. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #18727, #18731; commits: 7056dc9)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0625] Fix padding issue with Keccak implementation on s390x platform, use intermediate message digest calculation instead and handle Keccak padding manually. (Architecture-related: Platform compatibility) (PRs: #18794; commits: 086d88a)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0642] Fixed the byte order problem in the AES-GCM-SIV implementation, replaced the byte exchange function and corrected the mulx_ghash processing logic under the big-endian architecture. (Architecture-related: Platform compatibility: Big-endian architecture) (PRs: #18911, #18920; commits: 6f74677)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0648] Limit the maximum block size of symmetric ciphers to 2^30 bytes, and fix possible crashes and infinite loops caused by differences in sizeof(long) and sizeof(size_t). (Architecture-related: public API behavior restrictions) (PRs: #18997; commits: 709d4be)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0661] Fixed an issue on s390x where IKM was not used correctly when generating X25519 and (PRs: #19278; commits: d12b824)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0665] Make CRYPTO_secure_malloc() correctly trigger the ERR_R_MALLOC_FAILURE error when memory allocation fails. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #19301; commits: 9167a47)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0666] Stopped reporting ERR_R_MALLOC_FAILURE directly in most places, instead reported more specific subsystem error codes, and fixed related custom malloc wrapper issues. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #19301; commits: e077455)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0667] Fixed propagation of error return values in BN_check_prime(), ensuring that internal functions correctly return -1 when returning an error, allowing callbacks to abort primality testing or key generation. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #19314; commits: 0b38676)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0671] Fixed an issue where the CMS_decrypt_set1_* series of functions incorrectly added misleading error queue entries in the case of non-receiver mismatch. (Architecture-related: public API: CMS_decrypt_set1_*) (PRs: #19222; commits: 60ea150)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0672] Add a check for memory allocation failure in the SRP_VBASE_init function to catch potential memory errors in time. (Architecture-related: public API: SRP_VBASE_init) (PRs: #19435; commits: f44d32f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0674] Fixed the problem of make update failure due to error code number conflict, and adjusted the error code number of SSL_R_SEQUENCE_CTR_WRAPPED. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #19457; commits: 3c153d8)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0675] Fix SSL_alloc_buffers and SSL_free_buffers functions to make them work properly again. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #19472; commits: 7eb39ec)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0677] Check whether the private key exists before calling the eddsa signature function, and add relevant test cases to cover scenarios such as missing private keys, short keys, etc. (Architecture-related: external behavior) (PRs: #19524, #19525; commits: f5a10d5)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0678] Fixed an issue where selection parameters were not propagated correctly when exporting keys, ensured that private keys would not be attempted to be exported when only the public key was required, and updated the caching mechanism to avoid misuse of incomplete information in subsequent operations. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #19648; commits: 98642df)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0682] Fixed the problem of obtaining the PSS salt length from the provider, resolved the inconsistency between the provider's interpretation of magic constants and the OpenSSL core, and added related tests. (Architecture-related: provider and core compatibility) (PRs: #19724; commits: 5a3bbe1)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0692] Fixed the issue where EVP_KDF_CTX_get_kdf_size() incorrectly returns 0 when SSKDF is used in combination with KMAC, and now returns SIZE_MAX correctly. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #19934, #19935; commits: e8add4d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0694] Fixed an issue when implementing Keccak XOF via CPACF on the s390x platform, simulating XOF behavior by using empty input blocks for single block operations. (Architecture-related: Platform compatibility) (PRs: #19983; commits: 76aa4f3)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0698] The creation of DSA and DH keys with missing parameters is prohibited, and parameters must be provided when importing the key; the decoder no longer creates DSA keys with missing parameters. (Architecture-related: external behavior) (commits: 604247b, 9ac82e2)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0700] Fixed the Ed448 pre-hash function on the S390X platform, falling back to a non-accelerated implementation when performing pre-hashing. (Architecture-related: Platform compatibility) (PRs: #20067; commits: f225fbf)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0701] Remark X25519 and X448 as FIPS-approved algorithms, restoring their use in FIPS mode. (Architecture-related: FIPS Compliance) (PRs: #20079; commits: 8948b57)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0704] The IV length of ChaCha20-Poly1305 must now be 12 bytes, truncation is no longer supported, and passing in other lengths will return an error. (Architecture-related: public API: IV length constraints) (PRs: #20084, #20151; commits: a011523)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0726] Fixed the issue where when setting the key length and IV length in EVP_CIPHER_CTX_ctrl, if the value is the same as the current one, the call to the underlying provider will be skipped. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #20625, #20664; commits: eb52450)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0733] Sort the stack before multiple lookup operations to ensure correctness, involving X509 storage, EVP_PBE_find_ex, provider lookup, X509_policy_check, X509V3_EXT_get_nid and X509_TRUST_get_by_id. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #20782, #20842; commits: 07f9c81, 3ef5b60, efe0222, fb7a7f0)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0741] Fix the parameter verification error in the EVP_MD_CTX_get_params function, and correct the incorrectly called function pointer to the correct get_ctx_params. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #21022; commits: b501df3)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0743] In PBKDF2 key derivation and PKCS12 MAC generation, ignore EVP_MD_fetch fetch errors when using legacy algorithms to avoid error stack contamination. (Architecture-related: external behavior) (PRs: #21035; commits: dc4ccc7)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0747] Fixed the problem that the decoder did not correctly pass the property query parameters (propq), and added a settable context parameter interface for all decoders to ensure that internal functions can use the incoming property query parameters. (Architecture-related: decoder interface) (PRs: #21198, #21219; commits: 39ed763)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0752] Disable the spliced AES-GCM implementation on the PPC32 architecture because it is not available. (Architecture-related: Platform compatibility) (PRs: #21301, #21312; commits: 44957a4)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0756] Fixed the problem of data loss caused by selecting value 0 when exporting/importing keys after decoding. All selection flags will now be used. (Architecture-related: External behavior: Key export) (PRs: #21493, #21519; commits: 2acb0d3)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0769] Fixed the memory leak problem of OSSL_DECODER_CTX_new_for_pkey() function in the wrong path. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #21668, #21723; commits: 3d254b3)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0771] Avoid sending CONNECTION_CLOSE frames repeatedly and clear the to-be-sent flag after sending. (Architecture-related: QUIC protocol behavior) (PRs: #21798; commits: 0b31072)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0773] Fixed the problem of failing to return SSL_ERROR_ZERO_RETURN correctly when FIN has been received but the buffer is empty in QUIC reading, to avoid SSL_read blocking for too long. (Architecture-related: public API: SSL_read behavior fix) (PRs: #21780; commits: 72622c0)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0774] Adjusted the error handling when creating a QUIC stream, updated the error code value of the stream count limit, and changed the related error reporting from non-IO errors to abnormal errors. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #21815; commits: 96fe5e5)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0775] Fixed the QUIC stream backpressure condition, changed the related error type from abnormal error to non-I/O error, and updated the corresponding error code; added QUIC_RAISE_NON_IO_ERROR error macro. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #21700, #21811; commits: 7a5f58b, 8ee3ee1)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0776] Fixed a merge error caused by function renaming in the quic-multi-stream.c example, updated SSL_set_initial_peer_addr() to SSL_set1_initial_peer_addr(). (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #21842; commits: dac42bd)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0778] Implemented the dupctx method for chacha20 ciphers, supports the EVP_PKEY_CTX_copy function, and correctly handles deep copies of tlsmac pointers to avoid double frees. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #20978, #21878, #21887, #21933; commits: 61cfc22, df93b3c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0780] SSL_clear in QUIC is no longer a no-op, instead it returns an error and reports that it is not supported. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #21905; commits: b139f7a)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0782] Fix the seed source usage problem in the FIPS module to ensure that the seed source requested by the user is used instead of the default value. (Architecture-related: FIPS seed source behavior) (PRs: #21909, #21964; commits: 4cde758)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0785] Remove C11 _Static_assert related macro definitions and their usage to solve compatibility issues on some platforms. (Architecture-related: Platform compatibility) (PRs: #22017, #22091; commits: fc785a5)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0788] Fixed a memory leak issue caused when FIPS providers are used in different threads, ensuring that the main thread is registered to receive thread stop notifications. (Architecture-related: FIPS provider thread safety) (PRs: #21964; commits: be203ea)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0789] Fixed an issue with inconsistent printing behavior of EVP_PKEY_print_private in OpenSSL 3.0, now only prints what the backend considers the private key component, and adjusted the output logic of the DSA and ECX encoders to match expected behavior. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #22233, #22237; commits: 1296c2e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0799] Fixed an issue where encryption operations were not returning errors correctly after the key was not set or the key length was changed. (Architecture-related: Encryption behavior) (PRs: #22590; commits: e7cb211)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1522] Update the reference counting operation in the store module to a structure-based atomic operation. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #21260; commits: 2a1f467)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2039] Optimize AES-GCM performance for p9+ ppc64le platform, introduce splicing method and add related macro definitions and function declarations. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #16854; commits: 44a563d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2040] Using SM4 hardware instructions on the ARM processor to implement SM4 algorithm optimization, the performance is improved by about 8 to 40 times. (Architecture-related: Platform compatibility: ARM SM4 hardware acceleration) (PRs: #17455; commits: 15b7175)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2045] Adds an optimized implementation of SM4 based on ASIMD instructions for the ARM platform (N1/V1 microarchitecture), which can improve performance in parallel modes such as ECB, CTR, GCM and CBC decryption, and adds VPSM4-related key settings and encryption and decryption functions. (Architecture-related: Platform compatibility: ARM ASIMD SM4 acceleration) (PRs: #17951; commits: 4908787)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2052] Optimize the KMAC algorithm on the s390x architecture, by manually filling the last data block and using the kimd instruction to achieve hardware acceleration, improving performance by 2 to 3 times; at the same time, using the klmd instruction to optimize keccak XOF processing, accelerating multi-result block scenarios. (Architecture-related: Platform compatibility: s390x) (PRs: #18863, #20431; commits: affc070, de13699)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2058] When the hardware-accelerated SHA3 absorption function is enabled on an Apple CPU compatible with ARMv8.2, the performance of large block data processing is improved by about 36%. (Architecture-related: ARMv8.2 hardware acceleration) (PRs: #21380, #21398; commits: f6484de)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2135] Introduced new option RSA_PSS_SALTLEN_AUTO_DIGEST_MAX for RSA-PSS signature and set as default value to comply with FIPS 186-4. (Architecture-related: public API) (PRs: #19724; commits: 6c73ca4)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2137] According to the release of FIPS 186-5, update the approval status of EdDSA (Ed25519 and Ed448) in FIPS mode: first marked as unapproved, then remarked as approved. (Architecture-related: FIPS compliance) (PRs: #20020, #20219, #20343; commits: 09627a8, 8c02b98, 9fa5532)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2139] Rolled back the change that marked EdDSA as a FIPS-approved algorithm, re-marking it as unapproved in the FIPS provider. (Architecture-related: FIPS algorithm status changes) (PRs: #20343; commits: 759ab59)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2140] Limit the digest algorithms that can be used by HMAC and Hash DRBG in FIPS mode, allowing only SHA1, SHA2-256, SHA2-512, SHA3-256, SHA3-512. (Architecture-related: FIPS digest algorithm restrictions) (PRs: #20521, #21386; commits: 7a3d32a, f553c0f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2148] When changing the IV length, the previously set IV is invalidated, which enhances the security of the password implementation. (Architecture-related: external behavior) (PRs: #22590; commits: 6941eb4)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2213] Updated FIPS build instructions to provide guidance on how to build a compliant FIPS provider and use it with the latest version. (Architecture-related: FIPS build instructions) (PRs: #20907; commits: 2b42290)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2326] Repair VMS installation, add check for the existence of built-in provider in IVP script. (Architecture-related: VMS installation verification repair) (PRs: #16842; commits: 0c5307e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2351] Fix build issues in Cygwin environment by conditionally including the Windows.h header file. (Architecture-related: Platform compatibility: Cygwin build fixes) (PRs: #19531, #21006; commits: 9b9c42d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2353] Added continuous integration support to the Cygwin environment, and corrected the reference format of Windows header files. (Architecture-related: Platform compatibility: Cygwin CI support) (PRs: #21006; commits: e3b01eb)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2363] Fixed the problem of SHA3 build failure when there is no assembly mode on the ARM64 platform. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #22089, #22090; commits: 46b43c9)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2455] Fixed extra semicolons in ossl_rsa_pss_get_param_unverified, dh_validate_private and dh_validate_public, and used DH_check_pub_key_ex instead of DH_check_pub_key in dh_validate_public. (Architecture-related: public API changes) (PRs: #17525, #17528; commits: d715dbd)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2458] Prioritize the use of the GNU library initialization mechanism and advance the definition of __attribute__((constructor)) and __attribute__((destructor)) to improve platform compatibility. (Architecture-related: platform compatibility) (PRs: #18147; commits: 2dc3a4a)

## Routine Changes

### Architecture Changes / Changed Modules

- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0063] Move the calculation logic of the record layer overhead to the inside of the record layer and clean up the related code. (PRs: #19424; commits: 4f428e8)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0064] Move recordmethod.h to an internal header file and update the reference paths of related files. (PRs: #19748; commits: ca20f61)

### Bug Fixes

- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0822] Fixed an issue where spurious errors could be generated when loading private keys, avoided by properly managing the error stack. (PRs: #15283; commits: da198ad)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0823] Fixed an issue where fingerprint calculation failure was incorrectly regarded as an invalid certificate, and updated test cases. (PRs: #16043; commits: 2c05607)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0824] Fix the description and diagnostic information of -key, -in and other options in openssl req and openssl x509 commands, and add clearer warning prompts. (PRs: #16440; commits: 611ef4f, cc0d1b0)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0825] Fix the problem when generating AKID through v2i_AUTHORITY_KEYID(), and correctly handle the AKID suppression logic in the self-signed certificate scenario. (PRs: #16300, #16442; commits: 9bf1061)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0826] Improve the diagnostic information of the OpenSSL command line tool for missing/redundant parameters and unknown passwords or digests, making error reporting clearer and more accurate. (PRs: #16450; commits: d9f0735)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0827] Improve the file access error prompts of load_key_certs_crls related functions and remove the redundant load_key_certs_crls_suppress function. (PRs: #16452; commits: 6e24994)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0828] Moved the decoder input structure match check to the processing stage, ensuring that it is only skipped if the first decoder in the chain that specifies an input structure does not match the user-specified structure. (PRs: #16466; commits: 73dd5d6)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0829] Explicitly specify data structure names for certificate and CRL objects in the PEM to DER decoder, and add support for SM2 parameters. (PRs: #16224, #16466; commits: 9840885)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0830] In the file: scheme of OSSL_STORE, set the decoder context input structure name based on the type of certificate or CRL the user expects to load, avoiding password prompts for unrelated password-protected objects. (PRs: #16224, #16466; commits: 821b395)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0831] Fixed resource cleanup issue when BIO creation fails. (PRs: #16316; commits: f11c01a)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0832] Fixed the double free problem in EVP_PKEY_CTX_dup() caused by not setting the failed release pointer to empty. (PRs: #16495; commits: 85407b7)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0833] Fix reference counting error when copying DH key exchange context. (PRs: #16495; commits: 21a0d9f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0834] Improve the boundary check of the parameter num_ in the bio_write function and correctly handle negative input. (PRs: #16503; commits: a04b065)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0835] Fix the nc_email function and add checking for null bytes in the middle of ASN1 strings. (PRs: #16524; commits: 485d079)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0836] Fix the parameter comparison logic in the dh_cmp_parameters function so that it correctly compares the parameters of the two keys. (PRs: #16562, #16568; commits: cf1a231)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0837] Fix a double release issue that may occur when obtaining PRNG seeds fails. (PRs: #16631, #16636; commits: 52dcc01)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0838] Fixed an issue where pointers may be released incorrectly when DRBG instantiation fails. (PRs: #16631, #16636, #16640; commits: caf569a)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0839] Fixed an issue with incomplete return value checking of EVP_PKEY_fromdata_init and EVP_PKEY_fromdata to ensure correct handling of negative value error returns. (PRs: #16783, #16801; commits: 5e199c3, 884400d, d11cab4)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0840] Fixed BN_CTX leak issue and added missing memory allocation failure check. (PRs: #16788, #16804; commits: 9224221)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0841] Fixed a possible BIO resource leak in cmp_vfy.c and encoder_lib.c. (PRs: #16787, #16804; commits: 374d5cf)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0842] Fixed a memory leak caused by unreleased path strings during configuration loading. (PRs: #16813; commits: 74b4858)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0843] Fixed a possible memory leak when handling includedir fields in configuration files. (PRs: #16813; commits: 19b30f1)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0844] Fix the handling of ENGINE references in the pkey_set_type function to ensure correct reference counting. (PRs: #16757, #16846; commits: f7d6868)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0845] Fixed a lock leak in evp_keymgmt_util_export_to_provider that caused the lock to not be released when caching failed. (PRs: #16847, #16849; commits: fb0f65f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0846] Change the -reqexts option to an alias of -extensions to unify extension configuration processing. (PRs: #16865; commits: 251e941)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0847] Fixed the problem of repeatedly copying and embedding EVP_PKEY in X509_dup. (PRs: #16606, #16648; commits: bf585c9, e0c5184)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0848] Fix the double release problem of memory BIO in OCSP_sendreq_bio. (PRs: #16886; commits: f99b349)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0849] Added checks on the return values of BN_new, sk_RSA_PRIME_INFO_new_reserve and other functions to prevent memory errors caused by allocation failures. (PRs: #16892; commits: 9dddcd9)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0850] Fixed an issue that would cause a crash if the public key value was empty when encoding the EC public key, and instead returned an error. (PRs: #16911; commits: 6187d9e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0851] Fixed a memory leak causing the Post-Handshake Auth digest to not be released when saving the handshake digest failed. (PRs: #16917; commits: 963eb12)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0852] Fixed a segfault caused by ctx->p2 being a null pointer when calling fix_dh_rfc5114. (PRs: #16913; commits: 09d9126)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0853] Disabled cipher suites are no longer included when setting up TLSv1.3 cipher suites. (PRs: #16925; commits: 6cb814d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0854] Fixed a memory leak in the SSL module, including the ssl_create_cipher_list and tls_parse_stoc_key_share functions. (PRs: #16954, #16956; commits: 3a069c1, b3c3440)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0855] Removed the isinited variable in child_prov_globals and always acquired the lock; also fixed resource release and function call parameters during child provider creation. (PRs: #16980; commits: 464c2b9)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0856] Fixed an issue where deactivation could not be performed correctly when the provider was not associated with a store, ensuring that the cleanup operation could be completed even if the store was empty. (PRs: #16980; commits: 1e8ed3e, e39bd62)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0857] Actively unregister the sub-callback before releasing the sub-libctx to avoid receiving callbacks when libctx is half-released. (PRs: #16980; commits: cad2220)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0858] Change the read lock to a write lock in ossl_provider_find to correctly synchronize stack sorting operations that may occur under multi-threading. (PRs: #16980; commits: 4aced11)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0859] Fixed the problem of concurrency count confusion caused by not holding flag_lock when calling the sub-provider callback, and adjusted the lock release timing to ensure correctness. (PRs: #16980; commits: addbd7c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0860] Fixed do_X509_REQ_verify and do_X509_verify functions to correctly return -1 when initialization fails, and added a new cert_matches_key function to replace X509_check_private_key to optimize key matching logic. (PRs: #17001; commits: bc42cf5)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0861] Adjust the public key of subject_cert to be used first when calculating SKID, and only use the public key of subject_req when the certificate is empty to clarify parameter semantics. (PRs: #17145; commits: 15ac84e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0862] Fix OBJ_obj2txt return value check, use TEST_int_gt in tests instead to correctly validate return values. (PRs: #17005; commits: 2349d7b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0863] Added missing return value null pointer checks in multiple functions to fix potential null pointer dereference issues. (PRs: #17007; commits: ed5b26c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0864] Reset rwstate to SSL_NOTHING before calling ASYNC_start_job to fix the problem that the status is not reset correctly when an asynchronous job resumes after being paused. (PRs: #16809, #17013; commits: 07f620e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0865] Fix incomplete EVP_Cipher return value check to ensure all error conditions are handled correctly in CMAC_Final. (PRs: #17027, #21102; commits: 6d77473, dc19f2f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0866] Fix asn1_item_embed_d2i return value checking logic to ensure correct identification of error conditions. (PRs: #17028; commits: 7f608e4)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0867] Fixed return value checking of random byte generation functions to ensure correct handling of error conditions. (PRs: #17028; commits: a8f4cdd)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0868] Fix the return value check of the EVP_PKEY_paramgen_init function to ensure that initialization failure is correctly detected. (PRs: #17030; commits: 6e0b05f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0869] Fixed the return value check of EVP_PKEY_keygen_init to correctly handle error conditions that may return negative values. (PRs: #17031; commits: bf4ceed)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0870] Fix the RAND_bytes_ex function return value check and change the incorrect logical NOT judgment to the correct <=0 judgment. (PRs: #17032; commits: c9007bd)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0871] Fix the return value check of SSL_export_keying_material and change the logical negation judgment to compare with zero to ensure correct detection of failure conditions. (PRs: #17028; commits: 40649e3)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0872] Fixed return value check of BIO_read_filename, changed !BIO_read_filename to <= 0 to ensure correct detection of errors. (PRs: #17033; commits: e3f0362)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0873] Fixed an issue where SSL BIO did not support the BIO_gets method causing HTTP clients to be unable to load non-ASN.1 content under TLS, by falling back to the BIO_get_line function as an alternative. (PRs: #17317; commits: 606c79e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0874] By introducing dynamic engine identifiers and dedicated linked lists, we prevent the same dynamic engine from being loaded repeatedly and repair the memory corruption problem caused by repeated loading. (PRs: #17023, #17073; commits: e2571e0)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0875] Fixed the problem of PSS parameter setting sequence in RSA signature verification initialization. By default, unrestricted PSS parameters are used until the key setting is completed. (PRs: #17075, #17080; commits: eaae5d6)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0876] Fix logical dead code reported by Coverity, adjust exponent bit calculation in RSAZ_mod_exp_x2_ifma256 function and add assertion to ensure rem is not zero. (PRs: #17123; commits: 23effeb)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0877] Fixed multiple issues where the return value of ossl_bio_new_from_core_bio() was not checked, and added the msblob2key_does_selection function. (PRs: #17154; commits: 352a0bc)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0878] Fix diagnostic output and return value of parse_http_line1 in error cases, add retry timeout check function may_still_retry, and add tracking of text content type in OSSL_HTTP_REQ_CTX_nbio. (PRs: #17171; commits: e2b7dc3)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0879] Check the return value of BN_dup() to avoid using a null pointer when memory allocation fails. (PRs: #17168; commits: 9d1a270)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0880] Add checks on the return value of BIO_new() in cmp_vfy.c and t_x509.c, log an error and return when memory allocation fails; and remove redundant ERR_raise calls. (PRs: #17175; commits: 318e979, ecf60b9)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0881] The signature algorithm type acquisition function has been fixed so that it returns the algorithm name instead of a null pointer for unknown algorithms; the certificate release function has been revised to use a new stack release interface and the code style has been unified. (PRs: #17176; commits: 5fae09f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0882] Fixed an issue in the PVK encoder where the passphrase callback was not initialized correctly, refactored the password acquisition logic, and improved error handling and memory cleanup. (PRs: #17054, #17181; commits: baa88d9)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0883] Fixed an issue where caller keys were incorrectly released in the wrong path when setting up temporary DH keys. (PRs: #17196, #17209; commits: e819b57)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0884] Fixed the error handling of network-related options (such as -server) in CMP tools under socketless compilation, and made multiple code cleanups and feature enhancements; optimized the documentation description of multiple options in the openssl-cmp command; added a description of the criticality of server authentication when receiving trust anchor certificates in the CMP application and API documentation. (PRs: #17226, #21086, #21129; commits: 168d93a, 1a9e286, 83b424c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0885] Fix -rspin option so that it works properly when -reqin is not also used. (PRs: #17222; commits: 7ee0954)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0886] Fixed the mutual exclusion logic of -server, -use_mock_srv, -port and -rspin options and improved related documentation. (PRs: #17254; commits: a56bb5d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0887] Fix s_server not properly skipping the GET / prefix when returning file paths in -WWW mode. (PRs: #17231; commits: 2e3b829)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0888] Fixed an issue where s_client was not sending SNI data correctly when using the -proxy option. (PRs: #17232, #17248; commits: ea24196)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0889] Fix the error handling in the shacrypt function, change the direct return to jump to the error cleanup path, and ensure that allocated resources are released on failure. (PRs: #17404; commits: ea4d16b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0890] Fixed an issue where resources allocated via CRYPTO_malloc were not properly released under certain wrong paths. (PRs: #17412; commits: 1b87116)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0891] Fixed multiple issues with incomplete EVP_PKEY_CTX_ctrl return value checking, changing the condition from <0 to <=0 to properly capture all error conditions. (PRs: #17413; commits: 7b1264b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0892] Fixed the problem that the EVP_MD_CTX resource was not released correctly in the tls1_mac function, ensuring that the resource can also be released on the wrong path. (PRs: #17415; commits: 949e4f7)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0893] Fixed resource leak issues in multiple core modules, and optimized string comparison methods. (PRs: #17417; commits: 10481d3)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0894] Fixed indentation issue in providers/implementations/digests/sha3_prov.c and providers/implementations/kdfs/pbkdf2.c. (PRs: #17529; commits: 2c9da41)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0895] Fixed the problem of Decoder, Encoder and Store loader failing due to internal assertion error when retrieving with query string, and added relevant test cases. (PRs: #17456, #17459; commits: cd1981a, f5e97b3)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0896] Fixed a regression in the -kdflen option in the pkeyutl command, and adjusted the parameter checking method and the buffer size for input data reading. (PRs: #17461; commits: b82fd89)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0897] Fixed the logic of setting pointers to NULL when clearing old MD data, ensuring that md_data is set to NULL only after the memory is released. (PRs: #17255, #17472, #17473; commits: 8086b26)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0898] Fixed a conditional error when counting unprocessed records in the kTLS RX offload path. (PRs: #17492; commits: d73a7a3)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0899] Fixed the evp_keymgmt_util_match function so that it correctly attempts a cross-export in the other direction after the first cross-export attempt fails. (PRs: #17482, #17487; commits: 37b8507)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0900] Fixed the null pointer dereference problem and added null value checks in the load_cert_certs and next_item functions. (PRs: #17488; commits: 8c870f6)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0901] Fixed an issue in s_server that caused 2^14 byte long records to not be handled correctly due to BIO_gets null termination, adjusted buffer allocation and read size to support full record length. (PRs: #15706, #17538; commits: 148b592)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0902] Fixed BIO_FLAGS macro definition conflict, fixed duplicate macro definitions and added comments in public header files to avoid future conflicts. (PRs: #17545, #17546; commits: e278f18)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0903] Added return value check for X509_STORE_lock to prevent dirty data when lock acquisition fails. (PRs: #17575; commits: 814999c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0904] Added checks on whether the global pointer is NULL in multiple BIO core operation functions to avoid null pointer dereference. (PRs: #17581; commits: 7f1cb46)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0905] Added a null pointer check for memory allocation failure in the UI data copy function, and returns 0 on failure. (PRs: #17582; commits: 3f6a12a)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0906] Fixed undefined behavior issue with hash function in lhash caused by 32-bit right shift of 32-bit value. (PRs: #17583, #17589; commits: 2ce0a3d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0907] Fixed the IV length of the DES EDE ECB implementation to be set to 0 to comply with the ECB mode specification of not using IVs. (PRs: #17587, #17591; commits: d450eb8)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0908] Fixed the issue where the ciphers command ignored the -propquery option to ensure that this option takes effect correctly. (PRs: #17595; commits: 4ed3817)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0909] The IV length is cached in the EVP encryption context to avoid expensive parameter lookups on each query and related bugs are fixed. (PRs: #17064, #17543; commits: b30b45b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0910] Use opt_int_arg() in the speed command to parse integer arguments to avoid hexadecimal input processing errors, and update the help information to explain the input format. (PRs: #17585; commits: 78212c6)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0911] Fixed issue with default_context_thread_local thread local data not being cleaned up correctly. (PRs: #17622; commits: 8e012cd)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0912] Output auxiliary messages to the standard error stream, fixed output destination for self-signed verification of certificate requests. (PRs: #17613, #17650; commits: 2a6994c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0913] Fixed unchecked memory allocation failure in openssl rehash, added check for OPENSSL_strdup return value and freed memory on failure. (PRs: #17676; commits: 79cda38)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0914] Add a null pointer check to the return value of rand_get_global to avoid potential null pointer dereferences and return an error if the fetch fails. (PRs: #17690; commits: 09dca55)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0915] Added missing null pointer checks for calls such as BIO_new to avoid null pointer dereference problems caused by returning NULL. (PRs: #17710; commits: ba0b60c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0916] During CMP initialization, if no protection certificate or old certificate is provided, the subject of the CSR is used as the default message sender. (PRs: #17723; commits: cd7ec0b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0917] Check the return value of CRYPTO_strdup() and fix the null pointer dereference problem caused by possible memory allocation failure. (PRs: #17741; commits: 37be6fe)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0918] In async_posix, memory allocation error reporting is now triggered when stack memory allocation fails. (PRs: #17762; commits: 83c48d9)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0919] Rename the function parameter name strlen to strlength to avoid conflict with C++ reserved words. (PRs: #17753, #17764; commits: 28e141c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0920] Fixed a null pointer dereference problem that could occur with BN_mod_exp2_mont when argument m is zero, and added regression tests. (PRs: #17648, #17783; commits: 43135a5)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0921] Fixed an issue in openssl s_server -WWW that caused the request to fail when trying to call SSL_sendfile when KTLS was not actually enabled, added a KTLS availability check and gave a warning if it was unavailable. (PRs: #17503, #17788; commits: aea68b0)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0922] In s_client and s_server, a check on the return value of BIO_new_file() is added, and an error message is output and exited when the file opening fails. (PRs: #17820; commits: 625b099)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0923] Fix the memory leak caused by not releasing the allocated stack when memory allocation fails in the append_ia5 function. (PRs: #17827, #17828; commits: 1753559)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0924] Fix the problem that the BIO_read return value in PEM_read_bio_ex is not checked, add failure handling and optimize the error path. (PRs: #17754; commits: 2823e2e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0925] Fixed the TCP protocol detection logic in the init_client() function, using BIO_ADDRINFO_protocol() to correctly obtain the protocol type to set the NODELAY option. (PRs: #17838; commits: 54b6755)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0926] Fixed the problem of invalid IV length cache in EVP encryption code to avoid cache invalidation due to excessive invalidation. (PRs: #17064, #17862; commits: b9a2f24)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0927] Fixed an issue where DH key exchange padding was not always enabled when using X9.42 KDF, padding is now forced to be enabled. (PRs: #17834, #17859; commits: 01b1877, 4413fe3)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0928] Fix the bug caused by wrong parameter type in the dup method of scrypt KDF provider, reconstruct the function signature and add correct parameter conversion. (PRs: #16519, #16521, #17873; commits: 1e7479e, e04c2c0)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0929] Added a new detection function in dynamic engine loading to avoid false errors caused by misjudgment of the OpenSSL 1.1.x engine. (PRs: #17901; commits: bd5c91c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0930] Fix uninitialized reads in SSL_shutdown and SSL_do_handshake. (PRs: #17893; commits: 09134f1)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0931] Fix the issue of inconsistent parameter declaration in Camellia decryption function. (PRs: #17911, #17912; commits: a12a71f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0932] Fix openssl check -rsa command to support checking of both RSA and RSA-PSS keys. (PRs: #17167, #17920; commits: 388d6f4)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0933] Fixed the problem that the -proxy and -starttls options in openssl s_client cannot be used at the same time, and the HTTP proxy connection processing is advanced before the STARTTLS protocol is selected. (PRs: #17925; commits: 802cacf)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0934] Fixed a null pointer dereference issue caused by unchecked when evp_pkey_get_legacy() returned NULL, and added null pointer checks in multiple Ed25519/Ed448 signature and verification functions. (PRs: #17967; commits: b9a86d5)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0935] Fixed a crash in ERR_load_strings() when no-err is configured, skipping the initialization and cleanup of the error string hash table through conditional compilation. (PRs: #17971, #17974; commits: 11e85b8)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0936] Fix the issue of incorrect error reporting in CMS I/O functions and add relevant test cases. (PRs: #17841, #18109; commits: 45a3c59)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0937] Fixed an issue where the client certificate chain check failed due to ca_dn being NULL when the server did not send the CA name under TLS-1.3. (PRs: #17986; commits: 89dd854)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0938] Fix a null pointer dereference issue that may occur when memory allocation fails in sshkdf. (PRs: #18009, #18011; commits: 148176c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0939] Fix the unchecked return value issue in Coverity reporting, explicitly ignoring the return value of EVP_CipherInit_ex. (PRs: #18013; commits: b11183b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0940] Fixed an issue where the return value of bn_rshift_fixed_top was not checked, correctly jumping to error handling when the function failed. (PRs: #18010, #18034; commits: bc6bac8)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0941] Allow setting the SM2 distributed ID parameter to an empty string. (PRs: #18022, #18052; commits: 2904d0a, 707d4e0)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0942] Fix the null pointer dereference problem in the create_cert_store function due to X509_STORE_new possibly returning NULL, add a null value check and return an error in case of failure. (PRs: #18057; commits: 3f07596)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0943] Fix the null pointer dereference problem that may be caused by EVP_KDF_fetch returning a null pointer in the PBKDF2 implementation, and add return value checking. (PRs: #18062; commits: 5f1424c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0944] Fix the null pointer dereference problem that may be caused by not checking the return value of EVP_PKEY_copy_parameters in the ssl_set_cert_and_key function. (PRs: #18071; commits: 6646e01)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0945] Fix the assertion failure problem in the DTLS server caused by the MTU exceeding the maximum fragment length, and add regression testing. (PRs: #18093; commits: e915c3f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0946] Fix the wild pointer dereference problem that may be caused by not checking the return value of OCSP_basic_add1_status in the make_ocsp_response function. (PRs: #18081; commits: 4d50a54)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0947] Fixed a MAC key usage issue caused by a copy-paste error in the RC4-MD5 cipher suite that incorrectly used TLS AAD data as the MAC key. (PRs: #18112; commits: 3321993)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0948] Delete the misused function ossl_provider_clear_all_operation_bits and all its calls, and fix the problem of operation bits being cleared incorrectly when the method cache is refreshed. (PRs: #18150, #18151; commits: 20b6d85)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0949] Fix bug in OPENSSL_LH_flush function where num_items counter was not reset. (PRs: #18139, #18141; commits: e5da681)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0950] Fixed a possible error when calling finalization in an uninitialized SipHash context, failure is now correctly returned. (PRs: #18140, #18142; commits: 650b142)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0951] Fix the problem of incorrectly setting the mac size of sipcopy when siphash copies, and ensure the complete copy context. (PRs: #18142; commits: 905fec4)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0952] Fixed the problem of incomplete copying of the entire structure during poly1305 context copy, and reset the update flag during initialization. (PRs: #18143; commits: bbe909d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0953] Fixed an issue where method storage was incorrectly cleared when refreshing the query cache, renamed related functions and adjusted internal fields to clarify their purpose. (PRs: #18150, #18151; commits: 60640d7)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0954] Fix issue where locale is not initialized before calling evp_pkey_name2type. (PRs: #18158, #18199; commits: e560655)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0955] Distinguish between fatal errors and non-fatal errors when creating a record layer, so that non-fatal errors can be tried to be handled by other record layers. (PRs: #18132; commits: 7c29399)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0956] Add cache flushing and removal functionality to method stores for encoders, decoders and storage loaders to ensure proper cleanup when providers are deactivated or modified. (PRs: #18151; commits: 32e3c07)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0957] Fixed an issue where optional properties could be incorrectly ignored when a new provider was added, constructors are now always tried. (PRs: #18262, #18269; commits: 4b1b629)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0958] Fixed the compilation failure caused by disabling the compression function, and added conditional compilation protection to the relevant code. (PRs: #18132; commits: 976b263)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0959] Fix the problem of incorrectly passing digest when signing Ed25519/Ed448 in CRMF, and ensure that such keys do not use digest when signing POPO. (PRs: #18184, #18230; commits: de56f72)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0960] In s_server, when initializing the connection fails and no read is attempted, an error is now reported to distinguish configuration errors from errors caused by the client. (PRs: #18047, #18154; commits: a6d52f1)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0961] Fixed the crash problem of the fix_dh_paramgen_type function caused by invalid parameter types, added checking for invalid values and returned an error. (PRs: #18202; commits: 359dad5)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0962] Adjust the order of header file inclusion and place internal/e_os.h before string.h to fix compilation problems. (PRs: #18244, #18282; commits: cf91a2b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0963] Fixed the problem of clang-14 compiler incorrectly optimizing code due to strict alias rules, and changed related functions to inline macros to avoid accidental removal of code. (PRs: #18225, #18258; commits: 8712db5)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0964] Adjust loop conditions to make the risk of zero-length loops more clear and fix related issues. (PRs: #18073, #18327; commits: 36c269c)
