- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0965] Fixed the issue where the -CAfile option was ignored in x509 applications. Now -CAfile can take effect correctly when using the -new or -in option. (PRs: #18373; commits: 10c7887)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0966] Fixed a memory leak in the crl_set_issuers function that caused the temporary object not to be released when allocating or inserting the issuers stack failed. (PRs: #18391; commits: e9007e0)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0967] Fix the leak problem caused by incorrect memory release in the X509_issuer_and_serial_hash function. (PRs: #18371; commits: b7e28c0)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0968] Fix security issue with buffer sizing in GOST key exchange, use dynamic allocation of buffers instead to avoid overflow. (PRs: #18381; commits: 2b5e899)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0969] Fixed a crash in ssl_security_cert_chain caused by the certificate chain being a null pointer, and added a null pointer check. (PRs: #18376; commits: dc0ef29)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0970] Fixed the issue of incorrect EVP_CIPHER_CTX_rand_key return value check to ensure correct judgment of whether key generation is successful. (PRs: #18397; commits: f15e3f3)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0971] Fix UI_method_set_ex_data return value check bug, the function returns 0 or 1 instead of negative number, so change the condition from < 0 to logical negation. (PRs: #18397; commits: 1aef2c1)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0972] Fix the error in EVP_PKEY_CTX_set_group_name return value check and change the error condition from logical negation to less than or equal to zero. (PRs: #18399; commits: 56876ae)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0973] Fix the return value check of the X509_LOOKUP_* series of functions, and change the judgment of equal to 0 to less than or equal to 0 to correctly handle error conditions. (PRs: #18400; commits: e22ea36)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0974] Fix memory leak in decoder on wrong path, ensure decoder reference count is released correctly. (PRs: #18355, #18410, #18411; commits: 9ec9b96, da31939)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0975] Fix the return value handling of EVP_PKEY_check and EVP_PKEY_param_check so that it correctly handles the case where the return value is 0 or a negative value. (PRs: #18424; commits: 92d0d7e, e85bef9)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0976] Fix memory leaks in EVP_PKEY related functions, including correctly releasing pkey when evp_pkey_copy_downgraded() fails, and fixing the problem that tmp_keymgmt is not released when keymgmt is empty in evp_pkey_export_to_provider. (PRs: #18494, #18499; commits: 115eb94, d873280)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0977] Fix the UI_add_input_string return value checking logic to ensure correct judgment of its execution results. (PRs: #18424; commits: 5755c11)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0978] Fixed a memory leak in ossl_provider_doall_activated: when the callback fails, the provider that was previously pushed onto the stack and the reference count is incremented is now correctly released. (PRs: #18413; commits: b4be10d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0979] Fix the problem of calling the release function before the reference count is initialized when creating a provider. Make sure that ossl_provider_free is called after the reference count is correctly initialized. (PRs: #18355, #18417; commits: c4ed6f6)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0980] Fix memory leak in ossl_method_store_add() caused by ossl_prop_defn_set() failure. (PRs: #18355, #18458; commits: fed8dbe)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0981] Fixed an issue where CONF_modules_unload still tried to use the lock even if it failed to initialize the lock inside CONF_modules_finish. It will now check the initialization result and return early if it fails. (PRs: #18355, #18460; commits: 697d0b5)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0982] Fix an issue where the add_provider_groups function did not clean up the algorithm pointer on failure. (PRs: #18494; commits: a7863f9)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0983] Fix the use-after-free problem in error handling of hmac_dup function to ensure that the digest field is cleared when copying fails. (PRs: #17263, #18493, #18502; commits: 27f7f52, cec1699)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0984] Fix potential use-after-free issue with md in sm2_dupctx, clearing the md field of the target context after copying the context. (PRs: #18494; commits: 926c698)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0985] Fixed a bug where BN_mod_exp_mont_consttime on x86_64 could incorrectly return the modulus m instead of zero, as a final step by uniformly using the standard Montgomery reduction instead of the intrinsic function bn_from_montgomery. (PRs: #18510; commits: 0ae365e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0986] Add an additional reduction step to the RSAZ modular exponentiation implementation to ensure that the result is fully reduced to the modulus range. (PRs: #18510; commits: 6d702ce)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0987] Fallback of the fixed-length expansion Montgomery multiplication optimization for PPC architecture, as this implementation produces erroneous results. (PRs: #18512; commits: 712d9cc)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0988] Fixed a crash in X509v3_asid_subset() caused by the asnum or rdi field being a null pointer, and added corresponding test cases. (PRs: #18514; commits: 01fc9b6)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0989] Fixed the crash of v2i_IPAddrBlocks() caused by the IP address prefix length being too large, and added a legality check on the prefix length. (PRs: #18523; commits: b91ad3c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0990] Fixed the flaw in the range_should_be_prefix() function that incorrectly determined whether the IP address range should be represented as a prefix, and added corresponding test cases. (PRs: #18524; commits: 30532e5)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0991] Fixed the problem of insufficient adaptation of the input buffer length parameter when calling EVP_KDF_derive() in the kdf_derive() function, and added an error check for an output buffer that is too small. (PRs: #18517, #18533; commits: 0feb138)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0992] In order to avoid recursive locking, the lock used to protect initialization state variables is separated from init_lock and a new independent lock is added. (PRs: #18535, #18545; commits: e9a806b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0993] Fixed an issue where the c_rehash tool incorrectly retained the prefix when calculating the CRL hash, and will now correctly remove the prefix. (commits: 87eee75)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0994] Fix set_dateopt() function return value so that -dateopt option works properly. (PRs: #18553, #18554; commits: 55b7fa2)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0995] Add a check on the return value of OPENSSL_malloc() in ASN1_TIME_to_tm() to prevent the use of a null pointer when memory allocation fails. (PRs: #18575; commits: 8547cd6)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0996] Improve error diagnostics when setting up TLS groups: clear error messages and explicitly report invalid groups. (PRs: #18585, #18591; commits: ce8822b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0997] Fixed the problem of incorrectly handling negative return values in the dh_priv_encode function to prevent incorrect transmission. (PRs: #18587; commits: be54ad8)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0998] Fix the handling of negative return values when encoding DSA private keys, and add length checks to avoid errors. (PRs: #18587; commits: 3ee2611)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0999] Add a null pointer check for the return value of OPENSSL_strdup in the by_store_ctrl_ex function, and return an error immediately if the allocation fails. (PRs: #18593; commits: e163969)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1000] In s_server's UNIX socket option handling, add a null pointer check for the OPENSSL_strdup return value and jump to error handling on failure. (PRs: #18595; commits: a6a2dd9)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1001] Added validity checks for saltlen and trailerfield parameters to the RSA key writer to prevent invalid parameters from causing errors. (PRs: #18168, #18615; commits: 4832099)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1002] Fix memory leak in EC_GROUP_new_from_ecparameters where allocated BIGNUM objects were not properly released after ASN1_INTEGER_to_BN failed when parsing elliptic curve parameters. (PRs: #18633; commits: be50862)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1003] Fix the problem in rsaz_mod_exp_avx512_x2 that the parameter unit is wrong when calling bn_reduce_once_in_place, converting the modulo bit size to the number of words. (PRs: #18625, #18626; commits: 4d8a88c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1004] Fixed an issue where KDF objects were not returned correctly when memory allocation failed. (PRs: #18638; commits: 7260709)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1005] Fix lock contention and resource release logic in provider storage to avoid double unlocking and potential memory leaks. (PRs: #18641; commits: 61f5106)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1006] Fixed an issue where negative return values in the RSA key pair verification function were not correctly considered verification failures. (PRs: #18538, #18651; commits: 518f1ee)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1007] Fix memory leak in ossl_rsa_fromdata caused by collect_numbers memory allocation failure. (PRs: #18365, #18646; commits: 28adea9)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1008] Use OPENSSL_zalloc to allocate the EVP_PBE_CTL structure and fix the problem of uninitialized memory. (PRs: #18598, #18666; commits: 3211266)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1009] Fix code formatting issues in crypto/x509/v3_addr.c and adjust the order of IPAddressFamily length checks. (PRs: #18668; commits: 30d398a)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1010] Fix Content-Length calculation logic in HTTP client, including BIO_CTRL_INFO return value based on file BIO and calculation in set1_content(). (PRs: #18701, #18779; commits: 243465f, 8c65e1f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1011] Fixed a crash caused when use_ssl is not set but SSL_CTX is provided, and added parameter consistency check. (PRs: #18674; commits: 96e13a1)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1012] Fixed parameter errors caused by copy-paste errors in the OSSL_HTTP_REQ_CTX_nbio function, and corrected the retry check object from rbio to req. (PRs: #18674; commits: 059a4ad)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1013] Fix a possible crash caused by calling CONF_modules_unload() after calling OPENSSL_cleanup(). (PRs: #18669, #18673; commits: d840f07)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1014] Fix the problem that the return value of BN_one() call in bn_gcd is not checked to avoid potential errors when memory allocation fails. (PRs: #18355, #18697; commits: 7fe7cc5)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1015] Fix parameter passing error in SSL_set_srp_server_param_pw function, correct GN->N and GN->g to s->srp_ctx.N and s->srp_ctx.g. (PRs: #18699; commits: 12e4883)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1016] Fix the release order of HTTP TLS messages in CMP context, avoid crashes, and add null pointer protection. (PRs: #18702; commits: 8c09474)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1017] Fixed a memory leak in EVP_PKEY_get1_encoded_public_key caused by the failure of the second call to EVP_PKEY_get_octet_string_param. (PRs: #18355, #18739; commits: 4e9a499)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1018] Rollback core changes to chain_build() error reporting, no longer setting errors in check_issued. (PRs: #14094, #18691, #18758; commits: 1f00dc4)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1019] Fix segfault in SSL code caused by EVP_MD pointer being NULL. (PRs: #18355, #18784; commits: b740012)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1020] Fixed an issue where the keylength field was not copied when copying FFC parameters. (PRs: #18480; commits: 5f311b1)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1021] Fix the possible null pointer dereference problem in the ossl_provider_ctx function and add null value check. (PRs: #18799; commits: f809103)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1022] Fixed an issue where the verify_callback function in openssl s_client/s_server did not check whether the certificate was empty before printing the wrong certificate information. (PRs: #18805; commits: fad0f80)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1023] Fix memory leak in PKCS12_pbe_crypt_ex function, ensure out variable is released on wrong path. (PRs: #18689, #18808; commits: af801ec)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1024] Fix the null pointer dereference problem in the ossl_provider_ctx function, check whether prov is NULL before accessing prov->provctx. (PRs: #18822; commits: f913c3c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1025] Fixed the problem of null pointer dereference in the ossl_sa_free function, and added a null value check for input parameters. (PRs: #18837; commits: 93429fc)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1026] Fixed the null pointer dereference issue caused by incorrect function pointer setting in GCM operations on the s390x platform. (PRs: #18862; commits: 48e35b9)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1027] Fixed an issue where the record limit counter in GCM mode was incorrectly reset when the AAD changed, and is now only reset when the key changes. (PRs: #18860; commits: 3ebcb2f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1028] Fixed a potential double-free issue caused by not making the rrl object empty after releasing it. (PRs: #18132; commits: efc84ea)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1029] Fixed the crash caused by the public key not being set when obtaining the encoded public key parameters, and added an error report when the group was not set, and also corrected the data size parameter. (PRs: #18495, #18902; commits: b5db237)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1030] Fix the problem of no null check before dereference found by Coverity. (PRs: #18907; commits: 76ad9ae)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1031] Fix the resource leak caused by direct return in the do_ssl3_write function and jump to the error handling path instead. (PRs: #18924; commits: 771fef7)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1032] Fixed an issue where the X509_REQ_get_extensions function did not log an error when encountering a malformed extension attribute and now adds the corresponding entry to the error queue. (PRs: #18931; commits: e128eaa)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1033] Fixed an issue where the Ed25519 and Ed448 signature operations on the s390x platform did not set the signature length correctly and returned an error when the signature failed. (PRs: #18912, #18928; commits: bbedc05)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1034] Fix a crash that may occur when the actual parameter in the check_transactionID_or_nonce() function is NULL. (PRs: #18929; commits: aeadd29)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1035] Add test cases for the trace API, and add parameter validity checks in the function that obtains category names and numbers. (PRs: #17422, #19096; commits: fcff5bd)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1036] Fix the return values of CRYPTO_mem_debug_push() and CRYPTO_mem_debug_pop() so that they correctly return 0 instead of -1. (PRs: #18967; commits: f868454)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1037] Fixed a memory leak that may occur when passing in NULL parameters in BN_rand_range(), which will now return an error immediately. (PRs: #18951, #18982; commits: 70f589a)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1038] Fix an issue in TLSv1.3 where session tickets are not sent when the ticket key callback returns 0, and adjust the state machine to support constructor decisions not to send messages. (PRs: #18977, #18990; commits: 3e93c5f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1039] Fixed cookie and PSK age calculation errors caused by flipping the low 32 bits of time_t, extending the timestamp field to 64 bits to avoid overflow. (PRs: #19004; commits: e8a557d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1040] Fix the problem of incorrectly calling RLAYERfatal when the record layer pointer is NULL or is about to be released. Use ERR_raise to record errors instead. (PRs: #19027; commits: 7b7ad9e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1041] Fixed a possible null pointer dereference issue in the ssl3_cipher function when the encryption context is empty or no cipher is set. (PRs: #19027; commits: 35bcac1)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1042] Fixed a memory leak in TLS 1.2 compression functionality, properly freeing the SSL3_RECORD structure when releasing the record layer. (PRs: #19030; commits: 6b5c7ef)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1043] Fixed an out-of-bounds access issue due to type mismatch in DH key derivation. (PRs: #19033; commits: eb7a5cc)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1044] Fixed the issue where the speed tool did not wait for child processes in multi-process mode, resulting in incorrect statistics, and added child process waiting and status checks. (PRs: #19093; commits: 56233ba)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1045] Fixed a bug that caused the Initial key to be discarded prematurely during Handshake packet processing in QUIC. The Initial key is now only discarded after successfully decrypting and authenticating the Handshake packet. (PRs: #19176; commits: 45e7ef5)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1046] Fixed handling of status fields in CMP context, used mnemonic constants instead of magic numbers, and updated related documentation. (PRs: #19205; commits: 19ddcc4)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1047] Fixed the record layer memory leak, adjusted the release order to ensure that the record layer is cleaned before releasing the connection BIO. (PRs: #19198; commits: 9ff5195)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1048] Fixed handling logic when failInfo PKI status information is not set or missing. (PRs: #19205; commits: cba0e2a)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1049] Fixed logic errors and memory leaks in the CMS_decrypt* series of functions. (PRs: #19222; commits: 25dd780)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1050] Fixed an issue with checking for wrong ITAVs in the gen_new function. (PRs: #19216; commits: 7e30349)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1051] Added a non-null check for the return value of sk_SSL_CIPHER_value in the ciphers command to avoid potential null pointer dereference issues. (PRs: #19162, #19233; commits: 630d312)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1052] Always use binary format for input files (including standard input). (PRs: #19234; commits: 4689fe1)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1053] Enhanced the robustness of pipeline data parsing in speed.c, by adding safe conversion functions and repairing parsing logic to avoid potential parsing errors. (PRs: #19050, #19238; commits: 18af4d1)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1054] Fixed an issue in the duplicated function where parsing errors were not reported correctly, and simplified the function implementation. (PRs: #19271; commits: 66fc90f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1055] Fixed the resource leak problem in the pkcs12 command caused by not jumping out of the loop in time. (PRs: #19275; commits: 8bc703c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1056] Fixed the problem of missing null pointer check when strchr returns NULL to avoid undefined behavior caused by this. (PRs: #19279, #19282; commits: b85d53c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1057] Fixed an issue in the dgram_pair_read_inner function where the pointer was still moved when the buf pointer was NULL. (PRs: #19284; commits: 9643ddb)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1058] When an asynchronous encryption operation fails, the error code returned by the kernel is added to the OpenSSL error queue, along with detailed error information. (PRs: #19289; commits: bd19999)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1059] Fixed an issue where cipher specific cleanup functions were not called when cleaning EVP_CIPHER_CTX. (PRs: #19300; commits: f817a74)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1060] Fixed regression in i2d_re_X509_REQ_tbs(), removed redundant i2d_X509_REQ_INFO calls and returned their results directly. (PRs: #19297, #19299; commits: 928f15e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1061] Fixed possible divide-by-zero exception in division operations. (PRs: #19310; commits: 3189e12)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1062] Fixed a recursion problem that err_set_debug() could cause when malloc failed. (PRs: #19331, #19332; commits: ed49476)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1063] Fix infinite recursion issue that CRYPTO_THREAD_lock_new could cause when memory allocation fails, use CRYPTO_zalloc instead and avoid setting an error when allocation fails. (PRs: #19334, #19335; commits: 894f216)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1064] Fix MGF1 digest setting bug for RSA, ensure digest names and attributes are passed correctly when setting OAEP and MGF1 digests, and add support for implicitly reject parameters. (PRs: #19290, #19342; commits: e5a7536)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1065] Fix type overflow issues in QUIC congestion control and ACK management on 32-bit Windows, change size_t in related shift operations and delay calculations to uint64_t. (PRs: #19345; commits: 5506fbe)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1066] Fix the problem that the old version of EVP_PKEY_CTX object does not support the group parameters of X25519 and X448, and add parameter conversion processing to avoid error reports. (PRs: #19313, #19348; commits: c048779)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1067] Fixed the problem of returning an error code when receiving a record with only header and no data in DTLS scenario. (PRs: #19343; commits: f78c519)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1068] Fix for subtraction result not being explicitly converted to size_t type in QUIC stream code on 32-bit Windows. (PRs: #19345; commits: 44bc72a)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1069] Fix type conversion and length checking issues with QUIC line format decoding functions on 32-bit Windows. (PRs: #19345; commits: e251e7b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1070] Correct the return value of dtls_write_records, use the standard record layer return value instead, and migrate error handling from SSLfatal to RLAYERfatal. (PRs: #19424; commits: 4cdd198)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1071] Fix incorrect memory free in EC key encoding functions, ensure ASN1_STRING_free is only called on ASN1_STRING types, and remove unnecessary ASN1_OBJECT_free calls on OBJ_nid2obj return objects. (PRs: #19138, #19367; commits: 8b5424e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1072] Fix the total_timeout processing logic for RR and GENM transactions in the CMP client to ensure that timeout checks are correctly applied to all transaction types. (PRs: #19391; commits: d7d1d09)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1073] Fixed the problem of the resource release sequence of DRBG and engine in the global default context, releasing DRBG early before the engine is released. (PRs: #17995, #18578, #19386; commits: a88e97f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1074] Fix a possible memory leak in the CMS_decrypt_set1_password function to clear the previously set decryption key before setting a new password. (PRs: #19222; commits: 911045a)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1075] Move the sequence number increment operation when writing DTLS records from post-writing processing to post-encryption processing stage, and add corresponding processing functions. (PRs: #19424; commits: 421386e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1076] Refactor thread join and cleanup logic, introduce a universal synchronization wrapper to serialize concurrent join operations, fix race conditions, and remove unsupported force termination functionality. (PRs: #19433; commits: 4e43bc0)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1077] Delay the release operation of BIO to the final stage of connection cleanup, and ensure that all record layer objects are released before releasing BIO to avoid memory leaks. (PRs: #19424; commits: cd6e89b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1078] Fixed the regular expression error in file extension matching in the c_rehash tool to prevent non-certificate files from being mistakenly matched. (PRs: #19427; commits: 706fc5f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1079] Fix the tainted scalar problem discovered by Coverity, and add zero value checks for compressed length and uncompressed length when printing compressed certificates to avoid division by zero and null pointer risks. (PRs: #19440; commits: 5e569f0)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1080] Fix an issue reported by Coverity to check if the buffer is empty before cleaning up temporary packages to avoid uninitialized access. (PRs: #19440; commits: d06d5d6)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1081] Add a check on the return value of BIO_new_fp when creating a BIO output chain. If NULL is returned, NULL will be returned directly to avoid building an invalid BIO chain. (PRs: #19445; commits: fb03e61)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1082] Fixed an issue caused by reseeding after forking when using EGD random source, and adjusted related test conditions to avoid running fork security tests in EGD mode. (PRs: #19396, #19454; commits: 04d07ff)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1083] Fix asan error caused by insufficient input/output buffer array lifetime in pipeline mode, declare the array in advance to ensure it is still accessible during EVP_Cipher call. (PRs: #19456; commits: 3961af3)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1084] Re-enable TLS 1.3 encryption testing, and fix null pointer check in tls_free function. (PRs: #19470; commits: 50bed93)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1085] Fix the lock type error in provider_remove_store_methods and change the read lock to a write lock. (PRs: #19474, #19481; commits: 6962e21)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1086] Fix the calculation of the encryption growth limit, allowing stitched cipher suites to grow more when encrypting, and add assertions to verify that the reserve space is sufficient. (PRs: #19516; commits: 830eae6)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1087] Fix insufficient buffer size when reading is pipelined, and remove unnecessary early data movement logic. (PRs: #19456; commits: 8ccde3f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1088] Fix the dtls_get_max_record_overhead function so that it returns a more accurate value for the maximum record overhead and ignores compression. (PRs: #19516; commits: b05fbac)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1089] Fix the problem in the ssl3_dispatch_alert function that cannot send alerts when there is pending data writing, and correctly handle retry scenarios. (PRs: #19550; commits: 7324350)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1090] Fix memory leak caused by PEM_write_bio_PrivateKey_traditional not releasing the key copy on the wrong path. (PRs: #19536; commits: 608aca8)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1091] Fix the segmentation fault caused by EVP_PKEY being a null pointer when PEM writes the private key, and add corresponding test cases. (PRs: #19536; commits: 373d901)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1092] Unified the use of cryptographic growth macros and fixed the calculation of reserved bytes when MAC is added independently of the cryptographic algorithm, avoiding buffer overflows. (PRs: #19622; commits: ecacbc5)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1093] Limits the maximum length of the modulus in large number modulo operations to prevent exceptions caused by excessive allocation on the stack. (PRs: #20005; commits: 30667f5)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1094] Fixed null pointer dereference and resource leak issues in X.509 v3 address validation. (PRs: #19700; commits: 26cfa4c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1095] Allow passing NULL pointer when calling ossl_quic_rstream_free to avoid null pointer dereference. (PRs: #19703; commits: 2124779)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1096] Fixed handling of zero-length parameters in QUIC transport parameter encoding. (PRs: #19703; commits: 6946f11)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1097] Fixed the parsing width of the certificate compression algorithm field, changing it from single-byte to double-byte reading. (PRs: #19600; commits: ce74e3f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1098] In the dgst command, when the first argument is a built-in hash name, set it to the digest algorithm name. (PRs: #19589, #19606; commits: 1e5780d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1099] Fixed a memory leak when releasing the DTLS record layer, ensuring that old record layer objects are released correctly when the send message queue is empty. (PRs: #19586; commits: 20c7feb)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1100] Fixed double locking issue in X.509 policy map, removed redundant flag settings. (PRs: #19643, #19652; commits: 4d0340a)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1101] Fixed undefined behavior caused by left-shifting signed integers in init_info_strings, converting related types to unsigned longs. (PRs: #19668; commits: ee17dcc)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1102] Fixed error checking for ECDH and FFDH performance tests in apps/speed.c, changing return value comparison to less than or equal to zero to correctly handle error conditions. (PRs: #19678; commits: 9dd009d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1103] Fixed the checking logic of the return value of the EC_GROUP_check_named_curve function to ensure correct validation of named curves. (PRs: #19678; commits: 3b6154c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1104] In PKCS7 decryption, the implicit reject mechanism is disabled for RSA keys and a new decryption distribution function is used instead to fix the key matching assumption. (PRs: #13817; commits: 056dade)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1105] Fixed the assertion failure caused by concurrent access to attribute cache in a multi-storage environment. Now when caching attribute definitions, it will check whether entries already exist and reuse them. (PRs: #18631, #19762; commits: 92a25e2)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1106] Added tests for RR/GENM messages, verifying end_time is properly initialized, and adjusted internal handling of total_timeout. (PRs: #19391; commits: b908ec0)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1107] Fixed multiple Coverity issues in HPKE: Removed redundant null pointer checks, changed goto to return; Added checks for RAND_bytes_ex and KEM_INFO_find return values; Removed unused variables and logging code. (PRs: #19774; commits: 450f96e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1108] Fixed bug using wrong encryption level index during handshake key negotiation in QUIC channel, and code cleanup (using assertions and simplified memory copying). (PRs: #19703, #22368; commits: d8a4451, e28f512)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1109] Added a null pointer parameter check to the ossl_gen_deterministic_nonce_rfc6979 function, and changed the goto end in the error handling path to return directly to fix the resource leak problem reported by Coverity. (PRs: #19799; commits: 5e42118)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1110] Fixed an issue where errors were incorrectly raised in retryable read operations of BIO_s_dgram_pair, avoiding spurious errors in normal operation. (PRs: #20030; commits: 3f968ec)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1111] Improved QUIC network error handling: Explicitly handle network errors in the demuxer, including detecting stateless reset tokens and returning permanent failure results; distinguish permanent failures from temporary errors in QTX; treat network errors in channels as connection fatal events, terminate the connection directly when the network BIO fails permanently, and fix related transport parameter parsing and idle timeout handling. (PRs: #19703; commits: 0550829, 66eab5e, df15e99)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1112] Fixed a divide-by-zero error in the ssl3_mac function caused by a hash size of zero. (PRs: #19912; commits: 624efd2)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1113] Fixed the problem of incorrectly returning FIN when infinite skipping in QUIC send stream processing, and added auxiliary functions and cleanup code. (PRs: #19703; commits: 05f9735)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1114] Fixed the double free problem that may be caused by the failure of OPENSSL_strdup() in set_trace_data(), and adjusted the order of setting channel data to avoid accidental release. (PRs: #19959; commits: 0fec212)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1115] Fixed a null pointer dereference crash that could occur in the CMS decryption function when handling unsupported content types. (PRs: #19975, #19981; commits: 69b995c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1116] Fixed the QUIC server connection status judgment, changing the status from "Handshake Completed" to "Handshake Confirmed" to ensure that the server is not considered connected until the handshake is confirmed. (PRs: #20030; commits: ce8f20b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1117] Fixed an issue where RSA_generate_multi_prime_key would segfault when a NULL index was passed in, and added a corresponding test case. (PRs: #20025; commits: 7efc653)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1118] Fixed the TLS hostname selection problem in CMP applications, ensuring that hostnames without ports are obtained correctly, and adding parameter verification and proxy adaptation; at the same time, the diagnostic information of TLS options was improved, warnings when necessary options were missing, and the verification order and flag bit processing were adjusted. (PRs: #16747, #20031, #20034; commits: 30b9a6e, ad1a1d7)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1119] Fixed the issue where the host and port fields in the APP_HTTP_TLS_INFO structure in CMP applications were released prematurely to ensure correct memory management. (PRs: #20034; commits: 20d4dc8)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1120] Fixed a null pointer dereference issue in EC key generation due to memory allocation failure, ensuring that parameters are only set after successful context allocation. (PRs: #20055; commits: 235ef96)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1121] Fixed corruption caused by not adding a write lock to OPENSSL_STACK when searching for CRLs in hashed directories, avoided sk_find mutations by sorting the stack after each modification, and optimized the handling of installations without CRLs. (PRs: #20076; commits: 3147785)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1122] Fixed bug checking for RAND_bytes_ex() in generate_q_fips186_4(), changing the condition from <0 to <=0 to properly capture failure cases where 0 is returned. (PRs: #20100, #20106; commits: a2b01ae)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1123] Fixed an issue where errors were not reported correctly when calling BIO_recvmmsg() using a dgram pair, ensuring that the cause of the error can be logged via ERR_raise when an error occurs. (PRs: #20030; commits: 533390e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1124] Fixed an issue in the padlock engine that caused key byte swapping to be skipped due to conditional compilation. (PRs: #20073, #20146; commits: 849ed51)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1125] Fixed an issue in QUIC where cipher suites were sent repeatedly, removed the QUIC-specific TLSv1.3 cipher suite list, and adjusted the cipher suite list creation logic to avoid allocating memory when the list is empty. (PRs: #20148; commits: d518854)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1126] For applications such as storeutl and gendsa, an error message is given when additional parameters appear after the file/URI parameter, and the documentation is improved to indicate that the option must be given before the file/URI parameter. (PRs: #20156; commits: 323c475)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1127] Fixed the problem of incomplete check of X509V3_add1_i2d() return value, and now can capture both -1 and 0 error codes. (PRs: #20157; commits: ecd4454)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1128] Fixed error checking in default_fixup_args() on the return value of default_check(), changing the condition from less than zero to less than or equal to zero to correctly handle internal error conditions. (PRs: #20175; commits: 650f047)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1129] Fixed an issue with incomplete return value checking of EVP_CIPHER_param_to_asn1(), changing the condition from < 0 to <= 0 to correctly capture error return values. (PRs: #20180; commits: e366371)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1130] Fixed the problem of incomplete check of CMS_SharedInfo_encode return value, and now correctly determines error conditions less than or equal to zero. (PRs: #12628, #20181; commits: ba06181)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1131] Fix BIO_set_indent() return value check, change the error condition from less than zero to less than or equal to zero. (PRs: #17028, #20186; commits: 8263749, a9ed63f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1132] Fix two return value errors in ossl_cmp_msg_check_update(), and extend the CMP version number check to support PVNO_2 and PVNO_3; also correct the error reason code when recipNonce does not match. (PRs: #20190; commits: 7f7dafe, ed9c6f3)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1133] Fixed the problem of incomplete return value checking of multiple BIO related functions (BIO_dup_state, BIO_set_md, BIO_set_accept_name) to ensure correct capture of error conditions. (PRs: #20194, #20195, #20206; commits: 89601c7, a811b63, abf6546)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1134] Fix the handling of -reqin and -rspin options in the CMP client, ensure that the parameters take effect correctly and add necessary nonce updates; also fix the typo in the -newkeypass warning and improve the help text. (PRs: #18657, #20204, #20295; commits: 1f757df, 77aa006, bbaabd1, f1e144f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1135] Fixed multiple bugs in tools in the apps directory: memory leaks in psk_find_session_cb in s_server, incorrect diagnosis of encryption loops in speed testing tools, and crashes in commands such as openssl chacha20. (PRs: #16713, #16714, #20110, #20213, #20291, #20306; commits: 07626ea, 8e2552b, a8cc0ef)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1136] Fix idle timeout handling for QUIC channels, preventing transport parameters from setting idle timeout to 0. (PRs: #20348; commits: 4648eac)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1137] Fixed the lock problem during the QUIC handshake process, refactored the handshake function to correctly acquire and release locks, and added an option mask helper function. (PRs: #20348; commits: 4a53018)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1138] Fix typo in QUIC Thread Assisted Mode, correct mutex type from CRYPTO_RWLOCK to CRYPTO_MUTEX, and adjust compilation conditions. (PRs: #20348; commits: c4208a6)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1139] Fixed the time unit error in early data age calculation and corrected the misused second granularity to millisecond granularity. (PRs: #20387; commits: 0513a38)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1140] Fixed the setting and copying issues of digest property in FFC parameters. (PRs: #20385; commits: 3307338)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1141] Fixed an issue where irrelevant error entries remained in the error queue when early data decryption failed. (PRs: #20377, #20401; commits: 79abf0d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1142] Fix the problem of certStatus memory leak in CMP message construction, and release certStatus when push fails. (PRs: #20406; commits: c9c9901)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1143] Add a null pointer check for memory allocation failure in the stream_frame_new function to prevent potential null pointer dereference. (PRs: #19794; commits: bf762f9)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1144] Check the return value of DSA_set0_key in the ossl_dsa_key_from_pkcs8 function and generate an error message if it fails. (PRs: #20409; commits: dd573a2)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1145] Fix the sendto problem caused by address family size mismatch in dgram_sendmmsg, use BIO_ADDR_sockaddr and BIO_ADDR_sockaddr_size to obtain the address and size instead. (PRs: #20449; commits: a868170)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1146] Fixed the error of misusing local instead of peer when initializing time field and clearing address in QUIC DEMUX. (PRs: #20451; commits: 29fb7f0)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1147] Fix the mismatch between size_t and int types in cms_ec.c and improve error handling. (PRs: #20435, #20457; commits: 559e078)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1148] Fix segfault caused by passing in the wrong context when cleaning up the parent random number generator. (PRs: #20454; commits: 6d45fd4)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1149] Fix a bug in the key pair verification logic in the rsa_has function to ensure that the presence of modulus n is correctly verified when checking the private key. (PRs: #20455; commits: a320716)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1150] Fix the comparison logic of pkeyid in tls12_check_peer_sigalg, use EVP_PKEY_KEYMGMT instead of hard-coded -1. (PRs: #20497, #20501; commits: a2a543e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1151] Fix the resource leak problem caused by memory allocation failure in the ssl_cert_dup function. (PRs: #20504; commits: b36e677)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1152] Fix Windows macro name detection error in quic_reactor, and use INVALID_SOCKET instead of -1 as an invalid socket value. (PRs: #20514; commits: d293ebd)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1153] Limit the maximum sleep time of the sleep function to avoid long loop waiting caused by passing in too large values. (PRs: #20524, #20533; commits: bea92b8)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1154] Fix compatibility issue of UINT64_C macro in argon2 KDF implementation under old compilers, use explicit UL suffix instead. (PRs: #20547; commits: 4b738c1)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1155] The KDF context is no longer released in the derive error path to avoid problems caused by premature release. (PRs: #20528; commits: 6ec3d31)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1156] Fixed mutex operation and lock release issues in QUIC thread-assisted mode, and introduced NAT timeout macro. (PRs: #20348; commits: 9cf091a)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1157] Fix DSA_sign's bounds checking for negative p, q, g values and add related tests. (PRs: #20268, #20553; commits: 9559ad0)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1158] Fix the memory leak caused by ECDSA_sign calling i2d_ECDSA_SIG when the signature pointer is NULL, and add test cases. (PRs: #20553, #20554; commits: 4befe81)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1159] Fix the segfault caused by missing null pointer check in EVP_PKEY_CTX_dup, and add null value judgment for dupctx function pointer when copying signature, key exchange, asymmetric cipher and key encapsulation context. (PRs: #20581; commits: 864c70e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1160] It is forbidden to use QUIC connection when ALPN is not specified, otherwise an error will be reported and terminated; related test cases will be added. (PRs: #20580, #22066; commits: 122d4e2, d98f421)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1161] Fix the lock contention issue during QUIC datagram injection and ensure correct locking when calling SSL_inject_net_dgram. (PRs: #20348; commits: 5129e59)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1162] Fixed the problem in DTLS that application data records were not processed correctly due to out-of-order, and now allow buffering of application data records from the next epoch. (PRs: #20597, #20628; commits: 5c47697)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1163] Fix condition variable wait logic, use correct functions for signaling and adjust wait state masks. (PRs: #20348; commits: 7123606)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1164] Fixed a potential null pointer dereference issue in the get_payload_public_key_ec function, which returns directly when checking eckey for null. (PRs: #20629; commits: bbe9d2d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1165] Fix incorrect null pointer check in evp_keymgmt_gen_cleanup function, change condition from checking gen to checking gen_cleanup. (PRs: #20646; commits: 6469043)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1166] Move the initialization call of configuration loading out of RUN_ONCE to avoid initialization problems caused by recursive calls. (PRs: #20653, #20662; commits: 540c2d1)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1167] Fixed an issue where the public key operation was incorrectly used during ECX key generation and changed to the correct key generation operation. (PRs: #19580, #20680; commits: 40f4884)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1168] Fix bug in least common multiple (LCM) calculation in RSA multi-prime key verification. (PRs: #20693, #20708; commits: efbff4d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1169] Fixed an issue where the set decryption key function was incorrectly used in SM4-GCM hardware acceleration initialization on Kunpeng-920. (PRs: #20751; commits: 524c2ca)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1170] Fixed parameter checking and memory allocation issues in EVP and X509 modules, including check_curve return value judgment, curve name string space allocation and ctrl_cmd default settings. (PRs: #20161, #20770, #20780; commits: 1009940, 4e5f3d6, ac52fe5)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1171] Fix self-signed warning when loading a CSR file with the -vfyopt option, by passing the validation options argument to the CSR load function. (PRs: #20799; commits: a75f707)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1172] Fixed a memory leak in engine_cleanup_add_first() caused by unreleased memory due to insertion failure. (PRs: #20870, #20880; commits: 8c63b14)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1173] Fixed an issue where SSL_trace could not print certificate data under non-default libctx, and corrected the certificate parsing logic to correctly use the specified libctx and attribute query. (PRs: #20914; commits: b946a3e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1174] Fix the problem of unstable rehash results and ensure that file entries are sorted correctly by implementing a comparison function. (PRs: #21013; commits: 31c94b5)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1175] Fix the usage of time callback in QUIC TXP to ensure that the time callback function is called correctly. (PRs: #21029; commits: b98c38d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1176] Fixed a use-after-free issue caused by memory allocation failure when adding x509 policy nodes. (PRs: #21040; commits: de53817)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1177] Fix the error handling of ossl_policy_level_add_node and sk_X509_POLICY_NODE_push in the tree_calculate_user_set function to prevent memory leaks and correctly return the failure status when user_policies allocation fails. (PRs: #21040; commits: 95a8aa6)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1178] The SMIME_crlf_copy function adds a null pointer check for input parameters and returns an error when a null pointer is passed in. (PRs: #21058; commits: 23450cf)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1179] Improve the management of CMP client HTTP callback parameter pointers, release old values before setting new values, and make them empty during cleanup to avoid memory leaks or dangling pointers. (PRs: #21086; commits: dea5e26)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1180] Fixed the issue of pport_num not being initialized in OSSL_parse_url, and added the init_pint auxiliary function to initialize the integer value pointed to by the pointer. (PRs: #21083, #21109; commits: ba189e0)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1181] Fixed the issue where parameters were not converted to unsigned char type when calling functions such as isdigit. (PRs: #21123, #21127; commits: 8229874)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1182] Fix a memory leak that could occur in the ASN1_TIME_normalize function when the input pointer is null. (commits: a33842e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1183] Fix an issue where X509_NAME_cmp could incorrectly return -2 when handling empty names. (PRs: #21155, #21156; commits: ec59752)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1184] Fix an issue where inappropriate groups are requested in HelloRetryRequest, ensuring that groups that have been sent by the client but do not comply with TLSv1.3 requirements are not requested again. (PRs: #21157, #21163; commits: 7a949ae)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1185] Fixed an issue where int_ctx_new() reported an error when using 1.1.1n SM2 keys and EC method engine. (PRs: #21170; commits: 4c4fefa)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1186] Fixed an issue that caused a busy loop due to miscalculation of the next tick deadline when an ACK could not actually be sent. (PRs: #21181; commits: ca71165)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1187] When trying to decode a PKCS#12 object, first check if a MAC exists for authentication, to avoid always prompting for a password without a MAC. (PRs: #21197; commits: 7a52061)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1188] Fixed the issue where the openssl speed -multi -evp command outputs incorrect algorithm name. The specified algorithm name can now be displayed correctly. (PRs: #21216; commits: 33c0934)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1189] Fixed an issue where input data was truncated during pkeyutl decryption, now allowing the complete input data to be read. (PRs: #21272; commits: 8494507)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1190] Fixed memory allocation and reference counting issues in EC_KEY, EVP_RAND and SSL_CTX, including correctly initializing reference counting, releasing allocated resources when allocation fails, and supplementing the error log. (PRs: #21341; commits: 97beb77)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1191] Fix the processing logic of non-ACK trigger packets in QUIC TXP, ensure that the probe request count is correctly decremented, and optimize the flow frame generation and filling behavior. (PRs: #21349; commits: 178c104)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1192] QUIC ACKM no longer records non-transmitting data packets to congestion control to avoid incorrect notification to CC in pseudo packet loss scenarios. (PRs: #21349; commits: 427a02a)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1193] Remove the unreachable type checking code in SSL_CTX_use_certificate_file and fix the error code. (PRs: #21394; commits: 33ef5fc)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1194] Fix an issue in QUIC packet encoding where pointers may become invalid when dynamic buffers grow. (PRs: #21411; commits: 69aef72)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1195] Fix the conditional judgment when handling write retry in ssl3_do_write(), and change the return value check from less than 0 to less than or equal to 0 to correctly handle the situation where BIO returns 0. (PRs: #21422, #21434; commits: 404fb99)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1196] Fixed parameter handling error when setting/getting tags in RSA OAEP in older versions of the engine. (PRs: #21401; commits: 64b1d2f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1197] Fix the problem of incorrect generation of forced PING frames in QUIC TXP to ensure that PING is generated correctly when forced acknowledgment is required. (PRs: #21458; commits: 1e2e683)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1198] Use safe multiplication and division functions in QUIC flow control calculations to expand the range of possible results. (PRs: #21429; commits: 24ae2d7)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1199] Fixed the unreachable code in cms_main(), adjusted the processing logic of recipient certificate parameters during non-encrypted operations, and added corresponding warning information. (PRs: #21474; commits: 8c34367)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1200] Fix a bug generated by STREAM FIN in QUIC TXP to correctly clear the FIN flag when the data block is truncated. (PRs: #21484; commits: 553122c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1201] Fix typo in QUIC channel status enumeration name, correct incorrect QUIC_CSM_STATE_ACTIVE to QUIC_CHANNEL_STATE_ACTIVE. (PRs: #21429; commits: 6861f5a)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1202] Fixed the issue where the X509at_add1_attr function searches the wrong stack when checking for duplicate attributes, and instead searches the target stack correctly. (PRs: #21505; commits: 7551264)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1203] Fixed the problem of mac_export function exporting empty data when empty selection, and added check for private key selection flag. (PRs: #21519; commits: 1ae4678)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1204] Add offset bounds checking to QUIC protocol decoding function, compliant with RFC 9000 section 19.6 specification. (PRs: #21547; commits: 67e72ed)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1205] Added QUIC test cases to verify malformed encrypted stream data, and fixed type conversion issues in related code. (PRs: #21565; commits: de56eeb)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1206] Added a fault injection test case for NEW_CONN_ID frames for QUIC testing, and fixed the processing logic for non-1-RTT packets when injecting ordinary frames. (PRs: #21565; commits: ed75eb3)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1207] Fixed the bug in QUIC TXP where the TXPIM package may still be used after being released, and introduced a reference counting flag to avoid access after release. (PRs: #19206, #21565; commits: 6a2b70e, 6db5cb8)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1208] Added fault injection test cases for QUIC multi-stream testing for STREAM, MAX_DATA and MAX_STREAM_DATA, and fixed the injection function's check of packet type. (PRs: #21565; commits: 1623bf3)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1209] Fixed the issue in the QUIC protocol that the handshake was not advanced when writing with null length, ensuring that the handshake can be completed normally even if zero bytes are written. (PRs: #21578; commits: 33f6ad1)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1210] Fix quicserver not properly tracking buffer write locations when handling requests arriving in chunks. (PRs: #21578; commits: 747b51f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1211] Fixed the regression problem in QUIC UINT_SET removal operation, corrected the range overlap judgment condition and added range truncation processing. (PRs: #21565; commits: dc5e5c5)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1212] Fix the memory leak problem caused by memory allocation failure in the event queue. (PRs: #21565; commits: 77a6611)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1213] Fixed an issue with handling negative IV length values in QUIC QRX to avoid assertion errors caused by type mismatch. (PRs: #21565; commits: b538ae4)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1214] Fix possible null pointer dereference issue in QUIC QTLS. (PRs: #21565; commits: 4d6ca88)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1215] Fix type conversion issue when handling negative IV lengths in QUIC QTX, ensuring nonce is constructed correctly. (PRs: #21565; commits: a2d4915)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1216] Fix the problem that the block_until_pred return value is not checked when the QUIC connection is closed, to avoid errors caused by continued execution after the blocking wait fails. (PRs: #21565; commits: 23406e3)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1217] Fix the problem that the return value of ossl_quic_rstream_read in QUIC TSERVER is not handled correctly, and the related variables are correctly cleared when the read fails. (PRs: #21565; commits: f540b6b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1218] Fixed a null pointer dereference problem in QUIC UINT_SET due to unchecked memory allocation results. (PRs: #21565; commits: 8761efb)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1219] Add selection checks for msblob and pvk decoders, ensuring they are only used if the requested key selection matches. (PRs: #21603; commits: 6207f2b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1220] Fix the error message format in the load_key_certs_crls function: add missing newlines and remove redundant error prompts. (PRs: #21659; commits: 81d037b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1221] Improve the warning text when skipping unknown name attributes in parse_name(), and fix the processing logic when maxlen is -1 in bio_to_mem(). (PRs: #21659; commits: 49e0973)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1222] Fixed a bug where -csr and -serial option values could not be reset. (PRs: #21659; commits: 374945a)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1223] Fixed the problem of incorrectly triggering the wrong envelope content type when releasing the signature content, and added a dedicated function to clean up the envelope content. (PRs: #21676; commits: 13342ef)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1224] Fixed the issue where the el->md pointer was not cleared under the wrong path to avoid potential memory errors. (PRs: #21677; commits: 9d005ba)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1225] Fixed the life cycle management problem of dummybio in QUIC record layer to avoid use-after-free errors. (PRs: #21686; commits: 643f542)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1226] Fixed the problem of reason_len not being set in QUIC protocol error handling, and corrected the return value logic of has_read_ended function when reading fails. (PRs: #21565; commits: f260900)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1227] Fixed a divide-by-zero error in kmac_setkey caused by a zero block size, returning an error instead. (PRs: #21698; commits: 91895e3)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1228] Added appropriate error lifting in multiple locations in QUIC, including protocol errors and parameter errors, and optimized polling descriptor validation logic. (PRs: #21700; commits: 9601484)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1229] Improved QUIC application layer error reporting, changed some non-protocol errors to non-I/O error types, and restructured the channel configuration process to use a unified context; also added the transmission of the connection closure reason and its length. (PRs: #21565, #21715; commits: 2e17601, 40c8c75)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1230] QUIC channels now only handle the first protocol error, subsequent protocol errors will be ignored. (PRs: #21715; commits: 549d0a7)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1231] Improved QUIC TLS error messages, returning more clear error codes and prompts when ALPN is not configured. (PRs: #21715; commits: 881e329)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1232] Fixed the implementation of the BIO_CTRL_DGRAM_GET_LOCAL_ADDR_ENABLE control command in BIO_s_dgram_pair so that it correctly returns the result through the pointer parameter. (PRs: #21715; commits: c20b78d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1233] Added return value check for RAND_bytes(), calling app_bail_out to exit when failed; at the same time, changed the output buffer parameter to inp. (PRs: #21706; commits: 8d120ae)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1234] Fixed the problem of premature discarding of the INITIAL encryption level in the QUIC protocol. The discarding is now only triggered when the server successfully decrypts the HANDSHAKE packet, and the client discards when the HANDSHAKE packet is successfully sent. (PRs: #21713; commits: b6125b5)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1235] Fixed the problem of incorrect use of ossl_assert when bn_wexpand memory allocation fails, and instead directly checks whether the return value is NULL. (PRs: #21668, #21725; commits: 69b9a99)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1236] Fixed a memory leak caused by process_data_dest in the wrong path being not released when copying OSSL_DECODER_CTX. (PRs: #21668, #21741; commits: e16c010)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1237] Fixed premature release of the previous BIO when setting up a new record layer, avoiding release while it was still referenced. (PRs: #21668, #21741; commits: 92e3f43)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1238] Fixed memory leak in tls_new_record_layer: when setting encryption status fails, tls_int_free is now correctly called to free the partially allocated record layer structure. (PRs: #21668, #21753; commits: 0577dba)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1239] Added a check for the return value of ASN1_STRING_set in the spkac_main function to fix potential unchecked return value issues. (PRs: #21755; commits: 20baa24)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1240] Removed a redundant NULL check in cmp_genm.c that would not actually trigger. This change should eliminate Coverity false positives. (PRs: #21755; commits: 2b2eedf)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1241] Fixed an issue in ossl_x509_store_ctx_get_by_subject() where the return value of X509_STORE_lock() was not checked. (PRs: #21755; commits: 6404d06)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1242] Fixed the problem that when sending data, when the data volume exceeds the capacity of a single datagram, datagrams are continuously generated and sent until all data is sent. (PRs: #21798; commits: aa43301)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1243] Correctly initializes all fields of RXE for QUIC unencrypted packets. (PRs: #21764; commits: 413a427)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1244] Fixed the QUIC channel sending logic to ensure that the sent packets can still be refreshed when packet generation fails; at the same time, an error reason parameter was added for closing the local connection. (PRs: #21700; commits: 64fd699)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1245] Fixed a memory leak caused by the incorrect path in the ossl_encode_ctx_setup_for_pkey function not releasing the allocated name stack. (PRs: #21668, #21796; commits: 8ef63b6)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1246] Change TLS record type from int to uint8_t to avoid endianness issues in SSL_trace(). (PRs: #21823; commits: 1cc8c53)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1247] Fixed the processing logic of PING frames in QUIC, adjusted the error code of unknown frames and optimized the frame callback mechanism. (PRs: #21764; commits: de85ec0)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1248] Dynamically determine I/O errors, improve QUIC error classification and processing logic; reconstruct QUIC I/O error processing, add a new auxiliary function to uniformly set last_error, and initialize the error status to no error when entering I/O operations. (PRs: #21829, #21915; commits: 72ca0b8, a954f76)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1249] Generalize the CMP protection calculation function to correctly support Edwards curves, and add corresponding test cases. (PRs: #21564, #21884; commits: e664ef7)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1250] Added data availability check when peeking at the range number of ACK frames to prevent memory over-allocation due to declaring too many ranges. (PRs: #21917; commits: a31601c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1251] Handle non-IO retry errors during the TLS handshake in QUIC connections (such as SSL_ERROR_WANT_RETRY_VERIFY, SSL_ERROR_WANT_X509_LOOKUP, etc.) and propagate these errors up to QCSO. (PRs: #199, #21922; commits: 3a0012c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1252] Fix the condition judgment error in the dupctx function of the aes_gcm_siv cipher family so that it can correctly copy data when aad is not empty. (PRs: #21887, #21933; commits: c32c3f2)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1253] Fix possible memory leaks in CRYPTO_zalloc and CRYPTO_realloc when OPENSSL_MALLOC_FAILURES is enabled, and adjust the fault injection check location. (PRs: #21944; commits: e2cf38d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1254] Fix the ossl_param_build_set_multi_key_bn function to avoid incorrectly setting NULL BIGNUM values to zero. (PRs: #21935, #22334; commits: 15a39e7)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1255] Fix error handling in the engine cleanup function, resolve memory leaks and adjust rollback logic. (PRs: #21971; commits: 00f2efc)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1256] Fix the clearing and setting logic of error flags in OSSL_STORE. (PRs: #21968, #21976; commits: 17dd9a2)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1257] In several demos and tools, NULL is explicitly returned when creating a BIO fails. (PRs: #21950; commits: 11b7d46)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1258] Fix the memory leak caused by users_pwd not being released in the SRP_VBASE_new function. (PRs: #21981; commits: 68e95f7)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1259] Fix the valtype value of the dkeyform option from uppercase F to lowercase f to support engine functionality. (PRs: #21982; commits: b9a189c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1260] Correct the checking method of error return value in cms_sd_asn1_ctrl() function. (PRs: #21986, #21988; commits: 00a413e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1261] Fix memory leak when RSA-PSS parameter encoding fails in rsa_pub_encode function. (PRs: #22032; commits: 285eb16)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1262] Fix corrupted output in req command when used with -out and -modulus options. (PRs: #21403, #22026; commits: d287394)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1263] Fix memory leaks in OpenSSL applications to ensure proper cleanup when key generation and parameter generation fail. (PRs: #22049, #22055; commits: 8c040c0)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1264] Fix memory leak in prepare_rsa_params function when negative salt length or out of memory. (PRs: #22049, #22061; commits: 46def82)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1265] Fixed the issue where the ccm and gcm variants of sm4, aes, and aria omit the redirection key pointer during dupctx. (PRs: #22076, #22102; commits: 0ca5cf9)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1266] Fixed build issues when SSL tracing support is disabled and warning messages are output when the -trace option is specified. (PRs: #21958; commits: b12c07c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1267] Fixed the issue where the error message falsely reported bad decrypt when the encryption operation failed. Bad encrypt will now be displayed correctly when encryption fails. (PRs: #22134; commits: 0e138b7)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1268] Fix the null pointer dereference problem caused by missing jump in engine_table_register. (PRs: #22138; commits: be01f60)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1269] Fix the error handling when there is insufficient memory in CRYPTO_get_ex_new_index to ensure that the meth stack is correctly released and reset when the reserved index fails to avoid subsequent data overwriting. (PRs: #22149; commits: d4f22a9)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1270] Fixed the problem that the contract function in lhash returns directly when realloc fails, resulting in inconsistent hash table status. Now it continues to perform the shrink operation and updates the internal pointer only when realloc succeeds. (PRs: #22154; commits: 5fbfd64)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1271] Fixed the issue where the special processing of foreign key does not take effect correctly when signing using legacy key based on application method under no-engine build. (PRs: #14859, #22163; commits: 1acc3e8)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1272] Fix the quotation mark processing of strings by the ossl_property_list_to_string function to ensure that the generated property strings can be parsed correctly. (PRs: #22182; commits: fb20e66)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1273] Fixed the issue with the null value checking order of global structure pointers when initializing random number seeds. (PRs: #22211; commits: 1541083)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1274] Fix use-after-free vulnerability in random number seed generation function. (PRs: #22211; commits: 6bd0794)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1275] Fixed undefined behavior due to shifting of negative values in the BN_gcd function, avoiding the problem by explicitly converting the relevant shift operand to an unsigned type. (PRs: #22216, #22272; commits: 0f7a3b0)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1276] Fixed segfault caused by private data structure inconsistency in SCTP BIO implementation, embedding generic dgram data into SCTP data structure. (PRs: #20643, #22278; commits: 4bad474)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1277] Fixed the problem of condition variable wait timeout using the wrong time in the QUIC auxiliary thread, ensuring that the real time is passed in to avoid test hangs; at the same time, the lock operation and thread stop signal methods were adjusted. (PRs: #22020, #22301; commits: 2b8d815)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1278] Fixed multiple bugs related to packet processing in the QUIC channel, including default stream pop-up, tick deadline calculation error, and assertion failure caused by Retry packet processing. (PRs: #22106, #22333, #22368, #22410, #22411; commits: 098f27f, 1f8a8c1, 56e3032, cd138c3)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1279] Fixed memory leaks and asynchronous task parameter setting issues in the speed command for KEM and signature algorithm performance tests. (PRs: #21491, #22399; commits: 4e09305, cc7e2b2, df5f419)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1280] Fixed multiple QUIC related issues, including initial token length check, time conversion truncation, protocol violation handling, URXE order, ACK timeout calculation, packet loss detection and null pointer release. (PRs: #21547, #21668, #22436, #22437, #22440, #22452, #22476, #22536, #22538, #22541; commits: 457678d, 461d411, 4d100bb, 82b7a0e, 86e11b1, 9d67bd5, c239bc9, e99c771)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1281] Fixed possible error handling in CMS and PKCS7 due to unchecked EVP_PKEY_get_size returning zero. (PRs: #22459; commits: 7eab00e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1282] Added multiple null pointer checks in ossl_ctrl_internal to prevent null pointer dereferences. (PRs: #22470; commits: 1c03353, 4230172, 54fa5b3, 6f1d3e1)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1283] Fixed memory leak when creating tag octet string failed in RSA OAEP encryption. (PRs: #22336, #22495; commits: f770578)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1284] Fixed an issue where the HTTP client was not properly returning 0 when response length check failed. (PRs: #22504; commits: e63c1a1)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1285] Fixed use of uninitialized variables in TLS extension parsing. (PRs: #22523; commits: 96e58e3)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1286] Fixed the problem of quicserver being unable to bind when there are duplicate host name mappings. Now it will try all addresses and start as long as one is successfully bound. (PRs: #22559; commits: 28932ab)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1287] Fixed an issue where quicserver would get stuck in an infinite loop if a handshake or receive request failed, which would now kill the server and return an error. (PRs: #22557; commits: bb8ecea)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1288] Fixed an issue where s_client would throw an error when receiving data exactly equal to BUFSIZZ. (PRs: #22551, #22558; commits: b4a33ba)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1289] Fixed the resource leak problem caused by wrong injection in ossl_quic_new. (PRs: #22572; commits: b45d053)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1290] Fixed an issue where the want_ack flag was not properly checked when generating ACK frames. (PRs: #22568, #22579; commits: 4febab7)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1291] Fixed an issue where ossl_quic_sstream_is_totally_acked incorrectly returned 0 when no data was appended to the stream. (PRs: #22580; commits: d6b7545)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1292] Fixed a memory leak that could occur when loading built-in compression algorithms. (PRs: #22585; commits: 21f1c2d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1293] Fixed possible macro usage error in ossl_rand_uniform_uint32. (PRs: #22593; commits: 173dca8)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1294] Fixed an issue causing original length tracking errors due to flow control when generating QUIC stream frames. (PRs: #22601; commits: 61a468b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1295] Fixed an issue where keyslot could be leaked under wrong conditions. (PRs: #22600; commits: 478d14c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1296] Fixed a null pointer dereference problem caused by memory allocation failure in ossl_quic_new. (PRs: #22606; commits: e2c6a1d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1297] Fixed the problem of using the implicit length STREAM frame when the PATH_RESPONSE frame exists, and reconstructed the padding logic. (PRs: #22615; commits: ab3b836)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1298] Fixed issue with padding handling of ACK_ONLY type packets in QUIC TXP. (PRs: #22615; commits: 68ed191)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1299] Fixed possible memory leak of PSK temporary keys in ssl3_free. (PRs: #22637; commits: 0fdf6e0)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1300] Fixed a possible memory leak under certain error paths in the custom_ext_add function, ensuring that the custom extension's release callback is called on failure. (PRs: #22638; commits: 9f9dc85)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1301] Fixed a bug in the priority queue removal function that failed to correctly reclaim free slots when removing the last element in the heap. (PRs: #22644, #22646; commits: f5f4bc3)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1302] Fixed an issue in QUIC transports where packets were not marked as full when the stream had taken an implicit length. (PRs: #22658, #22662; commits: fc0dce3)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1303] Fixed the cap check for variable output size in the BLAKE2 algorithm so that it is correctly limited to the algorithm's default output size. (PRs: #22661; commits: aa95fb1)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1304] Fix the issue in dtls1_hm_fragment_new that the msg_header field is not initialized when allocating hm_fragment. Instead, use zero-initialized allocation to avoid referencing undefined data when freeing. (PRs: #22679; commits: 1ea038b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1305] Fixed an issue where the DTLS record layer may incorrectly release the current record layer under certain error conditions, and moved the release logic to dtls1_clear_sent_buffer. (PRs: #22664, #22679; commits: 2bb8394)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1306] Fixed the label length boundary check when calculating the plaintext payload length in the QUIC record layer, correctly returning no available space when the available space is equal to the label length. (PRs: #22699, #22715; commits: 2aba954)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1307] Fix QUIC connection level credit consumption tracking issue to ensure that streaming data stops being added after connection credits are exhausted. (PRs: #22706, #22718; commits: 915ec62)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1308] Fix EVP_PKEY_decrypt output length handling to use actual decryption output length when generating master key. (PRs: #17354; commits: fdef957)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1309] Changed the output of successful self-signed verification of the certificate request in the openssl req -verify command from standard error to standard output. (PRs: #20728, #20858; commits: c154f53)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2585] aes-gcm-avx512.pl: fix non-reproducibility issue (PRs: #20954, #22415; commits: 7aff0b2)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2591] Do not include sparse_array.o in libssl (PRs: #22111; commits: 34d36cd)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2596] Use armv8 .quad instead of .dword (PRs: #21939; commits: 852438a)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2598] Avoid clobbering non-volatile XMM registers (PRs: #21522, #21808; commits: 7b8e27b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2599] powerpc: ecc: Fix stack allocation secp384r1 asm (PRs: #21749; commits: 50f8b93)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2600] ecp_sm2p256-armv8.pl: Copy the argument handling from ecp_nistz256-armv8.pl (PRs: #21874, #21876, #21877; commits: 8a7f30e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2602] Update Cloudflare Quiche to fix a build issue (PRs: #21885; commits: 1cfdbdd)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2603] Fix ChaCha assembly code on 32-bit HPUX itanium systems (PRs: #17067, #21681; commits: 6d38cce)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2604] Fix handling of the "0:" label in arm-xlate.pl (PRs: #21647, #21653; commits: 9607f5c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2606] Fixed incorrect usage of vshuf.b instruction (PRs: #21530; commits: 780ce38)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2612] Fix no-thread-pool building (PRs: #21332; commits: 4890f1f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2614] riscv: Clarify dual-licensing wording for GCM and AES (PRs: #20649, #21018, #21357; commits: 3e76b38)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2615] Fix function signatures in aes-gcm-armv8 comments. (PRs: #21208; commits: 6c0ecc2)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2616] riscv: aes: dual-license under Apache + 2-clause BSD (PRs: #21018; commits: 6181a33)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2617] riscv: GCM: dual-license under Apache + 2-clause BSD (PRs: #20649; commits: 33523d6)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2620] Fix arm64 asm code back compatible issue with gcc 4.9.4 (PRs: #20963, #20967; commits: 09bd0d0)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2623] apps/openssl.cnf: fix reference to insta.ca.crt (PRs: #20832; commits: 14ca1b6)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2627] fix md5 bug on aarch64 big-endian plantform. (PRs: #20829; commits: 004bd8f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2632] Revise build.info (PRs: #20348; commits: 652fbb6)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2635] rsaz-*k-avx512.pl: fix wrong name of avx512 flag variable (PRs: #20519; commits: e5dd732)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2636] aes-gcm-avx512.pl: Fix the clang version detection on Apple Oses (PRs: #20519; commits: 2dbddfa)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2637] riscv: GCM: Use riscv.pm (PRs: #20078; commits: 75623ed)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2641] ec: Use .machine "any" explicitly in ecp_nistp521-ppc64 (PRs: #20378; commits: a6d7093)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2642] Use of sparse_array.c only in the shared libssl (PRs: #20238, #20240; commits: cd870db)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2643] Restrict the Arm 'LDR REG, =VALUE' pseudo instruction on Neon, to appease clang (PRs: #19914, #20202, #20222; commits: a97ca33)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2645] Fix SM4-XTS build failure on Mac mini M1 (PRs: #20202; commits: cded5d0)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2646] Do not include sparse_array.o in libssl with no-shared (PRs: #20137; commits: aea9b0e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2647] Avoid duplicating symbols in legacy.a with some build options (PRs: #20124, #20137; commits: f6a6f7b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2649] Padlock: fix byte swapping assembly for AES-192 and 256 (PRs: #20073, #20077; commits: 7331e7e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2650] Correct property EBNF for unquoted strings (PRs: #20023; commits: 9866200)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2651] Fix big-endian issue in chacha20 SVE implementation on aarch64 (PRs: #19902, #20028; commits: cd7a8e9)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2652] Fix SM4 test failures on big-endian ARM processors (PRs: #19910; commits: accd3bd)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2654] Update pyca-cryptography submodule to fix CI (PRs: #19926; commits: a8e7bc7)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2657] crypto/sha/asm/sha512-ia64.pl: When checking assembler file names, ignore case (PRs: #19604; commits: 119b7b5)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2663] Fix LLVM vs Apple LLVM version numbering confusion, for $avx512ifma (PRs: #16670, #19361; commits: 523e057)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2675] update oqsprovider/liboqs to v0.7.2 (PRs: #19184; commits: 0b66422)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2681] Improve chacha20 perfomance on aarch64 by interleaving scalar with SVE/SVE2 (PRs: #18901; commits: 3f42f41)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2684] Update the oqs-provider submodule to a more recent commit (PRs: #18132; commits: ac837d4)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2685] Fix unrolled montgomery multiplication for POWER9 (PRs: #18883; commits: 2f1112b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2686] Fix GHASH-ASM implementation on s390x (PRs: #18693, #18939; commits: cd854f2)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2690] Fix EC ASM flag passing (PRs: #18791; commits: 1218637)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2691] Fix SHA, SHAKE, and KECCAK ASM flag passing (PRs: #18747; commits: a8b238f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2695] Fix AES OCB encrypt/decrypt for x86 AES-NI (commits: 6ebf6d5)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2697] build.info changes for priority queue (PRs: #18274; commits: 4bb1fdf)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2705] Generate the preprocessed .s files for chacha and poly 1305 on ia64 (PRs: #18263; commits: db24ed5)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2706] Revert "Use .s extension for ia64 assembler" (PRs: #18259, #18263; commits: debd921)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2708] x86asm: Generate endbr32 based on __CET__. (PRs: #18334, #18353; commits: 2721387)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2713] Prefer .inst rather than .long for probe instructions in arm64cpuid.pl (PRs: #18086; commits: 4d63eaf)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2714] Use .s extension for ia64 assembler (PRs: #18136; commits: 6009997)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2716] Fix gcc 6.3 builds of aarch64 BSAES (PRs: #18114, #18127; commits: 5adddcd)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2723] Fix incorrect comments in aes-gcm-armv8-unroll8_64.pl (PRs: #17918; commits: 3b5b919)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2724] aes-gcm-avx512.pl: Fixed mingw64 build (PRs: #17868; commits: 224ea84)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2725] Fixed conditional statement testing 64 and 256 bytes (PRs: #17760; commits: 7e1f3ff)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2727] Use Perl to generate bsaes-armv8.S (PRs: #14592; commits: a35c3a9)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2728] ARM assembly pack: translate bit-sliced AES implementation to AArch64 (PRs: #14592; commits: 82551af)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2730] Fix build issue with aes-gcm-armv8-unroll8_64.S on older aarch64 assemblers (PRs: #17773, #17795; commits: 2507903)
