### Build and CI

- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2369] Fix the shebang of the test script to /usr/bin/env perl, and fix trailing spaces and spelling errors in the documentation and configuration files. (PRs: #16362; commits: 473664a)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2370] Fix linking errors caused by missing AArch64 specific symbols when building on non-AArch64 Armv8 systems. (PRs: #16949, #16951; commits: 3841d0f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2371] Replace the Windows 2016 environment in CI with Windows 2022 and add a Perl installation step. (PRs: #17177, #17183; commits: c87a4dd)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2372] Fix the header file protection macro name in include/crypto/dsa.h to avoid duplication with the protection macro in dsaerr.h. (PRs: #17303; commits: 7db69a3)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2373] Disable floating point formatting support in UEFI builds to avoid compilation issues; and roll back previous fixes to remove conditional compilation and header file inclusion. (PRs: #17547; commits: 619c9ba, f59d72f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2374] Fix build failure for XLC/XLCLANG compiler on AIX: check both _ARCH_PPC64 and __ILP32__ macros in 32-bit builds to avoid false positives. (PRs: #17087, #17497; commits: cfbb5fc)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2375] Fix DJGPP compilation issues: Add support for MSDOS platform in conditional compilation to provide in_addr_t definition, and add necessary Watt-32 header files in sockets.h. (PRs: #17623; commits: b9b211f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2376] Added test support for GCC 11, GCC 12, Clang 13 and Clang 14 compilers in CI, and upgraded the CI environment to Ubuntu 22.04. (PRs: #18225, #18639; commits: 6332f4c, 712c13c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2377] When the child process terminates due to a signal, the signal is now resent to the own process so that the upper shell can correctly capture and output the information. (PRs: #19041, #19042; commits: bf16ee4)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2378] Fixed build failure on FreeBSD due to missing data declarations, adjusted variable scope and added skip logic when matching addresses. (PRs: #19130; commits: ed82261)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2379] Fix the compilation error caused by the missing ipi_spec_dst field in struct in_pktinfo in Cygwin build, and skip the assignment of this field through conditional compilation. (PRs: #19134; commits: 7f4c657)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2380] Enable the usleep-based ossl_sleep() implementation for the djgpp platform to solve the problem of compilation failure caused by circular dependencies. (PRs: #19274; commits: 6512559)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2381] Fixed the problem of compilation failure due to undefined WATT32_NO_OLDIES on the DJGPP platform, which resulted in conflicting Watt-32 library declarations. Define this macro before including the socket header file. (PRs: #19274; commits: 8ae74c5)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2382] Removed explicit MSVC target architecture name in Windows CI configuration and let compiler automatically detect it instead. (PRs: #19281, #19285; commits: 0747f94)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2383] Added DJGPP build support for CI cross-compilation workflow. (PRs: #19307; commits: f9171a0)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2384] Enable QUIC support in Windows CI builds. (PRs: #19345; commits: 7622835)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2385] Added Clang 15 compilation tests in the CI configuration, and added steps to install Clang 15 from the LLVM PPA for Ubuntu 22.04. (PRs: #19450; commits: 75ecda9)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2386] Separate SCTP testing from daily CI workflow into a separate workflow to fix daily build errors. (PRs: #19511; commits: 41e4f72)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2387] Improved the definition of DSO_MALLOC macro to cover all possible compilation scenarios. (PRs: #19529; commits: 89d7231)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2388] Make sure SIZE_MAX is defined when defining OSSL_SSIZE_MAX, by including internal/numbers.h to compensate for operating system differences. (PRs: #19693; commits: 1a298b0)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2389] Fixed build issues on NonStop platforms, adjusted conditional compilation header inclusion. (PRs: #19810, #19811; commits: d861bc0)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2390] Replace no-shared with no-modules in CI configuration to fix ASAN memory leak detection issue. (PRs: #19860; commits: d569654)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2391] Replace snprintf calls in multiple source files with BIO_snprintf to fix Windows build issues. (PRs: #20109; commits: 4a6e5a1)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2392] Fallback of previously added cross-compilation CI configuration for RISC-V Zb* and Zk* extensions, as they are not supported by the current QEMU version. (PRs: #20139; commits: 4597200)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2393] Fix the build file selection logic to use the correct configuration variables; also update the documentation to explain how to specify a custom build file template through environment variables. (PRs: #20173; commits: aa2d7e0)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2394] In the argon2 KDF implementation, stdint.h and limits.h were replaced with openssl/e_os2.h and internal/numbers.h respectively to enhance compatibility with older compilers, and the header file inclusion order was adjusted. (PRs: #20537; commits: 46ce085)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2395] Removed CI workflow for FIPS cross-version compatibility checks for 3.0.0 providers with current versions. (PRs: #20552; commits: 5303608)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2396] Upgrade the actions/setup-python action version used in CI to v4.7.0. (PRs: #20792, #21047, #21455; commits: 3ac96c8, a1c8edc, dbe3635)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2397] Fix OS Zoo CI failure: Update OS matrix, remove obsolete versions and add new ones; install git in Alpine Linux step and remove fuzz/corpora submodule checkout; enable QUIC support in all configurations; optimize workflow configuration. (PRs: #21131, #21825, #21843, #22130; commits: 467e5c1, 597ff76, ab77026, e900942)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2398] Added no-docs build option, which is used to skip the generation and installation of documentation (such as man pages) during build. (PRs: #21240; commits: 956b4c7)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2399] Enable QUIC support by default, update build configuration and documentation, and add related change records. (PRs: #21332; commits: 8a76420)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2400] Provides a default definition for SSL_OP_CISCO_ANYCONNECT in the internal header file when it is not defined in the public header file to fix compilation issues. (PRs: #21626, #21677; commits: f7b2942)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2401] Removed the setting of PTHREAD_MUTEX_NORMAL to resolve build failures caused by this macro not being defined in some glibc configurations. (PRs: #21726; commits: e4d8086)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2402] Add a warning annotation to the provider cross-version compatibility check workflow stating that the PR branch will not be used when a pull request is triggered, but the master branch will be used. (PRs: #21964; commits: 54e60d2)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2403] Add steps to obtain CPU information in multiple CI workflows and continue execution in case of configuration errors in Windows workflows. (PRs: #22232, #22293; commits: 2d374e1, 4ace824)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2583] Configure: do not check for an absolute prefix in cross-builds (PRs: #22723; commits: 50a536a)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2584] Correct 50-nonstop.conf to support QUIC tests under SPT threading models. (PRs: #22588, #22697; commits: e3c3875)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2586] Link libatomic on riscv32 (PRs: #22460; commits: 492bccf)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2587] Update unix Makefile template to handle paths with spaces (PRs: #4668, #21821; commits: 4991841)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2589] Configurations/unix-Makefile.tmpl: Ensure that md-nits always works (PRs: #22240; commits: 0c74339)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2593] Regexp modifier "r" needs perl 5.14; OpenSSL should build with 5.11, so do not use the "r" shortcut. (PRs: #22019; commits: d30695b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2594] Modify 50-nonstop.conf to enable c99 extensions for uintptr_t. (PRs: #22002, #22005; commits: e9f8e92)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2595] VMS: More header inclusion compensation for VMS C compiler (PRs: #21959; commits: e0f4976)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2597] VMS: More header inclusion compensation for VMS C compiler (PRs: #21951; commits: d94bee8)

### Documentation

- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2217] Added annotation for chunk order in BIGNUM structure. (PRs: #17139; commits: 99d3349)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2218] Updated CHANGES.md file to add change entries for version 3.0.1 and adjust the version title format. (PRs: #17274; commits: c868d1f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2219] Add version title in CHANGES.md for upcoming 1.1.1n release. (PRs: #17714; commits: 522a32e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2220] Updated the upgrade guide link in the README to the migration_guide man page, and updated the copyright year. (PRs: #17840; commits: 96a7766)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2221] Add missing link to OpenSSL 3.0 man page in README. (PRs: #17855; commits: a20c9b6)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2222] Fix NOTES-Windows.md document format problem and resolve document check failure. (PRs: #17960; commits: bd28a23)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2223] Add Tomáš Mráz's key fingerprint to the release key fingerprint documentation. (PRs: #18138; commits: 2c0a944)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2224] Add design requirements document for QUIC packet demultiplexer. (PRs: #18249; commits: 6347b86)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2225] It is stated in CHANGES.md that case-insensitive string comparison no longer uses locale and has been implemented directly. (PRs: #18389; commits: 8a66b2f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2226] Added design documentation for QUIC connection ID caching, and updated related descriptions to support many-to-one matching. (PRs: #18824, #20749; commits: 4efc969, 538ee4e, 5be1543)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2227] Updated CHANGES.md and NEWS.md to add multiple change entries for the OpenSSL 3.1 version, and document security fixes and version changes. (PRs: #18833, #19379, #19565, #19808; commits: 3c53032, 79edcf4, cbb1cda, e0fbaf2)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2228] Remove obsolete ssl/record/README.md file. (PRs: #18132; commits: df60982)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2229] Add missing documentation for X509_REQ_get_extensions() and X509_REQ_add_extensions{,_nid}(). (PRs: #18931; commits: 47dc828)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2230] Add missing documentation for X509_gmtime_adj() function. (PRs: #18974; commits: 425e972)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2231] Fix missing closing bracket in rsa command help text. (PRs: #19049; commits: 3c1f8fb)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2232] Added design documentation for the QUIC Stream Receive Buffers module. (PRs: #19149; commits: fb8bdbe)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2233] Organize RIPEMD160 in CHANGES.md, add the version record of the default provider, and remove duplicate entries. (PRs: #19375, #19438; commits: b655379, ce9317a, fba3242)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2234] Added and improved the QUIC API overview design document, describing API design goals, new and changed interfaces, and updated the glossary. (PRs: #19769, #20856, #21029; commits: 2b54270, 5633a32, 5c0356a, 88e3a64, aef2496)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2235] Added and improved the QUIC I/O architecture design document, added block diagrams and adjusted wording. (PRs: #19770; commits: aed7082, dda8647)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2236] Add documentation for EVP_ASYM_CIPHER-RSA, and update the missing documentation list. (PRs: #19692; commits: ad60cd5)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2237] Add documentation and danger warnings for BIO_s_dgram related functions, and update missing documentation list. (PRs: #19795; commits: 408622b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2238] Added records for KMAC support in KBKDF in CHANGES.md. (PRs: #19943; commits: ec3342e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2239] Add a comment to the buffer adjustment function of the QUIC test fault injector stating that the buffer is over-allocated. (PRs: #20030; commits: 47d905f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2240] Add design documentation for QUIC thread-assisted mode. (PRs: #20348; commits: 27c49c0)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2241] Display the default number of iterations for PBKDF2 (10000) in the enc command's help and documentation. (PRs: #20466, #20471; commits: 6678b08, dc43f08)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2242] Document the new -no_drbg_truncated_digests option in the FIPS schema documentation and change log to disable the use of truncated digests in Hash and HMAC DRBGs. (PRs: #20521; commits: 808b30f, e14fc22)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2243] Add documentation for advanced command mode (-adv option) and QUIC mode for s_client. (PRs: #20566, #20580; commits: 90ae2c1, b21306b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2244] Update CHANGES.md and NEWS.md to note security fixes (CVE-2023-0465, CVE-2023-0464, CVE-2023-1255) and correct relevant documentation. (PRs: #20228, #20561, #20571, #20585, #20759; commits: 5ab3f71, 5f14b5b, 72dfe46, 83ff6cb, 986f9a6)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2245] Add a comment in Windows thread-related code stating that the LONG type is the same size as an int. (PRs: #20856; commits: a2c61e4)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2246] Add RFC 9000 compliance annotation in NEW_CONNECTION_ID frame processing to indicate that the current implementation meets relevant requirements. (PRs: #21135; commits: 5cc7369)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2247] Added a non-blocking QUIC client example, and improved the socket creation function to support non-blocking mode and error handling. (PRs: #21204, #21715; commits: 23fe02e, 92db6d6, b3e71db)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2248] Fix missing comma in option list in openssl-rsautl man page. (PRs: #21318; commits: 83f9d03)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2249] Updated comments in QUIC transport code to add reference to RFC 9000 section 10.2.3 to clarify compliance. (PRs: #21429; commits: d15d5ea)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2250] Added comments to the sample code and added a non-blocking QUIC client sample guide page. (PRs: #21765, #21950; commits: 8d74a13, e8a5b06)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2251] Expand instructions on how to do other useful work in non-blocking examples, and fix wrong protocol type in comments. (PRs: #21950; commits: 38c3c1d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2252] Rename the design document OSSL_PROVIDER_load_ex to ossl-provider-load-ex.md, and update the document title simultaneously. (PRs: #22029; commits: 0988de2)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2253] Added design documentation describing how to allow AlgorithmIdentifier parameter data to be passed to cryptographic operations and providing convenience functions for all algorithms. (PRs: #172, #22162; commits: 11f69aa)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2254] Added a link to the nghttp3 library in the README document for the HTTP/3 examples, and updated build and run instructions. (PRs: #22369, #22623; commits: 0f96c6e, 10c0424)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2255] Add comments to the HTTP/3 demo code to explain the design intent of key functions and processes. (PRs: #22369; commits: 47f8cfe)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2256] Added a reference to the demos subfolder in the README document to guide users to view the sample code. (PRs: #22505; commits: 5e3735b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2257] Supplemented the RFC 9114 reference for HTTP/3 in the QUIC documentation and added a link to the IANA standards list of ALPN protocol IDs. (PRs: #22505; commits: a214e06)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2258] Added separate README for guide examples, explaining how to set LD_LIBRARY_PATH to run, and updated links and descriptions in the main README and QUIC README. (PRs: #22505, #22552; commits: 0564778, 70de526, f7f40db)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2259] Updated changelog and news documentation for the OpenSSL 3.2 beta 1 release. (commits: 26ecab1)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2260] Modify the TLS example program to accept hostname and port from command line arguments and add support for IPv6 options. (PRs: #22552; commits: 151af35)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2261] It is documented in CHANGES.md that the BLAKE2b hash algorithm supports configuring the output length by setting the size parameter. (PRs: #22583; commits: 676f6e2)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2262] Add link to OpenSSL 3.2 manual page in README. (PRs: #22625; commits: 1b83adc)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2263] Updated CHANGES.md and NEWS.md to document CVE-2023-5678, OpenSSL 3.0.2 and 3.0.3 version changes, OPENSSL_str[n]casecmp function changes, and PVK KDF migrations. (PRs: #15968, #18302, #22647; commits: 4ee71b4, 73e044b, 8b97bfc, a40398a, c8ffd22)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2318] Update the format, header description and reference links of NEWS.md and CHANGES.md to make them more consistent with the release notes specification. (PRs: #22785; commits: 5e07ea4, d330fef)

### Functional Changes / Refactorings

- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1539] When processing TLS 1.3 records under KTLS, steps such as depadding, outer type checking and inner type extraction are skipped, and record length checking and type pointer passing in message callbacks are adjusted. (PRs: #16693, #17942, #19343; commits: 64da15c, a5fb960, b6f7519)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1540] Clarify the logic of the subject/req parameter in X509V3_set_ctx used to construct the SAN email address from the subject DN, and improve the coding style and error codes. (PRs: #17145; commits: 317acac)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1541] Improve diagnostic information for transactionID mismatches in CMP, unify the use of the i2s_ASN1_OCTET_STRING function to handle the string representation of ASN1_OCTET_STRING, and adjust the recipNone check for consistency. (PRs: #17224, #17251; commits: a3ea35c, e469971)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1542] Replaced unsigned long type in DER writer with uint32_t, and renamed related functions and updated call sites. (PRs: #18615; commits: 5919625)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1543] When the DTLS record layer is closed, push the unprocessed records in the buffer to the next record layer object and adjust the epoch setting of the unprocessed record queue. (PRs: #18132; commits: 7a15ed6)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1544] Use OSSL_TRACE_STRING to output message body instead in OSSL_HTTP_REQ_CTX_nbio, and optimize Content-Type matching, error status handling and trace output format. (PRs: #18704; commits: 35b76bc)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1545] Migrate the read buffer release logic of SSL_MODE_RELEASE_BUFFERS to the record layer, and clean up the legacy TODO code. (PRs: #18132; commits: 3de7695)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1546] Renamed the DTLS1_BITMAP structure and related functions to DTLS_BITMAP, and adjusted bit operations to support 64-bit systems. (PRs: #18132; commits: f6aab7b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1547] Renamed require_ca parameter to non_leaf to more accurately reflect the meaning. (PRs: #19064; commits: 8a2f9a7)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1548] Migrate the internal time representation of the libssl and crypto modules from time_t and struct timeval to OSSL_TIME, the public API keeps the original type but performs the conversion internally. (PRs: #18274, #18676, #18985, #19082; commits: 340fe50, 4d32f53, 4fc04c7, 5d1bb4f, d6bfdf6, f0131dc)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1549] Change the parameter type of the COMP_METHOD structure and related functions from unsigned int to size_t, change the return value to ossl_ssize_t, add NULL pointer checking, and remove non-functional COMP_METHODS. (PRs: #18186; commits: 7e3caca)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1550] Refactor the logging layer to provide correct return values, and replace SSLfatal calls with RLAYERfatal. (PRs: #19198; commits: 320145d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1551] Call get_max_records in the record layer code, decide how to divide the data to be written into records based on the returned data, and adjust the record release and write functions accordingly. (PRs: #19198; commits: 02719d5)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1552] Moved the QUIC_CONNECTION type definition to an internal header file, and added functions to get the QUIC_CONNECTION pointer from the SSL pointer, as well as setters/getters for the QRX and ACKM fields. (PRs: #18838; commits: d5ab48a)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1553] Remove legacy TODO comments and adjust alignment calculation logic when writing TLS records. (PRs: #19198; commits: 4fed6ee)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1554] Removed two no-op function pointers from OSSL_RECORD_METHOD that were never called. (PRs: #19472; commits: fba0206)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1555] QUIC RX adds reference counting support, no longer requires the caller to manage reference counting, and removes the OSSL_QRX_PKT_WRAP wrapping structure. (PRs: #19703; commits: 6d5d5fc)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1556] The logic of automatically discarding the Initial encryption layer in the QUIC receiver has been removed, and this operation is handled by the upper layer. (PRs: #19703; commits: 203b0d0)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1557] QUIC TX no longer manages BIO reference counting internally, and changes ossl_qtx_set1_bio to ossl_qtx_set_bio. The caller needs to ensure the BIO life cycle by itself. (PRs: #19703; commits: cdd3f73)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1558] Removed several no longer used fields in the SSL_CONNECTION structure, including encryption context, compression/expansion, IV and MAC keys, etc., to clean up the internal structure. (PRs: #19586, #19748; commits: 1e42708, 6d814fd, b83eac4, e95d6e1, f471f60)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1559] Roll back the timing attack fix in RSA decryption, but keep the change to move derive_kdk to a separate function. (PRs: #20281; commits: 4209ce6)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1560] Added CERTIFICATE_VERIFY_MAX_LENGTH constant, which is used to limit the maximum length of CertificateVerify message and is applied in the state machine. (PRs: #20486; commits: c6d14bf)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1561] Refactor the DRBG implementation so that it manages the acquisition and release of locks by itself and no longer relies on the lock/unlock prompts of the EVP layer. (PRs: #20286, #20970; commits: 189ad3a)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1562] Improve the checking logic of -tls_used option in CMP client: automatically enable -tls_used when -server URL is HTTPS. (PRs: #21176; commits: 4a9299a)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1563] The reference count operation for updating a DH object is a structure-based atomic operation. (PRs: #21260; commits: 9015cbb)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1564] Update DSA's reference counting operations to structure-based atomic operations. (PRs: #21260; commits: 495e6d3)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1565] Updated the DSO module's reference counting operations to be structure-based atomic operations. (PRs: #21260; commits: aaab365)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1566] Updated ASN1 locking functions to use struct-based atomic operations to manage reference counting. (PRs: #21260; commits: 420ad86)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1567] Updated the EC module's reference counting operations to a struct-based atomic implementation. (PRs: #21260; commits: 1353736)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1568] Change the provider's reference counting and activation counting operations to structure-based atomic operations. (PRs: #21260; commits: 8752694)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1569] Update RSA's reference counting to struct-based atomic operations. (PRs: #21260; commits: 97937cf)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1570] When sending congestion control probe packets, full-size packets are no longer generated, but only small packets containing PING and ACK frames are generated. (PRs: #21349; commits: d56b564)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1571] QUIC TXP has been significantly refactored to properly handle fill logic and introduce a new package generation geometry calculation and submission process. (PRs: #21458; commits: c206f2a, faebafd)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1572] Removed extra parentheses in felem_reduce method in secp384r1. (PRs: #21749; commits: 670e73d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1573] Removed the integrity check of private key parameters in ossl_rsa_todata so that it only returns the parameters that have been set. (PRs: #21935, #22334; commits: 4ad3a44)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1574] Pass the DTLS record version to the message callback function. (PRs: #22241; commits: 5f79670, b31597d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1582] Change the strength checking of TLS RC4 ciphers to be data-driven, adjusting the strength value and removing hard-coded security checks. (PRs: #16652, #16656; commits: c3b5fa4)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1599] Unify macro naming for SSL key exchange algorithms, replacing the old aliases SSL_kEDH and SSL_kEECDH with the more commonly used SSL_kDHE and SSL_kECDHE. (PRs: #17763; commits: 66914fc)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1600] The using_ktls auxiliary variable is introduced in ssl3_get_record() to simplify the conditional judgment of the KTLS receiving path. (PRs: #17942; commits: 031132c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2610] Move Keccak rhotates tables to rodata (PRs: #21440; commits: 2fd82c2)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2626] QUIC CC: Move dummy method to test code (PRs: #20423; commits: b633cf8)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2633] Refactor build.info (PRs: #20348; commits: 6c091ca)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2639] riscv: aes: Move reusable Perl code into Perl module (PRs: #20078; commits: c8a641c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2656] Move tls_pad.c into ssl/record/methods (PRs: #19586; commits: c89c21f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2659] providers/common/der/oids_to_c.pm: Remove use of Data::Dumper (PRs: #19546, #19548; commits: 57d2bcc)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2676] openssl.cnf: split option value and comment and remove leading space (PRs: #19173; commits: fd24de9)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2709] Rename x86-32 assembly files from .s to .S. (PRs: #18353; commits: 9968c77)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2721] Remove further uses of __ARMEL__ in AArch64 assembly (PRs: #14592, #17373, #17958, #17988; commits: 2bd5cde)

### Maintenance

- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2471] Fixed the tab character and indentation formatting issues in two Perl scripts and unified them to 4 spaces indentation. (PRs: #16459; commits: ea0d79d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2472] Fixed the indentation format issue of tls13_hkdf_expand function parameters. (PRs: #16489; commits: 1b9e467)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2473] Add Apache 2.0 copyright headers to CI workflow scripts. (PRs: #16628; commits: 08d8c2d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2474] Fixed coding style issues in 113 files, involving multiple modules. (PRs: #16918; commits: 1287dab)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2475] Add missing empty lines in NEWS.md. (PRs: #17274; commits: 1f8ca9e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2476] Fix coding style issues reported by check-format.pl in apps/cmp.c. (PRs: #17363; commits: fd989c7)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2477] Fix indentation issue in providers/common/provider_util.c. (PRs: #17572; commits: 1751356)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2478] Update .gitignore to ignore files generated by tests /test/evp_pkey_ctx_new_from_name. (PRs: #18251; commits: ac23650)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2479] Fix the code format indentation problem of BLOCK_CIPHER_custom macro call. (PRs: #18412; commits: 1c5a4e3)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2480] Fixed typos and terminology inconsistencies in multiple files, including changing fall thru to fall through, host name to hostname, time zone to timezone, etc., to align with the LDP standard. (PRs: #16712, #19059; commits: 9929c81, c734058)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2481] Improve the output format of openssl list command by adding a blank line at the end of each command and algorithm list to make the output clearer. (PRs: #19140, #19372; commits: ec1d597)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2482] Add TODO comments in the QUIC implementation to mark sections that are currently no-ops as well as protocol errors and cache optimizations that need to be handled in the future. (PRs: #19703; commits: c8b3fdc)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2483] Fix style issues in QUIC code, including adding blank lines and adjusting preprocessor directive indentation. (PRs: #19703; commits: d50e750)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2484] Add TODO comment about CCM support in QUIC implementation. (PRs: #19703; commits: c41c7ee)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2485] Style fixes were made to the QUIC connection state machine code, including indentation adjustments, comment updates and standardization of conditional judgments. (PRs: #19703; commits: 75b2920)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2486] Add missing copyright header to crypto/bn/rsa_sup_mul.c file. (PRs: #20461, #20470; commits: 93b0a1e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2487] Adjust the code format and fix a typo. (PRs: #20436; commits: f42d6b7)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2488] Fix comment punctuation errors in QUIC test files. (PRs: #20451; commits: 03fa512)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2489] Add a comment in the do_X509_sign() function to indicate that RFC 5280 compliance measures may be considered in the future. (PRs: #19805, #21086; commits: 586b540)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2490] Add comments to the cert_response and initial_certreq functions, mark unused parameters, and adjust status constants to enhance readability. (PRs: #21086; commits: afe7a43)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2491] Update the .gitignore file to add ignore entries related to LSP support to prevent developers from accidentally committing these files. (PRs: #21338; commits: 9e87e4e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2492] Add note about RFC 9000 section 10.2 persistence time in QUIC channel termination status. (PRs: #21429; commits: a441d08)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2493] Fixed indentation format issue in function code in QUIC server. (PRs: #21135; commits: f36504c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2494] Maintenance improvements have been made to the noisy dgram BIO test assistant, including adding optional debug output, removing unused redundant read and write functions, and unifying terminology naming. (PRs: #22157; commits: 523c5a0, c29b13a, fbfcc3f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2495] Update CHANGES.md and NEWS.md files to synchronize version change records and prepare for the OpenSSL 3.2.0 release. (PRs: #22647, #22805; commits: 2d0d3ed, cf28777)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2555] Fix uint64_t format specifier incompatibility in QUIC trace and test code. (PRs: #21332; commits: 0cea6df)

### New Features

- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0318] Sockets now display the target address when connecting. (PRs: #16392; commits: 994fa5f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0319] Enables UTF-8 name option by default, sets -nameopt utf8 as default behavior. (PRs: #16583; commits: 86cfd13)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0320] Added -ktls command line option to s_client to enable Kernel TLS functionality. (PRs: #16609; commits: e396c11)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0321] Added -ktls option to s_server to enable KTLS, and made -sendfile option dependent on -ktls; also added -tfo, -cert_comp, -enable_server_rpk, -enable_client_rpk and -zerocopy_sendfile options. (PRs: #16609; commits: e2ef7f1)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0322] Supports sending new extensions in the certificate request message, and the client can echo these extensions in the certificate message. (PRs: #16632, #16634; commits: cbb862f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0323] Allow empty or missing summary parameters to be passed in core_obj_add_sigid, and updated related documentation and tests. (PRs: #16770; commits: 4f71624)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0324] Enable the -sans option of the CMP application to support email addresses (rfc822Name type). (PRs: #16960; commits: 03ee2e5)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0325] Adds wrap mode to the enc command, brotli/zstd compression support, configurable salt length, and allows only printing of keys and IVs when no input file is available. (PRs: #17691, #17843; commits: 7850cc8, f4f397a)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0326] Implemented the AES-256-CTR encryption algorithm in the dasync engine. (PRs: #17532, #17995, #19386; commits: bd363ef)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0327] Enable tracing for HTTP clients, output request and response headers, and dump response content on errors. (PRs: #18386; commits: e8fdb06)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0328] Added request and response tracking logging functionality to the APPS HTTP server, restructured log output and normalized error messages. (PRs: #18434; commits: 8aff29f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0329] When using the -CA option and not specifying -CAserial or -CAcreateserial, the x509 tool generates random serial numbers and improves serial number loading error handling. (PRs: #18373; commits: ec8a340)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0330] Add a warning for x509 applications when the -CA option is not specified, and when options such as -CAkey are ignored. (PRs: #18373; commits: c54a6a4)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0331] Added the ability to print the recommended private key length in the DH key text output, and changed the key selection criteria from public keys to key pairs. (PRs: #18480; commits: ff54094)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0332] Added -macsaltlen option to pkcs12 command, allowing user to specify MAC salt length, and updating default salt length. (PRs: #18489, #18550, #21107; commits: 4c56539, e393064)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0333] Add interactive reconnect command C to s_client, allowing users to disconnect and reconnect to the server at any time during the session. (PRs: #18683; commits: 511c491)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0334] Add configuration option support for the speed command and introduce the CONF variable for reading configuration. (PRs: #16986, #18616; commits: 8403c73)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0335] A new ossl_time_muldiv utility function has been added to the time module. (PRs: #19082; commits: 364c3b7)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0336] Added -mlock option to speed command, which locks the throughput measurement buffer to memory to reduce paging effects, this feature is only supported on Linux and Windows. (PRs: #19091; commits: 9710d72)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0337] A new --all-algorithms option has been added to the list command to list all available algorithm categories. (PRs: #19145, #19168; commits: 35b6707)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0338] Added a limit on the number of delayed datagrams for the QUIC record layer receiver, and added related configuration parameters. (PRs: #19251; commits: 0ff9813)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0339] The configured maximum send fragment length is applied in the write record layer, and the buffer allocation logic is adjusted. (PRs: #19343; commits: 435d88d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0340] Added a new encryption post-processing step function, which is used to add MAC and other operations after encryption, and reconstructed the related write record function to call this new function. (PRs: #19343; commits: 2a354d5)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0341] Add is_empty function for list data structure and add corresponding tests. (PRs: #19377; commits: b6f1b05)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0342] Added a function to obtain PTO duration for QUIC ACKM. (PRs: #19703; commits: 4ed9e0a)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0343] Added connection ID encoder/decoder for QUIC transport parameters and made the ID field optional. (PRs: #19703; commits: a64d824)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0344] Add the function of re-injecting URXE and checking pending URXE to QUIC demux, and fix the return value logic of the injected function; at the same time, reconstruct the operation logic of the pending URXE list. (PRs: #19734; commits: 64222fc, 93e9b6c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0345] Add server-side state machine support to QUIC DHS, refactor the original client-side state machine code into an independent function, and introduce error status handling. (PRs: #19734; commits: b83cf3f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0346] Added error reason text for QUIC channel transport parameter handling, and added server-side support. (PRs: #19734; commits: 3c567a5)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0347] Adds basic server-side support for QUIC channels, including functionality for handling new connections, reset tokens, TLS clocks, receive preprocessing, and forged packet limit checks. (PRs: #19734; commits: b1b06da)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0348] Implement the QUIC test server module, add new interfaces for creation, release, connection status and handshake status query, and optimize resource release logic. (PRs: #19734; commits: 51a168b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0349] Added the option to specify the number of subprime q bits during DSA key generation through command line parameters to the dsaparam command, and optimized the context initialization and error handling of parameter generation and key generation. (PRs: #19898; commits: 535ddd3)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0350] Add help functionality to the genpkey command line tool that displays the pkeyopt parameter types and values. (PRs: #19931; commits: 2c1ec72)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0351] Change HKDF's info buffer to dynamic allocation, remove the fixed size limit and increase the upper limit. (PRs: #19909, #19923; commits: e8115bd)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0352] Public key checking is implicitly enabled when the -pubin option is specified. (PRs: #20044; commits: 3b1c0c8)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0353] Added condition variable waiting function ossl_crypto_condvar_wait_timeout with timeout, supporting POSIX and Windows platforms. (PRs: #20348; commits: 2b2b267)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0354] Add a lock mechanism to the QUIC front end to ensure multi-thread safety. (PRs: #20348; commits: a848925)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0355] Allow the caller to determine whether an ACK-eliciting packet has been sent, and add related tests. (PRs: #20348; commits: 134b79c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0356] Implements QUIC thread helper core functionality, including stopping, waiting for stop, cleanup, and notification of deadline changes. (PRs: #20348; commits: 9f7acf0)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0357] Added QUIC protocol support to the s_client tool, including command line options, I/O multiplexing, and sending FIN functionality. (PRs: #20580; commits: c4f74e7, f34e5d7)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0358] Optimize the timing of QUIC channel creation, create channels in advance, and store initial flow control parameters. (PRs: #20765; commits: 0815b72, 23c0470)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0359] The fipsinstall tool adds -no_drbg_truncated_digests and -pedantic options to control DRBG digest and strict FIPS mode. (PRs: #20521, #20752, #21057; commits: b345dbe, bc2a422, c88e01a, d30fec6)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0360] The openssl dgst -list command now also lists digest algorithms that are not available through the new API but are available through the old API. (PRs: #20651; commits: 7eab768)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0361] The CMP tool supports specifying certificates to be revoked by issuer and serial number, and improves the verification and warning logic of related options. (PRs: #21116; commits: 1d32ec2)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0362] Two new helper functions have been added to the params module, which are used to allocate a buffer and copy matching parameters, and allocate a buffer and concatenate all matching parameters. (PRs: #20717, #20724; commits: 79523d5)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0363] Added advanced command mode to s_client, commands are enclosed in curly braces {} and support parameters, while improving connection information output and user data processing. (PRs: #20566; commits: d07b763)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0364] Added CRYPTO_GET_REF function to support querying the current reference count value and reconstructing related reference counting operations. (PRs: #20765; commits: 008a61a)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0365] Added support for --version and its synonyms to the openssl command line tool, and fixed the resource release method of the wrong output stream. (PRs: #20936; commits: 831ef53)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0366] Add setter functions for message callbacks and callback parameters to ensure that these values are correctly propagated to QRX, QTX and TXP after channel creation. (PRs: #20914; commits: 5cf99b4)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0367] Add ERR_raise() calls to QUIC related functions to record errors in scenarios such as EVP call failure and improve error tracking capabilities. (PRs: #21700; commits: cb19528)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0368] Add the ability to set SSL_trace as a message callback to tserver, and refactor internal structures to support SSL objects and thread safety. (PRs: #21800; commits: cb93128)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0369] The RSA key generation controls rsa_keygen_pubexp and rsa_keygen_primes now also work with RSA-PSS keys. (PRs: #21818; commits: e2c2cca)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0370] The functions get_rsa_payload_n, get_rsa_payload_e and get_rsa_payload_d now also support RSA-PSS keys. (PRs: #21818; commits: cf71283)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0371] Broadcast support for condition variables has been implemented on Windows XP, and new functions such as condition variable creation, release, wait, timeout wait, signal and broadcast have been added. (PRs: #21827; commits: 425a780)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0372] A new -saltlen option has been added to OpenSSL's pkcs8 command line tool, allowing users to specify the salt value length. (PRs: #21858; commits: 9f679bd)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0373] Added -saltlen option to the OpenSSL enc command line tool, allowing users to customize the salt value length of PBKDF2 (default 16 bytes), replacing the previous fixed upper limit of 8 bytes. (PRs: #21858; commits: e399458)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0374] Add the -quiet option to the req command and adjust the progress point display behavior so that it is displayed when -verbose and hidden when -quiet. (PRs: #21937; commits: c1673a6)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0375] Control command processing related to polling descriptors is implemented in BIO_s_sock. (PRs: #21979; commits: b79e73c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0376] Added -outpubkey option to genpkey command to support outputting public key file separately. (PRs: #22173; commits: 6c03fa2)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0377] Replaced the jdkTrustedKeyUsage configuration option with the new -jdktrust command line option, which automatically enables -nokeys when specified. (PRs: #22215, #22422; commits: 21f7a09)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0378] In QUIC connection closure information query, added support for the frame type that caused the closure. (PRs: #22485; commits: 55abe74)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0379] Updated the QUIC sample program to support specifying hostname and port via command line arguments and added support for IPv6 addresses. (PRs: #22552, #22577; commits: 3b60efa, 3b86698)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0380] Added a fallback mechanism for key loading in store_result, which attempts to obtain the key management object from the storage provider when loading from the global key management object fails. (PRs: #17531, #17554; commits: 4cfcc7e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0396] The evp_test test framework adds thread support, and the maximum number of threads can be set through the Threads configuration item. (PRs: #12256; commits: ae1792e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0417] Added internal static assertion macro ossl_static_assert_type_eq for compile-time type checking. (PRs: #12256; commits: 0e200d2)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-0429] The subject or issuer name of an X.509 object is now displayed as a UTF-8 string by default. (PRs: #16583; commits: 537976d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2592] LoongArch64 assembly pack: add ChaCha20 modules (PRs: #21998; commits: 9a41a3c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2624] aes-gcm-armv8_64 asm support bigdian (PRs: #20489; commits: 5073565)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2631] Add openssl/fuzz-corpora repository as submodule (PRs: #20683; commits: 3565cc2)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2638] riscv.pm: Add improved error messages to read_reg code (PRs: #20078; commits: ac97cd6)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2640] aes/asm/bsaes-armv7.pl: Replace adrl with add (PRs: #20458; commits: 27093ba)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2677] add build support for riscv32 aes zkn (PRs: #18308; commits: b733ce7)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2678] Add RISC-V 32 cpuid support (PRs: #17640, #18308; commits: 6117064)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2680] Add AES implementation in riscv32 zkn asm (PRs: #18308; commits: b1b889d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2694] Add sparse array to libssl (PRs: #18345; commits: 5067c18)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2699] Add riscv scalar crypto extension capability (PRs: #18197; commits: d5dd608)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2700] add build support for riscv64 aes zkn (PRs: #18197; commits: 9912c38)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2701] Add AES implementation in riscv64 zkn asm (PRs: #18197; commits: 608cadf)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2702] Add VERSIONINFO resource to legacy provider if it is not builtin (PRs: #18416; commits: 9510661)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2711] Add AES implementation in generic riscv64 asm (PRs: #17640; commits: b3504b6)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2715] Add support for new release commit review requirement bypass (PRs: #18155; commits: a625354)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2745] Add missing define to enable AES-NI usage on x86 platform (PRs: #16858, #16866; commits: d92c696)

### Other Changes

- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2579] Updated documentation and release notes to correct IPv6 loopback address example. (PRs: #21802, #21825; commits: 7542bdb, 7a12e7a)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2580] Update manual to reference the IANA TLS cipher suite registry. (PRs: #16179; commits: 0865200)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2581] Update CHANGES.md to document default SSL/TLS security level changes. (PRs: #16760; commits: a4c4090)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2582] Cleaned up and fixed many comment errors and outdated comments in the code. (PRs: #19064, #19343, #19703, #19765, #19771, #19787; commits: 055d029, 27003aa, 3fa6dbd, 9bbc5b5, a63fa5f, f93c0f5)

### Performance

- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2069] Adds inline assembly acceleration support based on the RISC-V Zknh extension for the SHA256 algorithm. (PRs: #16710; commits: 657d192)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2070] Simplify the square root calculation of Ed25519 by replacing the original u*v^3 * (u * v^7)^((p-5)/8) with u * (u*v)^((p-5)/8), reducing 3 multiplications and 2 square operations. (PRs: #17544; commits: a822a0c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2071] Cache key lengths and IV lengths to avoid expensive parameter lookups on every query, and skip provider calls if parameters haven't changed. (PRs: #17064, #17543; commits: 70f39a4)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2072] Optimize SM4 encryption and decryption performance in non-assembly mode, and replace the original rotation operation by introducing a lookup table method. (PRs: #17766; commits: 13ba91c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2073] Convert NULL attribute queries to empty strings to fix performance degradation caused by non-caching. (PRs: #17752, #17769; commits: af788ad)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2074] Optimize SM4-GCM encryption/decryption performance on the ARM platform, and achieve up to 4 times performance improvement by reusing the existing high-performance CTR encryption interface to replace single-block encryption. (PRs: #17814; commits: 26efd0b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2075] Reconstruct the internal logic of the decoder to reduce repeated calls to OSSL_DECODER_is_a and EVP_KEYMGMT_is_a, and improve the performance of repeated decoding operations. (PRs: #15199, #17921; commits: 2475544)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2076] Optimize the evp_md_init_internal function to avoid reallocating the algorithm context when the digest algorithm has not changed, and reconstruct the release logic of the algorithm context. (PRs: #16947, #18105; commits: fe5c5cb)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2077] Adds ROTATE inline assembly implementation based on the RISC-V zbb/zbkb extension for DES. (PRs: #18290; commits: 6136408)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2078] Optimize the performance of the ossl_lh_strcasehash function, and the running time is approximately halved. (PRs: #18354; commits: a4e21d1)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2079] Ensure that prefix records use small buffers to avoid allocating full-size write buffers when only prefixes are needed. (PRs: #19198; commits: 85b358b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2080] Optimize the SM4 key setting function, improving performance by about 48% by reconstructing nonlinear transformation and loop unrolling. (PRs: #19270; commits: 704e809)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2081] Optimize DRBG parameter acquisition, allowing security parameters such as max_request to be read without locking, improving concurrency performance. (PRs: #20286, #20970; commits: 61f11ca)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2082] Optimize the key update in the QUIC channel, and add a mechanism to force the generation of ACK to speed up the completion of the key update. (PRs: #21029; commits: 37ba2bc)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2083] The state access of the FIPS module was changed from the lock mechanism to the use of memory sorting operations to improve performance and reduce synchronization overhead; HMAC-SHA-256 known answer testing and RNG recovery verification were also added. (PRs: #21179, #21187; commits: 8e9ca33)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2084] In non-blocking mode, when SSL_read fails, increase the reactor tick and retry reading to improve the success rate of data delivery. (PRs: #21381; commits: 780b252)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2085] Introducing a caching mechanism for the PKEY decoder to improve performance by reusing OSSL_DECODER_CTX objects with the same parameters. (PRs: #15199, #21426; commits: 32d3c3a)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2086] Introduce branch prediction hints and optimize input length conversion and division operations in EVP_EncryptUpdate and EVP_DecryptUpdate to improve performance under GCC/Clang. (PRs: #21425; commits: ed6dfd1)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2087] The Solinas reduction strategy of 56-bit redundant limbs is used for the secp384r1 curve to achieve efficient modular multiplication operations, increasing the digital signature speed by 446% and the verification speed by 106%. (PRs: #21471; commits: 01d901e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2088] Optimized SM2 performance on aarch64 architecture and added a dedicated SM2 P-256 elliptic curve implementation. (PRs: #20754; commits: 6399d78)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2089] Reused memory allocation between ACK frame processing in QUIC RXDP, and added improvements such as key update checks and flow control notifications. (PRs: #21917; commits: 8c792b0)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2090] In the QUIC mode of s_client, when there is user data to be written, the select timeout is set to 0 to avoid unnecessary waiting. (PRs: #22480; commits: 95420a2)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2588] Improve performance for 6x unrolling with vpermxor instruction (PRs: #21812; commits: 3d3a7ec)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2692] Fix reported performance degradation on aarch64 (PRs: #18445, #18581; commits: 6552375)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2696] Optimize chacha20 on aarch64 by SVE2 (PRs: #18522; commits: bcb52bc)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2720] disable 5x interleave on buffers shorter than 512 bytes: 3% speedup on Graviton2 (PRs: #17984; commits: 9c140a3)

### Security

- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2152] Adjusted the security strength rating of the CCM8 cipher suite from 64 bits to 80 bits and lowered the security level to level 0 to reflect the lower security strength brought by its short label. (PRs: #16154, #16652; commits: 1a473d1, 56ffcce, e071022)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2153] Fix potential out-of-bounds issue in help output caused by width exceeding buffer size, refactor output logic to use dynamic length calculation, and add width upper limit check. (PRs: #12265; commits: 0d1a0ed, aac6ae3)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2154] Add a range check to the -multi parameter in apps/speed.c, and fix the buffer length overflow problem caused by MAX_MISALIGNMENT. The upper limit of the unified loop is INT_MAX. (PRs: #16899, #16904, #17646; commits: 378c50f, 7220085)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2155] Use safe math functions to calculate rounding sizes to prevent integer overflow. (PRs: #17884; commits: 330ff7e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2156] Fix address leak and RAND_bytes_ex return value check in CBC_MAC_ROTATE_IN_PLACE, change to constant time loading. (PRs: #18033; commits: 3b83638)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2157] Fix multiple security vulnerabilities (CVE-2022-1292, CVE-2022-2068) in the c_rehash tool, no longer call openssl through the shell, and improve file operation security. (commits: 7c33270, ce60b13)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2158] Add assertions when reusing algorithm context to ensure that the summary object is provided to prevent security issues caused by implicit acquisition. (PRs: #18105, #18224; commits: 221d65b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2159] Disable the SSL2_VERSION version number from appearing in the SSLv3 record header to prevent ambiguity when verifying the record format. (PRs: #18132; commits: 014baa8)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2160] Fix a possible crash when creating an ASN1 template object when out of memory, make sure to check that the object has been properly initialized before calling the release callback. (PRs: #18360; commits: 557825a)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2161] Fix memory leak caused by BN_bin2bn failure in ec_key_simple_oct2priv function. (PRs: #18366; commits: 22a96c6)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2162] Increased the iteration factor for computing auxiliary primes in RSA key generation from 5 to 20 to match the FIPS 186-5 standard update. (PRs: #18321, #18429; commits: ad7e0fd)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2163] Add sensitive memory cleaning and error checking in DH, DSA, EC, RSA private key encoding functions. (PRs: #18540, #18541; commits: 1624934)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2164] Securely erase MAC keys for SSLv3 when clearing the record layer. (PRs: #18132; commits: c77d455)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2165] Fixed a heap buffer overflow in BIO_ADDR_dup caused by copying the entire structure, and copied the correct union members instead. (PRs: #18979; commits: 278b0d8)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2166] Fixed out-of-bounds reading in the ssl_cipher_process_rulestr function when the rule string ends with '-'. (PRs: #19166; commits: 428511c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2167] Verify that the random number generator (RNG) is restored correctly after the FIPS self-test. (PRs: #19510; commits: 33290c5)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2168] Fixed an out-of-bounds write vulnerability in the punycode decoder (CVE-2022-3602), and fixed an off-by-one bug in buffer bounds checking. (PRs: #19591; commits: 3b421eb, 8aa82b3, a0af4a3)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2169] Fixed the buffer overflow vulnerability in the punycode decoder (CVE-2022-3786), and solved the potential buffer overflow issue in the ossl_a2ulabel function. (commits: 680e65b)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2170] Fix the tainted scalar issue reported by Coverity and add an upper limit check on the variable uclen in compressed certificate processing. (PRs: #19519; commits: 00e38ed)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2171] Fix the security issue in BN_mod_exp_mont_consttime where the modulus is too large causing buffer length overflow, and add invalid parameter check. (PRs: #19632; commits: 4378e3c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2172] Fix the timing side channel vulnerability in RSA decryption by adjusting blinding processing, error reporting and padding check logic to eliminate exploitable timing differences. (commits: b1892d2)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2173] Fixed a type confusion vulnerability in nc_match_single caused by incorrect assumption of the OtherName type, which could cause certificate name constraint check errors. (commits: 748f478)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2174] Fix PEM_read_bio_ex not clearing header and data pointers on failure resulting in potential double free. (commits: ee6243f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2175] Fix the use-after-free vulnerability in the BIO_new_NDEF function caused by invalid BIO chain due to callback failure. (commits: 9cc8500)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2176] Clean the internal buffer used to generate k in the BN_generate_dsa_nonce function to prevent sensitive data leakage. (PRs: #9205, #19954; commits: 177d433)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2177] Fix the NULL pointer dereference vulnerability in the FFC public key and private key verification functions, add parameter null value checking and return the corresponding error code. (commits: bcec03c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2178] Fixed a security vulnerability in dereferencing a null pointer when PKCS7 object data is not set (CVE-2023-0216). (commits: 80253db)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2179] Fixed the issue in pk7_doit.c that the return value of BIO_set_md() was not checked, preventing null pointer dereference due to digest algorithm initialization failure, and fixed CVE-2023-0401. (commits: 6eebe6c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2180] Adjusted session ID generation and renegotiation check logic in fuzz testing mode so that the post-handshake phase can be triggered to support the discovery of CVE-2021-3449. (PRs: #20128; commits: 2b9e2af)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2181] Fixed a timing leak vulnerability in RSA private key decryption, and added a constant time function bn_correct_top_consttime to eliminate side channel risks. (PRs: #20281; commits: f06ef16)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2182] Fixed a security vulnerability where invalid policy extension flags in leaf certificates were not checked, ensuring that all certificates are correctly verified. (PRs: #20585; commits: e4142ec)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2183] Fixed the resource overuse vulnerability (CVE-2023-0464) in X.509 certificate policy constraint verification to prevent denial of service attacks by limiting the number of policy tree nodes. (PRs: #20571; commits: 3a81370)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2184] Fixed use-after-free vulnerability in argon2 KDF caused by not releasing MAC context. (PRs: #20528; commits: 7c45b7c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2185] Use safe multiplication functions to replace original calculations in QUIC NewReno congestion control to prevent integer overflow. (PRs: #20423; commits: d235f65)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2186] Replaced getenv() with ossl_safe_getenv() in HTTP proxy handling for safe reading of environment variables. (PRs: #20810; commits: e7cbb09)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2187] Fixed the stack use-after-free problem caused by local variables on the callback function stack in QUIC, and changed the protocol string in the ALPN selection callback to a static constant. (PRs: #20904; commits: ca9ef8e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2188] Fixed an integer operation that may overflow in the HKDF_Expand function, and changed the addition comparison to a subtraction comparison to avoid potential overflow risks. (PRs: #20990; commits: 56a51b5)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2189] Added a check for the receive packet forgery limit in QUIC channels and enforces the limit when processing receive packets. (PRs: #21029; commits: 48120ea)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2190] Fixed the final size check in the RESET_STREAM frame to ensure that the received final size is consistent with the previously recorded value to prevent illegal modification. (PRs: #21135; commits: 2cc0e2d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2191] Ensure QUIC packet number duplication suppression occurs after AEAD verification to avoid side channel attacks. (PRs: #21135; commits: dfe5e7f)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2192] Updated legacy authentication strength checks for DSA keys, tightening security conditions per SP 800-131Ar2. (PRs: #21185, #21186; commits: 71cf587)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2193] Added data clearing function to release, resize and pop operations of send and receive ring buffers. (PRs: #21311; commits: 292c9df)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2194] Fixed the vulnerability of ignoring empty associated data in AES-SIV mode, ensuring that zero-length associated data can also be authenticated correctly. (PRs: #21384; commits: c426c28)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2195] The QUIC ring buffer has been strengthened, and safe mathematical operations and offset upper limit checks have been added to prevent integer overflows caused by internal misuse. (PRs: #21895; commits: 6042189)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2590] Avoid having ecp_sm2p256-armv8.pl in fips.module.sources (PRs: #22194; commits: b07a0b1)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2611] fips: make installations FIPS compliant by default (PRs: #21363; commits: dc6f3b9)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2622] fips.module.sources: Add missing cpuid and related .c sources for other architectures (PRs: #20949; commits: c509c04)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2661] Avoid putting ripemd_prov.c in libcommon otherwise it is regarded as fips source (PRs: #19375; commits: 155a82d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2687] Fix quotes install_fips in Configurations/windows-makefile.tmpl (PRs: #18880, #18881; commits: 709651c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2703] Add Windows VERSIONINFO resource to fips provider dll. (PRs: #18388, #18416; commits: 18f0870)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2710] Building: For the FIPS module checksum, keep track of configuration,h (PRs: #16378; commits: 4d02d50)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2734] `make clean` should clean up fips provider shared object. (PRs: #17556; commits: d94c2f1)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-2750] install_fips: Create the OPENSSLDIR as it might not exist (PRs: #16564, #16569; commits: 85efdaa)

### Tests

- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1725] Added QUIC test server tool, expanded test server API, supports flow status query, local connection ID update and network activity waiting functions. (PRs: #20856, #20892, #21204, #21565, #21686; commits: 17340e8, 1a0de4c, 219db5e, 37f27b9, 614c08c, 644ef0b, 80b9eca, bbc9754)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1726] Replace the bitwise OR operator in the test code with a logical OR operator to eliminate Clang's -Wbitwise-instead-of-logical warning. (PRs: #18639; commits: 6162a24)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1727] Add the missing string.h header file to the test file test/v3ext.c. (PRs: #18719; commits: f9e578e)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1728] A new test is added to verify the order of TLS extensions, and an auxiliary function and related macro definitions for obtaining the extension type are added. (PRs: #19269; commits: ac44dea)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1729] A new fuzzer in PEM format is added to discover the CVE-2022-4450 vulnerability. (PRs: #20242; commits: bc07d37)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1730] Added provider-based speed testing functionality for KEM and SIG algorithms. (PRs: #19968; commits: 4557e28)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1731] Added decoder fuzzer, used to discover CVE-2023-0217. (PRs: #20269; commits: a9e6100)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1732] Added multiple info field splicing test cases for KBKDF, SSKDF and X963KDF. (PRs: #20724; commits: 8d18f20)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1733] Added the OPENSSL_TEST_RAND_SEED environment variable, which allows the test random number seed to be set independently without affecting the test order, and displays the correct seed environment variable when the test fails. (PRs: #22118; commits: 44fbe0d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1734] Introduce richer noise patterns in the noisy dgram BIO, support random packet loss, delay and repeated datagrams, and add debug output to track the noise processing process. (PRs: #22157, #22199, #22243, #22267; commits: 0a2369f, 19d79bb, 21d2041, 43b94c7, 5d3933e, 6dfc57f, 8f67c6b, b1584a8, c6bb25f, d3a8dac)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1735] Improve malloc failure test: allow percentage values in OPENSSL_MALLOC_FAILURES to support two decimal places, and add OPENSSL_MALLOC_SEED environment variable to support randomization. (PRs: #22127; commits: 3df5736)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1736] Fix potential array overflow issue reported by Coverity by increasing parameter array size in digest_test_run from 3 to 4. (PRs: #22521; commits: f205958)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1747] Adjust the test cases to adapt to the change of the default security level from 1 to 2, and fix the problem of DH automatic test failure caused by the increase in security level. (PRs: #16760; commits: 61cab65)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1788] Add test cases for Perfect Forward Security (PFS) in SECLEVEL >= 3 scenarios, and extend the testing tool to support new DH parameters and TLS version options. (PRs: #17763; commits: d71151a)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1848] Add test case for RSA's implicit reject disable option. (PRs: #13817; commits: 455db0c)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1855] Added digest XOF length support for evp_test. (PRs: #12256; commits: c8ebdd6)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1924] Skip or disable the QUIC SSL trace test according to compilation configuration conditions (specific options, no-ecx, DH disabled) to avoid test failure due to option interference. (PRs: #21332, #21372, #21373; commits: 1be2ee6, 47ef3b9, 61cc84d)
- [GT-OPENSSL-3.2.0-1931] Fix the condition of the blocking mode flag in QUIC test cases to ensure that test 2 correctly runs in blocking mode. (PRs: #21087, #21332, #21409; commits: ff9728c)
